kṛtavatyasi nāvadhīraṇāmaparādhhe'pi yadā ciraṃ mayi  |
kathamekapade nirāgasaṃ janamābhāṣyamimaṃ na manyase  || 8.48 ||

dhruvamasmi śaṭhaḥ śucismite vidhitaḥ kaitavavatsalastava  |
paralokamasaṃnivṛttaye yadanāpṛcchya gatāsi māmitaḥ  || 8.49 ||

dayitāṃ yadi tāvadanvagādvinivṛttaṃ kimidaṃ tayā vinā  |
sahatāṃ hatajīvitaṃ mama prabalāmātmakṛtena vedanām  || 8.50 ||

surataśramasaṃbhṛto mukhe dhriyate svedalavodgamo'pi te  |
atha cāstamitā'syaho bata dhigimāṃ dehabhṛtāmasāratām  || 8.51 ||

surataśramavāribindavo na hi tāvadviramanti te mukhe  |
kathamastamitā'syaho bata dhigimāṃ dehavatāmasāratām  || 8.51* ||

manasāpi na vipriyaṃ mayā kṛtapūrvaṃ tava kiṃ jahāsi mām  |
nanu śabdapatiḥ kṣiterahaṃ tvayi me bhāvanibandhanā ratiḥ  || 8.52 ||

kusumotkacitān valīmataścalayan bhṛṅgarucastavālakān  |
karabhoru karoti mārutastvadupāvartanśaṅki me manaḥ  || 8.53 ||

tadapohitumarhasi priye pratibodhena viṣādamāṣu me  |
jvalitena guhāgataṃ tamastuhinādreriva natamoṣadhiḥ  || 8.54 ||

idamucchvasitālakaṃ mukhaṃ viśrāntakathaṃ dunoti mām  |
niśi suptamivaikapaṅkajaṃ viratābhyantaraṣaṭpadasvanam  || 8.55 ||

śaśinaṃ punareti śārvarī dayitā dvandvacaraṃ patatriṇam  |
iti tau virhāntarakṣamau kathamatyantagatā na māṃ daheḥ  || 8.56 ||

navapallavasaṃstare'pi te mṛdu dūyeta yadaṅgamarpitam  |
tadidaṃ viṣahiṣyate kathaṃ vada vāmoru citādhirohaṇam  || 8.57 ||

iyamapratibodhaśāyinīṃ raśanā tvāṃ prathamā rahaḥsakhī  |
gativibhramasāda nīravā na śucā nānumṛteva lakṣyte  || 8.58 ||

kalamanyabhṛtāsu bhāṣitaṃ kalahaṃsīṣu gataṃ madālasaṃ  |
pṛṭatīṣu vilolamīkṣitaṃ pavanādhūtalatāsu vibhramaḥ  || 8.59 ||

tridivotsukayāpyavekṣya māṃ nihitāḥ satyamamī guṇāstvayā  |
virahe tava me guruvyathaṃ hṛdayaṃ na tvavalambituṃ kṣamāḥ  || 8.60 ||

mithunaṃ parikalpitaṃ tvayā sahakāraḥ phalinī ca nanvimau  |
avidhāya vivāhasatkriyāmanayorgamyata ivyasāṃpratam  || 8.61 ||

kusumaṃ kṛtadohadastvayā yadaśoko'yamudīrayiṣyati  |
alakābharaṇaṃ kathaṃ nu tattava neṣyāmi nivāpalālyatām  || 8.62 ||

smarateva saśabdanūpuraṃ caraṇānugrahamanyadurlabham  |
amunā kusumāśruvarṣiṇā tvamaśokena sugātri śocyase  || 8.63 ||

tava niḥśvasitānukāribhirbakulairardhacitāṃ samaṃ mayā  |
asamāpya vilāsamekhalāṃ kimidaṃ kiṃnarakaṇṭhi supyate  || 8.64 ||

samaduḥkhasukhaḥ sakhījanaḥ pratipaccandranibho'yamātmajaḥ  |
ahamekarasastathāpi te vyavasāyaḥ pratipattiniṣṭhuraḥ  || 8.65 ||

dhṛtirastamitā ratiścyutā virataṃ geyamṛturnirutsavaḥ  |
gatamābharaṇaprayojanaṃ pariśūnyaṃ śayanīyamadya me  || 8.66 ||

gṛhiṇī sacivaḥ sakhī mithaḥ priyaśiṣyā lalite kalāvidhau  |
karuṇāvimukhena mṛtyunā haratā tvāṃ vada kiṃ na me hṛtam  || 8.67 ||

madirākṣi madānanārpitaṃ madhu pītvā rasavatkathaṃ nu me  |
anupāsyasi bāṣpadūṣitaṃ paralokopanataṃ jalāñjalim  || 8.68 ||

vibhave'pi sati tvayā vinā sukhametāvadajasya gaṇyatām  |
ahṛtasya vilobhanāntarairmama sarve viṣayāstadāśrayāḥ  || 8.69 ||

vilapanniti kosalādhipaḥ karuṇārthagrathitaṃ priyāṃ prati  |
akarotpṛthivīruhānapi srutaśākhārasabhāṣpadurdinān  || 8.70 ||

atha tasya kathaṃcidaṅkataḥ svajanastāmapanīya sundarīṃ  |
visasarja kṛtāntyamaṇḍanāmanalāy' āgurucandanadihase  || 8.71 ||

pramadāmanu saṃsthitaḥ śucā nṛpatiḥ sanniti vācyadarśanāt |
na cakāra śarīramagnisātsaha devyā na tu jīvitāśayā  || 8.72 ||

atha tena daśāhataḥ pare guṇaśeṣāmupadiṣya gehinīm  |
viduṣā vidhayo maharddhayaḥ pura evopavane samāpitāḥ  || 8.73 ||

sa viveśa purīṃ tayā vinā kṣaṇadāpāyaśaśāṅkadarśanaḥ  |
parivāhamivāvalokayan svaśucaḥ pauravadhūmukhāśruṣu  || 8.74 ||

atha taṃ savanāya diṣitaḥ praṇidhānādgururāśramasthitaḥ  |
abhiṣaṅgajaḍaṃ vijajñivāniti śiṣyeṇa kilānvabodhayat || 8.75 ||

asamāptavidhiryato munistava vidvānapi tāpakāraṇam  |
na bhavantamupasthitaḥ svayaṃ prakṛtau sthāpayituṃ kṛtasthitiḥ  || 8.76 ||

mayi tasya suvṛtta vartate laghusaṃdeśapadā sarasvatī  |
śṛṇu viśrutasattvasāra tāṃ hṛdi caināmupadhātumarhasi  || 8.77 ||

puruṣasya padeṣvajanmanaḥ samatītaṃ ca bhavacca bhāvi ca  |
sa hi niṣpratighena cakṣuṣā tritayaṃ jñānamayena paśyati  || 8.78 ||

carataḥ kila duścaraṃ tapastṛṇabindoḥ pariśaṅkitaḥ purā  |
prajighāya samādhibedinīṃ harirasmai hariṇīṃ surāṅganām  || 8.79 ||

sa tapaḥpratibandhamanyunā pramukhāviṣkṛtacāruvibhramām  |
aśapadbhava mānuṣīti tāṃ śamavelāpralayormiṇā muniḥ  || 8.80 ||

bhagavan paravānayaṃ janaḥ pratikūlācaritaṃ kṣamasva me  |
iti copanatāṃ kṣitipṛśaṃ vivaśā śāpanivṛttikāraṇam  || 8.81 ||

krathakaiśikavaṃśasaṃbhavā tava bhūtvā mahiṣī cirāya sā  |
upalabdhavatī divaścyutaṃ vivaśā śāpanivṛttikāraṇam  || 8.82 ||

tadalaṃ tadapāyacintayā vipadutpattimatāmupasthitā  |
vasudheyamavekṣyatāṃ tvayā vasumatyā hi nṛpāḥ kalatriṇaḥ  || 8.83 ||

udaye madavācyamujjhatā śrutamāviṣkṛtamātmavattayā  |
manasastadupasthite jvare punaraklībatayā prakāśyatāṃ  || 8.84 ||

rudatā kuta eva sā punarbhavatā nānumṛtāpi labhyate  |
paralokajuṣāṃ svakarmabhirgatayo bhinnapathā hi dehinām  || 8.85 ||

ruditena na sā nivartate nṛpa tattāvadanrthakaṃ tava  |
na bhavānanusaṃsthito'pi tāṃ labhate karmavaśā hi dehinaḥ  || 8.85* ||

apaśokamanāḥ kuṭumbinīmanugṛhṇīṣva nivāpadattibhiḥ  |
svajanāśru kilātrisaṃtataṃ dahati pretamiti pracakṣate  || 8.86 ||

maraṇaṃ prakṛtiḥ śarīriṇāṃ vikṛtirjīvitamucyate budhaiḥ  |
kṣaṇamapyavatiṣṭhate śvasan yadi janturnanu lābhavānasau  || 8.87 ||

avagacchati mūḍhacetanaḥ priyanāśaṃ hṛdi śalyamarpitam  |
sthiradhīstu tadeva manyate kuśaladvāratayā samuddhṛtam  || 8.88 ||

avagacchati mūḍhacetanaḥ śruta dhṛtasaṃyogaviparyayau yadā  |
virahaḥ kimivānutāpayedvada bāhyairviṣayairvipaścitam  || 8.89 ||

na pṛthagjanavacchuco vaśaṃ vaśināmuttama gantumarhasi  |
drumasānumatāṃ kimantaraṃ yadi vāyau dvitaye'pi te calāḥ  || 8.90 ||

sa tatheti vineturudāramateḥ pratigṛhya vaco visasarja munim  |
tadalabdhapadaṃ hṛdi śokaghane pratiyātamivāntikamasya guroḥ  || 8.91 ||

tenāṣṭau parigamitāḥ samāḥ kathaṃcidbālatvādavitathasūnṛtena sūnoḥ  |
sādṛśyapratikṛtidarśanaiḥ priyāyāḥ svapneṣu kṣaṇikasamāgamtosavaiśca  || 8.92 ||

tasya prasahya hṛdayaṃ kila śokaśaṅkuḥ plakṣapraroha iva saudhatalaṃ bibheda  |
prāṇāntahetumapi taṃ bhiṣajāmasādhyaṃ lābhaṃ priyānugamane tvarayā sa mene  || 8.93 ||

samyagvinītamatha varmaharaṃ kumāramādiśya rakṣaṇavidhau vidhivatprajānām  |
rogopasṛṣṭatanudurvasatiṃ mumukṣuḥ prāyopaveśanamatirnṛpatirbabhūva  || 8.94 ||

tīrthe toyavyatikarabhave jahnukanyāsaryvordehatyāgādamaragaṇanālekhyamāsādya sadyaḥ  |
pūrvākārādhikatararucā saṃgataḥ kāntayāsau līlāgāreṣvaramata punarnandanābhyantareṣu  || 8.95 ||


_______________________________________________________________________________





pituranantaramuttarkosalān samadhigamya samādhijitendriyaḥ  |
daśarathaḥ praśaśāsa mahāratho yamavatāmavatāṃ ca dhuri sthitaḥ  || 9.1 ||

adhigataṃ vidhivadyadapālayatprakṛtimaṇḍalamātmakulocitam  |
abhavadasya tato guṇavattaraṃ sanagaraṃ nagarandhrakaraujasaḥ  || 9.2 ||

ubhayameva vadanti manīṣiṇaḥ samayavarṣitayā kṛtakarmaṇām  |
valaniṣūdanamarthpatiṃ ca taṃ śramanudaṃ manudaṇḍaharānvayam  || 9.3 ||

janapade na gadaḥ padamādadhāvabhibhavaḥ kuta eva sapatnajaḥ  |
kṣitirabhūtphalavatyajanandane śamarate'maratejasi pārthive  || 9.4 ||

daśadigantajitā raghuṇā yathā śriyamapuṣyadajena tataḥ param  |
tamadhigamya tathaiva punarbabhau na na mahī'namahīnaparākramam  || 9.5 ||

samatayā vasuvṛṭivisarjanairniyamanādasatāṃ ca narādhipaḥ  |
anuyayau yamapuṇyajaneśvarau savaruṇāvaruṇāgrasaraṃ rucā  || 9.6 ||

na mṛgayābhiratirna durodaraṃ na ca śaśipratimābharaṇaṃ madhu  |
tamudayāya na vā navayauvanā priyatamā yatamānamapāharā  || 9.7 ||

na kṛpaṇā prabhavatyapi vāsave na vitathā parihāsakathāsvapi  |
na ca sapatnajaneṣvapi tena vāgaparuṣā paruṣākṣaramīritā  || 9.8 ||

udayamastamayaṃ ca raghūdvahādubhayamānaśire vasudhādhipāḥ  |
sa hi nideśamalaṅghayatāmabhūtsuhṛdayohṛdayaḥ pratigarjatām  || 9.9 ||

ajayadekarathena sa medinīmudadhinemimadhijyaśarāsanaḥ  |
jayamaghoṣayadasya tu kevalaṃ gajavatī javatīrahayā camūḥ  || 9.10 ||

jaghananirviṣayīkṛtamekhalānanucitāśruviluptaviśeṣakān  |
sa ripudāragaṇānakarodbalādanalakānalakādhipavikramaḥ  || 9.10* ||

avanimekarathena varūthinā jitavataḥ kila tasya dhanurbhṛtaḥ  |
vijayadundubhitāṃ yayurarṇavā ghanaravā naravāhanasaṃpadaḥ  || 9.11 ||

śamitapakṣabalaḥ śitakoṭinā śikhariṇāṃ kuliśena puraṃdaraḥ  |
sa sāravṛṣṭimucā dhanuṣā dviṣāṃ svanavatā navatāmarasānanaḥ  || 9.12 ||

sphuritakoṭisahasramarīcinā samacinotkuliśena hariryaśaḥ  |
sa dhanuṣā yudhi sāyakavarṣiṇā svanavatā navatāmarasānanaḥ  || 9.12* ||

caraṇayornakharāgasamṛddhibhirmukuṭaratnamarīcibhiraspṛśan  |
sa dhanuṣā yudhi sāyakavarṣiṇā śatamakhaṃ tamakhaṇḍitapauruṣam  || 9.13 ||

nivavṛte sa mahārṇavarodhasaḥ sacivakāritabālasutāñjalīn  |
samanukampya sapatnaparigrahānanalakānalakānavamāṃ purīm  || 9.14 ||

upagato'pi ca maṇḍalanābhitāmanuditānyasitātapavāraṇaḥ  |
ajitamasti nṛpāspadamityabhūdanalaso'nalasomasamadyutiḥ  || 9.15 ||

kratuṣu tena visarjitamaulinā bhujasamāhṛtadigvasunā kṛtāḥ  |
kanakayūpasamucchrayaśobhino vitamasā tamasārasyūtaṭāḥ  || 9.16 ||

ajinadaṇḍabhṛtaṃ kuśamekhalāṃ yatagiraṃ mṛgaśṛṅgaparigrahām  |
adhivasaṃstanumadhvaradīkṣitāmasambhāsamabhāsayadīśvaraḥ  || 9.17 ||

avabhṛtaprayato niyatendriyaḥ surasamājasamākramaṇocitaḥ  |
namayati sma sa kevalamunnataṃ vanamuce namuceraraye śiraḥ  || 9.18 ||

tamapahāya kakutsthakulodbhavaṃ puruṣamātmabhuvaṃ ca pativratā  |
nṛpatimanyamasevata devatā sakamalā kamalāghavamarthiṣu  || 9.19 ||

sa kila saṃyugamūrdhni sahāyatāṃ maghavataḥ pratipadya mahārathaḥ  |
svabhujavīryamagāpayaducchritaṃ suravadhūravadhūtabhayāḥ śaraiḥ  || 9.20 ||

asakṛdekarathena tarasvinā harihayāgrasareṇa dhanurbhṛtā  |
dinakarābhimukhā raṇareṇavo rurudhire rudhireṇa suradviṣām  || 9.21 ||

tamalabhanta patiṃ patidevatāḥ śikhariṇāmiva sāgaramāpagāḥ  |
magadhakosalakekayaśasināṃ duhitaro'hitaropitamārgaṇam  || 9.22 ||

priyatamābhirasau tiṛbhirbabhau tisṛbhireva bhuvaṃ saha śaktibhiḥ  |
upagato vininīṣuriva prajā harihayo'rihayogavicakṣaṇaḥ  || 9.23 ||

atha samāvavṛte kusumairnavaistamiva sevitumekanarādhipam  |
yamakuberjaleśvaravajriṇāṃ samadhuraṃ madhurañcitavikramam  || 9.24 ||

jigamiṣurdhanadādhyuṣitāṃ diśaṃ rathayujā parivartitavāhanaḥ  |
dinamukhāni ravirhimanirgrahairvimalayanmalayaṃ nagamatyajat || 9.25 ||

himavivarṇitacandanapallavaṃ virahayanmalayādrimudaṅmukhaḥ  |
vihagayoḥ kṛpayeva śanairyayau raviraharvirahadhruvabhedayoḥ  || 9.25* ||

kusumajanma tato navapallavāstadanu ṣaṭpadakokilakūjitam  |
iti yathākramamāvirabhūnmadhurdrumavatīmavatīrya vanasthalīm  || 9.26 ||

surabhisaṃgamajaṃ vanamālayā navapalāśamadhāryata bhaṅguram  |
ramaṇadattamivārdranakhakṣataṃ pramadayā madayāpitalajjayā  || 9.26* ||

upahitaṃ śiśirāpagamaśriyā mukulajālamaśobhata kiṃśuke  |
praṇayinīva nakhakṣatamaṇḍanaṃ pramadayā madayāpitalajjayā  || 9.27 ||

parabhṛtā madanakṣatacetasāṃ priyasakhī laghuvāgiva yoṣitām  |
priyatamānakarotkalahāntare mṛduravā duravāpasamāgamān  || 9.27* ||

vraṇagurupramadādharaduḥsahaṃ jaghananirviṣayīkṛtamekhalam  |
na khalu tāvadaśeṣamapohituṃ raviralaṃ viralaṃ kṛtavān himam  || 9.28 ||

viśadacandrakaraṃ sukhamārutaṃ kusumitadrumamunmadakokilam  |
tadupabhogarasaṃ himavarṣiṇaḥ paramṛtorviralaṃ kṛtavān himam  || 9.28* ||

abhinayān paricetumivodyatā malayamārutakampitapallavā  |
amadayatsahakāralatā manaḥ sakalikā kalikāmajitāmapi  || 9.29 ||

nayaguṇopacitāmiva bhūpateḥ sadupakāraphalāṃ śriyamarthinaḥ  |
abhiyayuḥ saraso madhusaṃbhṛtāṃ kamalinīmalinīrapatriṇaḥ  || 9.30 ||

daśanacandrikayā vyabhāsitaṃ hasitamāsavagandhi madhoriva  |
bakulapuṣpamasevyata ṣaṭpadaiḥ śucirasaṃ cirasaṃcitamīpsubhiḥ  || 9.30* ||

kusumameva na kevalamārtavaṃ navamaśokataroḥ smaradīpanam  |
kisalayaprasavo'pi vilāsināṃ madayitā dayitāśravaṇārpitaḥ  || 9.31 ||

viracitā madhunopavanśriyāmabhinavā iva pattraviśeṣakāḥ  |
madhulihāṃ madhudānaviśāradāḥ kurabakā ravakāraṇatāṃ yayuḥ  || 9.32 ||

suvadanāvadanāsavasaṃbhṛtastadanuvādiguṇaḥ kusumodgamaḥ  |
madhukarairakaronmadhulolupairbakulamākulamāyatapaṅktibhiḥ  || 9.33 ||

suvadanāvadanāsavasaṃbhṛtastadanuvādiguṇaḥ kusumodgamaḥ  |
iti dayāta ivābhavadāyatā na rajanī rajanīśavatī madhau  || 9.33* ||

prathamamanyabhṛtābhirudīritāḥ praviralā iva mugdhavadhūkathāḥ  |
surabhigandhiṣu śuśruvire giraḥ kusumitāsu mitā vanarājiṣu  || 9.34 ||

śrutisukhabhramarasvanagītayaḥ kusumakomaladantaruco babhuḥ  |
upavanāntalatāḥ pavanāhataiḥ kisalayaiḥ salayairiva pāṇibhiḥ  || 9.35 ||

lalitavibhramabandhavicakṣaṇaṃ surabhigandhaparājitakesaram  |
patiṣu nirviviśurmadhumaṅganāḥ smarasakhaṃ rasakhaṇḍanavarjitam  || 9.36 ||

tilakamastakaharmyakṛtāspadaiḥ kusumamadhvanuṣaṅgasugandhibhiḥ  |
kalamagīyata bhṛṅgavilāsināṃ smarayutairayutairabalāsakhaiḥ  || 9.36* ||

śuśubhire smitacārutarānanāḥ striya iva ślathaśiñjitamekhalāḥ  |
vikacatāmarasā gṛhadīrghikā madakalodakalolavihaṃgamāḥ  || 9.37 ||

laghayati sma na patyaparādhajaṃ na sahakāratarustaruṇīdhṛtam  |
kusumito namito'libhirunmadaiḥ smarasamādhisamādhikaroṣitam  || 9.37* ||

upayayau tanutāṃ madhukhaṇḍitā himakarodayapāṇḍumukhacchaviḥ  |
sadṛśamiṣṭasamāgamanirvṛtiṃ vanitayā'nitayā rajanīvadhūḥ  || 9.38 ||

apatuṣāratayā viśadaprabhaiḥ suratarāga pariśramanodibhiḥ  |
kusumacāpamatejayadaṃśubhirhimakaro makarojitaketanam  || 9.39 ||

hutahutāśanadīpti vanśriyaḥ pratinidhiḥ kanakābharaṇasya yat |
yuvatayaḥ kusumaṃ dadhurāhitaṃ tad(?) alake dalakesarapeśalam  || 9.40 ||

alibhirañjanabindumaoharaiḥ kusumapaṅktinipātibhiraṅkitaḥ  |
na khalu śobahayitsma vanasthalīṃ na tilakastilakaḥ pramadāmiva  || 9.41 ||

amadayanmadghugandhasanāthayā kisalayādharasaṃgatayā manaḥ  |
kusumasaṃbhṭtayā navamallikā smitarucā tarucāruvilāsinī  || 9.42 ||

analasānyabhṛtā'nalasānmanaḥ kamaladhūlibhṛtā maruteritā  |
kusumabhāranatādhvagayoṣitāmasamaśokamaśokalatā'karot || 9.42* ||

aruṇarāganiṣedhibhiraṃśukaiḥ śravaṇalabdhapadaiśca yavāṅkuraiḥ  |
parabhṛtāvirutaiśca vilāsinaḥ smarabalairabalaikarasāḥ kṛtāḥ  || 9.43 ||

upacitāvayavā śucibhiḥ kaṇairalikadambakayogamupeyuṣī  |
sadṛśakāntiralakṣyata mañjarī tilakajā'lakajālakamauktikaiḥ  || 9.44 ||

dhvajapaṭaṃ madanasya dhanurbhṛtaśchavikaraṃ mukhacūrṇamṛtuśriyaḥ  |
kusumakesarareṇumalivrajāḥ sapavanopavanotthitamanvayuḥ  || 9.45 ||

anubhavannavadolamṛtūtsavaṃ paṭurpai priyakaṇṭhajigṛkṣayā  |
anayadāsanarajjuparigrahe bhujalatāṃ jaḍatāmabalājanaḥ  || 9.46 ||

tyajata mānamalaṃ bata bigrahairna punareti gataṃ caturaṃ vayaḥ  |
parabhṛtābhiritīva nivedite smaramate ramate sma vadhūjanaḥ  || 9.47 ||

atha yathāsukhamārtavamutsavaṃ samanubhūya vilāsavatīsakhaḥ  |
narapatiścakame mṛgayāratiṃ sa madhumanmadhumanmathasaṃnibhaḥ  || 9.48 ||

paricayaṃ calakṣyanipātane bhayaruṣośca tadiṅgitabodhanam  |
śramajayātpraguṇāṃ ca karotyasau tanumato'numataḥ sacivairyayau  || 9.49 ||

mṛgavanopagamakṣamaveṣabhṛdvipulakaṇṭhaniṣaktaśarāsanaḥ  |
gaganamaśvakhuroddhuta reṇubhirnṛsavitā savitānamivākarot || 9.50 ||

grathitamaulirasau vanamālayā taru palāśasavarṇatanucchadaḥ  |
turagavalganacaṇcalakuṇḍalo viruruce ruruceṣṭitabhūmiṣu  || 9.51 ||

tanulatāviniveśitavigrahā bhramarasaṃkramitekṣaṇavṛttayaḥ  |
dadṛṣuradhvani taṃ vanadevatāḥ sunayanaṃ nayananditakosalam  || 9.52 ||

śvagaṇvāgurikaiḥ prathamāsthitaṃ vyapagatānaladasyu viveśa saḥ  |
sthiraturaṃgamabhūmi nipānavanmṛgavayogavayopacitaṃ vanam  || 9.53 ||

atha nabhasya iva tridaśāyudhaṃ kanakapiṅgataḍidguṇasaṃyutam  |
dhanurradhijyamanādhirupādade naravaro ravaroṣitakesarī  || 9.54 ||

tasya stanapraṇayibhirmuhureṇaśāvairvyāhanyamānahariṇīgamanaṃ purastāt |
āvirbabhūva kuśagarbhamukhaṃ mṛgāṇāṃ yūthaṃ tadagrasaragarvitakṛṣnasāram  || 9.55 ||

tatprārthitaṃ javanvājigatena rājñā tūṇīmukhoddhṛtaśareṇa viśīrṇapaṅkti  |
śyāmīcakāra vanamākuladṛśṭipātairvateritotpaladalaprakarairivāmbhaḥ  || 9.56 ||

lakṣyīkṛtasya hariṇasya hariprabhāvaḥ prekṣya sthitāṃ sahacarīṃ vyavadhāya deham  |
ākarṇakṛṣṭamapi kāmitayā sa dhanvī bāṇaṃ kṛpāmṛdhumanāḥ pratisaṃjahāra  || 9.57 ||

tasyāpareṣvapi mṛgeṣu śarānmumukṣoḥ karṇāntametya bibhide nibiḍo'pi muṣṭiḥ  |
trāsātimātracaṭulaiḥ smarayatsu netraiḥ prauḍhapriyānayanavibhramaceṣṭitāni  || 9.58 ||

uttasthuṣaḥ śiśirapalvalapaṅkamadhyānmustāprarohakavalāvayavānukīrṇam  |
jagrāha sa drutavarāhakulasya mārgaṃ suvyaktamārdrapadapaṅktibhirāyatābhiḥ  || 9.59 ||

taṃ vāhanādavanatottarakāyamīṣadvidhyantamuddhatasaṭāḥ pratihantumīṣuḥ  |
nātmānamasya vividuḥ sahasā varāhā vṛkeṣu viddhamiṣubhirjaghanāśrayeṣu  || 9.60 ||

tenābhighātarabhasasya vikṛṣya pattrī vanyasya netravivare mahiṣasya muktaḥ  |
nirbhidya vigrahamaśoṇitaliptapuṅkhastaṃ pātayāṃ prathamamāsa papāta paścāt || 9.61 ||

prāyo viṣāṇaparimoṣalaghūttamāṅgān khaḍgāṃścakāra nṛpatirniśitaiḥ kṣurapraiḥ  |
śṛṇgaṃ sa dṛptavinayādhikṛtaḥ pareṣāmabhyucchritaṃ na mamṛṣe na tu dīrghamāyuḥ  || 9.62 ||

vyāghrānabhīrabhimukhopatitān guhābhyaḥ phullāsanāgraviṭapāniva vāyurugṇān  |
śikṣāviśeṣalaghuhastatayā nimeṣāttūṇīcakāra śarapūritavaktrarandhrān  || 9.63 ||

nirghātograiḥ kuñjalīnāñjighāṃsurjyānirghoṣaiḥ kṣobhayāmāsa siṃhān  |
nūnaṃ teṣāmabhyasūyāparo'bhūdvīryodagre rājaśabde mṛgeṣu  || 9.64 ||

tān hatvā gajakulabaddhatīvravairān kākutsthaḥ kuṭilanakhāgralagnamuktān  |
ātmānaṃ raṇakṛtakarmaṇāṃ gajānāmānṛṇyaṃ gatamiva mārgaṇairamaṃsta  || 9.65 ||

tān hatvā gajakulabaddhatīvravairān kākutsthaḥ kuṭilanakhāgralagnamuktān  |
ātmānaṃ raṇakṛtakarmaṇāṃ gajānāmānṛṇyaṃ gatamiva mārgaṇairamaṃsta  || 9.65 ||

camarān paritaḥ pravartitāśvaḥ kvacidākarṇavikṛṣṭabhallavarṣī  |
nṛpatīniva tān viyojya sadyaḥ sitavālavyajanairjagāma śāntim  || 9.66 ||

api turagasamīpādutpatantaṃ mayūraṃ na sa rucirakalāpaṃ bāṇalakṣyī cakāra  |
sapadi gatamanaskaścitramālyānukīrṇe rativigalitabandhe keśapāśe priyāyāḥ  || 9.67 ||

tasya karkaśavihārsaṃbhavaṃ svedamānanavilagnajālakam  |
ācacāma satuṣāraśīkaro bhinnapallavapuṭo vanānilaḥ  || 9.68 ||

iti vismṛtānyakaraṇīyamātmanaḥ sacivāvalambitadhuraṃ narādhipam  |
parivṛddharāgamanubhandhasevayā mṛgayā jahāra catureva kāminī  || 9.69 ||

sa lalitakusumapravālaśayyāṃ jvalitamahauṣadhidīpikāsanāthām  |
narapatirativāhayāṃ babhūva kvacidasametaparicchadastriyāmām  || 9.70 ||

uṣasi sa gajayūthakarṇatālaiḥ paṭupaṭadhavanibhirvinītanidraḥ  |
aramata madhurāṇi tatra śṛṇvan vihagavikūjitabandimaṅgalāni  || 9.71 ||

atha jātu rurorgṛhītavartmā vipine pārśvacarairalakṣyamāṇaḥ  |
śramaphenamucā tapasvigāḍhāṃ tamasāṃ prāpa nadīṃ turaṃgameṇa  || 9.72 ||

kumbhapūraṇabhavaḥ paṭuruccairuccacāra nando'mbhasi tasyāḥ  |
tatra sa dviradabṛṃhitaśaṅkī śabdapātinamiṣuṃ visasarja  || 9.73 ||

nṛpateḥ pratiṣiddhameva tatkṛtavān paṅktiratho vilaṅghya yat |
apathe padamarpayanti hi śrutavanto'pi rajonimīlitāḥ  || 9.74 ||

hā tāteti kranditamākarṇya viṣaṇṇastasyānviṣyan vetasagūḍhaṃ prabhavaṃ saḥ  |
śalyaprotaṃ prekṣya sakumbhaṃ muniputraṃ tāpādantaḥśalya ivāsītkśitipo'pi  || 9.75 ||

tenāvatīrya turagātprathitāngvayena pṛṣṭānvayaḥ sa jalakumbhaniṣaṇṇadehaḥ  |
tasmai dvijetaratapasvisutaṃ skhaladbhirātmānamakṣarapadaiḥ kathayāṃ babhūva  || 9.76 ||

taccoditaḥ ca tamanuddhrṛtaśalyameva pitroḥ sakāśamavasannadṛśornināya  |
tābhyāṃ tathāgatamupetya tamekaputramajñānataḥ svacaritaṃ nṛpatiḥ śaśaṃsa  || 9.77 ||

tau daṃpatī bahu vilapya śiśoḥ prahartrā śalyaṃ nikhātamudahārayatāmurastaḥ  |
so'bhūtparāsuratha bhūmipatiṃ śaśāpa hastārpitairnayanvāribhireva vṛddhaḥ  || 9.78 ||

diṣṭāntamāpsyati bhavānapi putraśokādante vayasyahamiveti tamuktavantam  |
ākrāntapūrvamiva muktaviṣaṃ bhujaṃgaṃ provāca kosalapatiḥ prathamāparāddhaḥ  || 9.79 ||

śāpo'pyadṛṣṭatanayānanapadmaśobhe sānugraho bhagavatā mayi pātito'yam  |
kṛṣyāṃ dahannapi khalu kṣitimindhaneddho bījaprarohajananīṃ jvalanaḥ karoti  || 9.80 ||

itthaṃ gate gataghṛṇaḥ kimayaṃ vidhattāṃ vadhyastavetyabhitite vasudhādhipena  |
edhān hutāśanavataḥ sa muniryayāce putraṃ parāsumanugantumanāḥ sadāraḥ  || 9.81 ||

prātānugaḥ sapadi śāsanamasya rājā saṃpādya pātakaviluptadhṛtirnivṛttaḥ  |
antarniviṣṭapadamātmavināśahetuṃ śāpaṃ dadhajjvalanamaurvamivāmburāśiḥ  || 9.82 ||

tadarthamarthajñagate gatatrapaḥ kimeṣa te vadhyajano'nusiṣṭhatu  |
sa vahnisaṃskāramayācatātmanaḥ sadārasūnorvidadhe ca tannṛpaḥ  || 9.82  ||

sameyivān raghuvṛṣabhaḥ svasainikaiḥ svamandiraṃ śithiladhṛtirnivartitaḥ  |
manogataṃ gurumṛṣiśāpamudvahan kṣayānalaṃ jaladhirivāntakāspadam  || 9.82  ||



_______________________________________________________________________________




pṛthivīṃ śāsatastasya pākaśāsanatejasaḥ  |
kiṃcidūnamanūnarddheḥ śaradāmayutaṃ yayau  || 10.1 ||

na copalebhe pūrveṣāmṛṇanirmokṣasādhanam  |
sutābhidhānaṃ sa jyotiḥ śaradāmayutaṃ yayau  || 10.2 ||

manorvaṃśaściraṃ tasminnanabhivyaktasaṃtatiḥ  |
nimajjya punarutthāsyannadaḥ śoṇa ivābhavat || 10.2* ||

atiṣṭhatpratyayāpekṣasaṃtatiḥ sa ciraṃ nṛpaḥ  |
prāṅmanthādanabhivyaktaratnotpattirivārṇavaḥ  || 10.3 ||

ṛṣyaśṛṅgādayastasya santaḥ saṃtānakāṅkṣiṇaḥ  |
ārebhire jitātmānaḥ putrīyāmiṣṭimṛtvijaḥ  || 10.4 ||

tasminnavasare devāḥ paulastyopaplutā harim  |
abhijagmurnidāghārtāśchāyāvṛkṣamivādhvagāḥ  || 10.5 ||

te ca prāpurudanvataṃ bubudhe cādipūruṣaḥ  |
avyākṣepo bhaviṣyantyāḥ kāryasiddherhi lakṣaṇam  || 10.6 ||

bhogibhogādanāsīnaṃ dadṛśustaṃ divaukasaḥ  |
tatphaṇāmaṇḍalodarcirmaṇidyotitavigraham  || 10.7 ||

śriyaḥ padmaniṣaṇṇāyāḥ kṣaumāntaritamekhale  |
aṅke nikṣiptacaraṇamāstīrṇakarapallave  || 10.8 ||

prabuddhapuṇḍarīkākṣaṃ bālātapanibhāṃśukam  |
divasaṃ śāradamiva prārambhasukhadarśanam  || 10.9 ||

prabhānuliptaśrīvatsaṃ lakṣmīvibhramadarpaṇam  |
kautsubhākhyamapāṃ sāraṃ bibhrāṇaṃ bṛhatorasā  || 10.10 ||

bāhubhirviṭapākārairdivyābharaṇabhūṣitaiḥ  |
āvirbhūtamapāṃ madhye pārijātamivāparam  || 10.11 ||

daityastrīgaṇḍalekhānāṃ madarāgavilopibhiḥ  |
hetibhiścetanāvadbhirudīritajayasvanam  || 10.12 ||

muktaśeṣavirodhena kuliśavraṇalakṣmaṇā  |
upasthitaṃ prāñjalinā vinītena garutmatā  || 10.13 ||

yoganidrāntaviśadaiḥ pāvanairavalokanaiḥ  |
bhṛgvādīnanugṛhṇantaṃ saukha śāyanikānṛṣīn  || 10.14 ||

praṇipatya surāstasmai śamayitre suradviṣām  |
athainaṃ tuṣṭuvuḥ stutyamavāṅmanasagocaram  || 10.15 ||

namo viśvasṛje pūrvaṃ viśvaṃ tadanu bibhrate  |
atha viśvasya saṃhartre tubhyaṃ tredhāsthitātmane  || 10.16 ||

rasāntarāṇyekarasaṃ yathā divyaṃ payo'śnute  |
deśe deśe guṇeṣvevamavasthāstvamavikriyaḥ  || 10.17 ||

ameyo mitalokastvamanarthī prārthanāvahaḥ  |
ajito jiṣṇuratyantamavyakto vyaktakāraṇam  || 10.18 ||

ekaḥ kāraṇatastāṃ tāmavasthāṃ pratipadyase  |
nānātvaṃ rāgasaṃyogātsphaṭikasy'eva te smṛtam  || 10.19 ||

hṛdayasthamanāsannamakāmaṃ tvāṃ tapasvinam  |
dayālumanaghaspṛṣṭaṃ purāṇamajaraṃ viduḥ  || 10.20 ||

sarvajñastvamavijñātaḥ sarvayonistvamātmabhūḥ  |
sarvaprabhuranīśastvamekastvaṃ sarvarūpabhāk || 10.21 ||

saptasāmopagītaṃ tvāṃ saptārṇavajaleśayam  |
saptārcirmukhamācakhyuḥ saptalokaikasaṃśrayam  || 10.22 ||

caturvargaphalaṃ jñānaṃ kālāvasthā caturyugā  |
caturvarṇamayo lokastvattaḥ sarvaṃ caturmukhāt || 10.23 ||

abhyāsanigṛhītena manasā hṛdayāśrayam  |
jyotirmayaṃ vicinvanti yoginastvāṃ vimuktaye  || 10.24 ||

ajasya gṛhṇato janma nirīhasya hatadviṣaḥ  |
svapato jāgarūkasya yāthātmyaṃ veda kastava  || 10.25 ||

śabdādīn viṣayān bhoktuṃ carituṃ duścaraṃ tapaḥ  |
paryāpto'si prajāḥ pātumaudāsīnyena vartitum  || 10.26 ||

bahudhāpyāgamairbhinnāḥ panthānaḥ siddhihetavaḥ  |
tvayyeva nipatantyoghā jāhnavīyā ivārṇave  || 10.27 ||

tvayyāveśitacittānāṃ tvatsamarpitakarmaṇām  |
gatistvaṃ vītarāgāṇāmabhūyaḥsaṃnivṛttaye  || 10.28 ||

pratyakṣo'pyaparicchedyo mahyādirmahimā tava  |
āptavāganumānābhyāṃ sādhyaṃ tvāṃ prati kā kathā  || 10.29 ||

kevalaṃ smaraṇenaiva punāsi puruṣaṃ yataḥ  |
anena vṛttayaḥ śeṣā niveditaphalāstvayi  || 10.30 ||

udadheriva ratnāni tejāṃsīva vivasvataḥ  |
stutibhyo vyatiricyante dūreṇa caritāni te  || 10.31 ||

anavāptamavāptavyaṃ na te kiṃcana vidyate  |
lokānugraha evaiko hetuste janmakaramaṇoḥ  || 10.32 ||

mahimānaṃ yadutkīrtya tava saṃhriyate vacaḥ  |
śrameṇa tadaśaktyā vā na guṇānāmiyattayā  || 10.33 ||

iti prasādayāmāsustava saṃhriyate vacaḥ  |
bhūtārthavyāhṛtiḥ sā hi na stutiḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ  || 10.34 ||

tasmai kuśalasaṃpraśnavyañjitaprītaye surāḥ  |
bhayamapralayodvelādācakhyurnairṛtodadheḥ  || 10.35 ||

atha velāsamāsannaśailarandhrānunādinā  |
svareṇovāca bhagavān paribhūtārṇavadhvaniḥ  || 10.36 ||

purāṇasya kavestasya varṇasthānasamīritā  |
babhūva kṛtasaṃskārā caritārthaiva bhāratī  || 10.37 ||

babhau sa daśanajyotsnā sā vibhorvadanodgatā  |
niryātaśeṣā caraṇādgaṅgevordhvapravartinī  || 10.38 ||

jāne vo rakṣasākrāntāvanubhāvaparākramau  |
aṅgināṃ tamasevobhau guṇau prathmamadhyamau  || 10.39 ||

viditaṃ tapyamānaṃ ca tena me bhuvantrayam  |
akāmopanateneva sādhorhṛdayamenasā  || 10.40 ||

kāryeṣu caikakāryatvādabhyarthyo'smi na vajriṇā  |
svayameva hi vāto'gneḥ sārathyaṃ pratipadyate  || 10.41 ||

svāsidhārāparihṛtaḥ kāmaṃ cakrasya tena me  |
sthāpito daśamo mūrdhā lavyāṃśa iva rakṣasā  || 10.42 ||

sraṣṭurvarātisargāttu mayā tasya durātmanaḥ  |
atyārūḍhaṃ ripoḥ soḍhaṃ candaneva bhoginaḥ  || 10.43 ||

dhātāraṃ tapasā prītaṃ yayāce sa hi rākṣasaḥ  |
daivātsargādavadhyatvaṃ martyeṣvāsthāparāṅmukhaḥ  || 10.44 ||

so'haṃ dāśarathirbhūtvā raṇabhūmerbalikṣamam  |
kariṣyāmi śaraistīkṣṇaistacchiraḥkamaloccayam  || 10.45 ||

acirādvajvabhirbhāgaṃ kalpitaṃ vidhivatpunaḥ  |
māyāvibhiranālīḍhamādāsyadhve miśācaraiḥ  || 10.46 ||

vaimānikāḥ puṇyakṛtastyajantu marutāṃ pathi  |
puṣpakālokasaṃkṣobhaṃ meghāvaraṇatatparāḥ  || 10.47 ||

moṣyadhve svargabandīnāṃ veṇībandhānadūṣitān  |
śāpayantritapaulastyabalātkārakacagrahaiḥ  || 10.48 ||

rāvaṇāvagrahaklāntamiti vāgamṛtena saḥ  |
abhivṛṣya marutsasyaṃ kṛṣṇameghastirodadhe  || 10.49 ||

puruhūtaprabhṛtayaḥ surakāryodyataṃ surāḥ  |
aṃśairanuyayurviṣṇuṃ puṣpairvāyumiva drumāḥ  || 10.50 ||

atha tasya viśāṃpatyurante kāmyasya karmaṇaḥ  |
puruṣaḥ prababhūvāgnervismayena sahartvijām  || 10.51 ||

hemapātragataṃ dorbhyāmādadhānaḥ payaścarum  |
anupraveśādādyasya puṃsastenāpi durvaham  || 10.52 ||

prājāpatyopanītaṃ tad(?) annaṃ pratyagrahīnnṛpaḥ  |
vṛṣeva payasāṃ sāramāviṣkṛtamudanvatā  || 10.53 ||

anena kathitā rājño guṇāstasyānyadurlabhāḥ  |
prasūtiṃ cakame tasmiṃstrailokyaprabhavo'pi yat || 10.54 ||

sa tejo vaiśnavaṃ patnyorvibheje carusaṃjñitam  |
dyāvāpṛthivyoḥ pratyagramaharpatirivātapam  || 10.55 ||

arcitā tasya kausalyā priyā kekayavaṃśajā  |
ataḥ saṃbhāvitāṃ tābhyāṃ sumitrāmaicchadīśvaraḥ  || 10.56 ||

te bahujñasya cittajñe patnyau patyurmahīṣitaḥ  |
carorardhārdhabhāgābhyāṃ tāmayojayatāmubhe  || 10.57 ||

sāpi praṇayavatyāsītsapatnyorubhayorapi  |
bhramarī vāraṇasyeva madanisyandalekhayoḥ  || 10.58 ||

tābhirgarbhaḥ prajābhūtyai dadhre devāṃśasambhavaḥ  |
saurībhiriva nāḍībhiramṛtākhyābhirammayaḥ  || 10.59 ||

samamāpannasattvāstā rejurāpāṇḍuratviṣaḥ  |
antargataphalārambhāḥ sasyānāmiva saṃpadaḥ  || 10.60 ||

guptaṃ dadṛśurātmānaṃ sarvāḥ svapneṣu vāmanaiḥ  |
jalajāsigadāśārṅgacakralāñchitamūrtibhiḥ  || 10.61 ||

hemapakṣaprabhājālaṃ gagane ca vitanvatā  |
uhyante sma suparṇena vegākṛṣṭapayomucā  || 10.62 ||

bibhratyā kaustubhaṃ nyāsaṃ stanāntaravilambinam  |
paryupāsyanta lakṣmyā ca padmavyajanahastayā  || 10.63 ||

kṛtābhiṣekairdivyāyāṃ trisrotasi ca saptabhiḥ  |
brahma rṣibhiḥ paraṃ brahma gṛṇadhbirupatasthire  || 10.64 ||

tābhyastathāvidhān svapnāñchrutvā prīto hi pārthivaḥ  |
mene parārdhyamātmānaṃ gurutvena jagdguroḥ  || 10.65 ||

vibhaktātmā vibhustāsāmekaḥ kuṣiṣvanekadhā  |
uvāsa pratimācandraḥ prasannānāmapāmiva  || 10.66 ||

athāgramahiṣī rājñaḥ prasūtisamaye satī  |
putraṃ tamo'pahaṃ lebhe naktaṃ jyotirivauṣadhiḥ  || 10.67 ||

rāma ityabhirāmeṇa tenāpratima tejasā  |
nāmadheyaṃ guruścakre jagatprathamamaṅgalam  || 10.68 ||

raghuvaṃśapradīpena tenāpratima tejasā  |
rakṣāgṛhagatā dīpāḥ pratyādiṣṭa ivābhavan  || 10.69 ||

śayyāgatena rāmeṇa mātā śātodarī babhau  |
saikatāmbhojabalinā jāhnavīva śaratkṛśā  || 10.70 ||

kaikeyyāstanayo jajñe bharato nāma śīlavān  |
janayitrīmalaṃcakre yaḥ praśraya iva śriyam  || 10.71 ||

sutau lakṣmaṇaśatrughnau sumitrā suṣuve yamau  |
samyagāgamitā vidyā prabodhavinayāviva  || 10.72 ||

nirdoṣamabhavatsarvamāviṣkṛtaguṇaṃ jagat |
anvagādiva hi svargo gāṃ gataṃ puruṣottamam  || 10.73 ||

tasyodaye caturmūrteḥ paulastyacakiteśvarāḥ  |
virajaskairnabhasvadbhirdiśa ucchvasitā iva  || 10.74 ||

kṛśānurapadhūmatvātprasannatvātprabhākaraḥ  |
rakṣiviprakṛtāvāstāmapaviddhaśucāviva  || 10.75 ||

daśānanakirīṭebhyastatkṣaṇaṃ rākṣasaśriyaḥ  |
maṇivyājena paryastāḥ pṛthivyāmaśrubindavaḥ  || 10.76 ||

putrajanmapraveśyānāṃ tūryāṇāṃ tasya putriṇaḥ  |
ārambhaṃ prathamaṃ cakrurdevadhundubhayo divi  || 10.77 ||

saṃtānakamayī vṛṣṭirbhavane cāsya petuṣī  |
samaṅgalopacārāṇāṃ saivādiracanābhavat || 10.78 ||

kumārāḥ kṛtasaṃskārāste dhātristanya pāyinaḥ  |
ānandenāgrajeneva samaṃ vavṛdhire pituḥ  || 10.79 ||

svābhāvikaṃ vinītatvaṃ teṣaṃ vinayakarmaṇā  |
mumūrcha sahajaṃ tejo haviṣeva havirbhujām  || 10.80 ||

parasparāviruddhāste tadraghoranaghaṃ kulam  |
alamuddyotayāmāsurdevāraṇyamivartavaḥ  || 10.81 ||

samāne'pi hi saubhrātre yathobhau rāmalakṣmaṇau  |
tathā bharataśatrughnau prītyā dvandvaṃ babhūvatuḥ  || 10.82 ||

teṣāṃ dvayordvayoraikyaṃ bibhide na kadācana  |
yathā vāyuvibhāvasvoryathā candrasamudrayoḥ  || 10.83 ||

te prajānāṃ prajānāthāstejasā praśrayeṇa ca  |
mano jahrurnidāghānte śyāmābhrā divasā iva  || 10.84 ||

sa caturdhā babhau vyastaḥ prasavaḥ pṛthivīpateḥ  |
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāmavatāra ivāṅgabhāk || 10.85 ||

guṇairārādhayāmāsuste guruṃ guruvatsalāḥ  |
tameva caturnateśaṃ ratnairiva mahārṇavāḥ  || 10.86 ||

suragaja iva dantairbhagnadaityāsidhārairnaya iva paṇabandhavyaktayogairupāyaiḥ  |
haririva yugadīrghairdorbhiraṃṣaistadīyaiḥ patiravanipatīnāṃ taiścakāśe  || 10.87 ||


_______________________________________________________________________________





kauśikena sa kila kṣitīśvaro rāmamadhvaravighātaśāntaye  |
kākapakṣadharametya yācitastejasāṃ hi na vayaḥ samīkṣyate  || 11.1 ||

kṛcchralabdhamapi labdhavarṇabhāktaṃ dideśa munaye salakṣmaṇam  |
apyasupraṇayināṃ raghoḥ kule na vyahanyata kadācidarthitā  || 11.2 ||

yāvadādiśati pārthivastayornirgamāya puramārgasatkriyām  |
tāvadāśu vidadhe marutsakhaiḥ sā sapuṣpajalavarṣibhirghanaiḥ  || 11.3 ||

tau nideśakaraṇodyatau piturdhanvinau caraṇayornipetatuḥ  |
bhūpaterapi tayoḥ pravatsyatornamrayorupari bāṣpabindavaḥ  || 11.4 ||

tau piturnayanajena vāriṇā kiṃcidukṣitaśikhaṇḍakāvubhau  |
dhanvinau tamṛṣimanvagacchatāṃ pauradṛṣṭikṛtamārgatoraṇau  || 11.5 ||

lakṣmaṇānucarameva rāghavaṃ netumaicchadṛṣirityasau nṛpaḥ  |
āśiṣaṃ prayuyuje na vāhinīṃ sā hi rakṣaṇavidhau tayoḥ kṣamā  || 11.6 ||

rejatuśca sutarāṃ mahaujasaḥ kauśikasya padavīmanudrutau  |
uttarāṃ prati diśaṃ vivasvataḥ prasthitasya madhumādhavāviva  || 11.6* ||

mātṛvargacaraṇaspṛṣau munestau prapadya padavīṃ mahaujasaḥ  |
rejaturgativaśātpravartinau bhāskarasya madhumādhavāviva  || 11.7 ||

vīcilolabhujayostayorgataṃ śaiśavāccapalamapyaśobhata  |
toyadāgama ivoddhyabhidyayornāmadheyasadṛśaṃ viceṣṭitam  || 11.8 ||

tau balātibalayoḥ prabhāvato vidyayoḥ pathi munipradiṣṭayoḥ  |
mamlaturna maṇikuṭṭimocitau mātṛpārśvaparivartināviva  || 11.9 ||

pūrvavṛttakathitaiḥ purāvidaḥ sānujaḥ pitṛsakhasya rāghavaḥ  |
uhyamāna iva vāhanocitaḥ pādacāramapi na vyabhāvayat || 11.10 ||

tau sarāṃsi rasavadbhirambubhiḥ kūjitaiḥ śrutisukhaiḥ patatriṇaḥ  |
vāyavaḥ surabhipuṣpareṇubhiśchāyayā ca jaladāḥ siṣevire  || 11.11 ||

nāmbhasāṃ kamalaśobhināṃ tathā śākhināṃ na ca pariśramacchidām  |
darśanena laghunā yathā tayoḥ prītimāpurubhayostapasvinaḥ  || 11.12 ||

sthāṇudagdhavapuṣastapovanaṃ prāpya dāśarathirāttakārmukaḥ  |
vigraheṇa madanasya cāruṇā so'bhavatpratinidhirna karmaṇā  || 11.13 ||

tau suketusutayā khilīkṛte kauśikādviditaśāpayā pathi  |
ninyatuḥ sthalaniveśitātaṭanī līlayaiva dhanuṣī adhijyatām  || 11.14 ||

jyāniniādamatha gṛhṇatī tayoḥ prādurāsa bahūlakṣapā chaviḥ  |
tāḍakā calakapālakuṇḍalā kālikeva nibiḍā balākinī  || 11.15 ||

tīvravegadhutamārgavṛkṣayā pretacīvaravasā svanograyā  |
abhyabhāvi bharatāgrajastayā vātyayeva pitṛkānanotthayā  || 11.16 ||

udyataikabhujayaṣṭimāyatīṃ śroṇilambipuruṣāntramekhalām  |
tāṃ vilokya vanitāvadhe ghṛṇāṃ pattriṇā saha mumoca rāghavaḥ  || 11.17 ||

yaccakāra vivaraṃ śilāghane tāḍakorasi sa rāmasāyakaḥ  |
apraviṣṭaviṣayasya rakṣasāṃ dvāratāmagamadantakasya tat || 11.18 ||

bāṇabhinnahṛdayā nipetuṣī sā svakānabhuvaṃ na kevalām  |
viṣṭapatrayaparājayasthirāṃ rāvaṇaśriyamapi vyakampayat || 11.19 ||

rāmamanmathaśareṇa tāḍitā duḥsahena dṛdaye niśācarī  |
gandhavadrudhiracandanokṣitā jīviteśavasatiṃ jagāma sā  || 11.20 ||

nairṛtaghnamatha mantravanmuneḥ prāpadastramavadānatoṣitāt |
jyotirindhanaipāti bhāskarātsūryakānta iva tāḍakāntakaḥ  || 11.21 ||

vāmanāśramapadaṃ tataḥ paraṃ pāvanaṃ śruamṛṣerupeyivān  |
unmanāḥ prathamajanmaceṣṭitānyasmarannapi babhūva rāghavaḥ  || 11.22 ||

āsasāda munirātmanastataḥ śiṣyavargaparikalpitārhaṇam  |
baddhapallavapuṭāñjalidrumaṃ darśanonmukha mṛgaṃ tapovanam  || 11.23 ||

tatra dīkṣitamṛṣiṃ rakaṣaturvighnato daśarathātmajau śaraiḥ  |
lokamandhatamasātkramoditau raśāmibhiḥ śaśidivākarāviva  || 11.24 ||

vīkṣya vedimatha raktabindubhirbandhujīvapṛthubhiḥ pradūṣitām  |
saṃbhramo'bhavadapoḍhakarmaṇāmṛtvijāṃ cyutavikaṅkatasrucām  || 11.25 ||

unmukhaḥ sapadi lakṣmaṇāgrajo bāṇamāśrayamukhātsamuddharan  |
rakṣasāṃ balamapaśyadambare gṛdhrapakṣapavaneritadhvajam  || 11.26 ||

tatra yāvadhipatī makhadviṣāṃ tau śaravyamakarotsa netarān  |
kiṃ mahoragavisarpivikramo rājileṣu garuḍaḥ pravartate  || 11.27 ||

so'stramugrajavamastrakovidaḥ saṃdadhe dhanuṣi vāyudaivatam  |
tena śailagurumapyapātayatpāṇḍupattramiva tāḍakāsutam  || 11.28 ||

yaḥ subāhuriti rākṣaso'parastatra tatra visasarpa māyayā  |
taṃ kṣurapraśakalīkṛtāṃ kṛtī pattriṇāṃ vyabhajadāśramādbahiḥ  || 11.29 ||

ityapāstamakhavighnayostayoḥ sāṃyugīnamabhinandya vikramam  |
ṛtvijaḥ kulapateryathākramaṃ vāgyatasya niravartayan kriyāḥ  || 11.30 ||

tau praṇāmacalakākapakṣakau bhrātarāvabhṛthāpluto muniḥ  |
āśiṣāmanupadaṃ samaspṛśaddarbhapāṭitatalena pāṇinā  || 11.31 ||

taṃ nyamantrayata saṃbhṛtakraturmaithilaḥ sa mighilāṃ vrajan vaśī  |
rāghavāvapi nināya bibhratau taddhanuḥśravaṇajaṃ kutūhalam  || 11.32 ||

taiḥ śiveṣu vasatirgatādhvabhiḥ sāyamāśramataruṣvagṛhyata  |
yeṣu dīrghatapasaḥ parigraho vāsavakṣaṇakalatratāṃ yayau  || 11.33 ||

pratyapadyata cirāya yatpunaścāru gautamavadhūḥ śilāmayī  |
svaṃ vapuḥ sa kila kilibiṣacchidāṃ rāmapādarajasāmanugrahaḥ  || 11.34 ||

rāghavānvitamupasthitaṃ muniṃ taṃ niśamya janako janeśvaraḥ  |
arthakāmasahitaṃ saparyayā dehabaddhamiva dharmamabhyagāt || 11.35 ||

tau videhanagarīnivāsināṃ gāṃ gatāviva divaḥ punarvasū  |
manyate sma pibatāṃ vilocanaiḥ pakṣmapātamapi vañcanāṃ manaḥ  || 11.36 ||

yūpavatyavasite kiryāvidhau kālavitkuśikavaṃśavardhanaḥ  |
rāmamiṣvasanadarśanotsukaṃ maithilāya kathayāṃ bvabhūva saḥ  || 11.37 ||

tasya vīkṣya lalitaṃ vapuḥ śiśoḥ pārthivaḥ prathitavaṃśajanmanaḥ  |
svaṃ vicintya ca dhanurdurānamaṃ pīḍito duhiṭśulkasaṃsthayā  || 11.38 ||

abravīcca bhagavanmataṅgajairyadbṛhadbhirapi karma duṣkaram  |
tatra nāhamanumantumutsahe moghavṛtti kalabhasya ceṣṭitam  || 11.39 ||

hrepitā hi bahavo nareśvarāstena tāta dhanuṣā dhanurbhṛtaḥ  |
jyānighātakaṭhinatvaco bhujān svān vidhūya dhigiti pratasthire  || 11.40 ||

pratyuvāca tamṛṣirniśamyatāṃ sārato'yamatha vā kṛtaṃ girā  |
cāpa eva bhavato bhaviṣyati vyaktaśaktiraśanirgirāviva  || 11.41 ||

evamāptavacanātsa pauruṣaṃ kākapakṣakadhare'pi rāghave  |
śraddadhe tridaśagopamātrake dāhaśaktimiva kṛṣṇavartmani  || 11.42 ||

vyādideśa gaṇaḥ sapārśvagān karmukābharaṇāya maithilaḥ  |
taijasaya dhanuṣaḥ pravṛttaye toyadāniva sahasralocanaḥ  || 11.43 ||

tatprasuptabhujagendrabhīṣaṇaṃ vīkṣya dāśarathirādade dhanuḥ  |
vidrutakratumṛgānausāriṇaṃ yena bāṇamasṛjadvṛṣadhvajaḥ  || 11.44 ||

ātatajyamakarotsa saṃsadā vismayastimitanetramīkṣitaḥ  |
śailasāramapi nātiyatnataḥ puṣpacāpamiva peśalaṃ smaraḥ  || 11.45 ||

bhajyamānamatimātrakarṣaṇāttena vajraparuṣasvanaṃ dhanuḥ  |
bhārgavāya dṛḍhamanyave punaḥ kṣatramudyatamiti nyavedayat || 11.46 ||

dṛṣṭasāramatha rudrakārmuke vīryaśulkamabhinandya maithilaḥ  |
rāghavāya tanayāmayonijāṃ rūpiṇīṃ śriyamiva nyavedayat || 11.47 ||

maithilaḥ sapadi satyasaṃgaro rāghavāya tanayāmayonijāṃ  |
saṃnidhau dyutimatastaponidheragnisākṣika ivātisṛṣṭavān  || 11.48 ||

prāhiṇocca mahitaṃ mahādyutiḥ kosalādhipataye purodhasam  |
bhṛtyabhāvi duhituḥ parigrahāddiśyatāṃ kulamidaṃ nimeriti  || 11.49 ||

utsukaśca sutadārakarmaṇā so'bhavadgururupāgataśca tam  |
gautamasya tanayo'nukūlavākprārthitaṃ hi sukṛtāmakālahṛt || 11.49* ||

anviyeṣa sadṛśīṃ sa ca snuṣāṃ prāpa cainamanukūlavāgdvijaḥ  |
sadya eva sukṛtāṃ hi pacyate kalpavṛkṣaphala dharmi kāṅkṣitam  || 11.50 ||

tasya kalpitapuraskriyāvidheḥ śuśruvān vacanamagrajanmanaḥ  |
uccacāla valabhitasakho vaśī sainyareṇumuṣitārkadīdhitiḥ  || 11.51 ||

āsasāda mithilāṃ sa veṣṭayan piḍitopavanapādapāṃ balaiḥ  |
prītirodhamasahiṣṭa sā purī strīva kāntaparibhogamāyatam  || 11.52 ||

tau sametya samayasthitāvubhau bhūpatī varuṇavāsavopamau  |
kanyakātanayakautukakriyāṃ svaprabhāvasadṛśīṃ vitenatuḥ  || 11.53 ||

pārthivīmudavahadraghūdvaho lakṣmaṇastadanujāmathormilām  |
yau tayoravarajau varaujasau tau kuśadhvajasute sumadhyame  || 11.54 ||

te caturthasahitāstrayo babhuḥ sūnavo navavadhūparigrahāḥ  |
sāmadānavidhibhedavigrahāḥ siddhimanta iva tasya bhūpateḥ  || 11.55 ||

tā narādhipasutā nṛpātmajaiste ca tābhiragaman kṛtārthatām  |
so'bhavadvaravadhūsamāgamaḥ pratyayaprakṛtiyogsaṃnibhaḥ  || 11.56 ||

evamāttaratirātmasaṃbhavāṃstānniveśya caturo'pi tatra saḥ  |
adhvasu triṣu visṛṣṭamaithilaḥ svāṃ purīṃ daśaratho nyavartata  || 11.57 ||

tasya jātu marutaḥ pratīpagā vartmasu dhavjatarupramāthinaḥ  |
cikliśurbhṛśatayā varūthinīmuttaṭā iva nadīrayāḥ sthalīm  || 11.58 ||

lakṣyate sma tadanantaraṃ ravirbaddhabhīmapairveṣamaṇḍalaḥ  |
vainateyaśamitasya bhogino bhogaveṣṭita iva cyuto maṇiḥ  || 11.59 ||

śyenapakṣaparidhūsarālakāḥ sāṃdhyamegharudhirārdravāsasaḥ  |
aṅganā iva rajasvalā diśo no babhūvuravalokanakṣamāḥ  || 11.60 ||

bhāskaraśca diśamadhyuvāsa yāṃ tāṃ śritāḥ pratibhayaṃ vavāśire  |
kṣatraśoṇitapitṛkriyocitaṃ codayantya iva bhārgavaṃ śivāḥ  || 11.61 ||

tatpratīpapavanādi vaikṛtaṃ prekṣya śāntimadhikṛtya kṛtyavit |
anvayuṅkta gurumīśvaraḥ kṣiteḥ svantamityalaghayatsa tadvyathām  || 11.62 ||

tejasaḥ sapadi rāśirutthitaḥ prādurāsa kila vāhinīmukhe  |
yaḥ pramṛjya nayanāni sainikairlakṣaṇīyapuruṣākṛtiścirāt || 11.63 ||

pitryamaṃśamupavītalakṣaṇaṃ mātṛkaṃ ca dhanurūrjitaṃ dadhat |
yaḥ sasoma iva gharmadīdhitiḥ sadvijihva iva candanadrumaḥ  || 11.64 ||

yena roṣaparuṣātmanaḥ pituḥ śāsane sthibhido'pi tasthuṣā  |
vepamānajananīśiraśchidā prāgajīyata ghṛṇā tato mahī  || 11.65 ||

akṣabhījavalayena nibabhau dakṣiṇaśravaṇasaṃsthitena yaḥ  |
kṣatriyāntakaraṇaikaviṃśatervyājapūrvagaṇanāmivodvahan  || 11.66 ||

taṃ piturvadhabhavena manyunā rājavaṃśanidhanāya dīkṣitam  |
bālasūnuravalokya bhārgavaṃ svāṃ daśāṃ ca viṣasāda pārthivaḥ  || 11.67 ||

rāmanāma iti tulyamātmaje vartamānamahite ca dāruṇe  |
hṛdyamasya bhayadāyi cābhavadratnajātamiva hārasarpayoḥ  || 11.68 ||

arghyamarghyamiti vādinaṃ nṛpaṃ so'navekṣya bharatāgrajo yataḥ  |
kṣatrakopadahanārciṣaṃ tataḥ saṃdadhe dṛśamudagratārakām  || 11.69 ||

tena kārmukaniṣaktamuṣṭinā rāghavo vigatabhīḥ purogataḥ  |
aṅgulīvivaracāriṇaṃ śaraṃ kurvatā nijagade yuyutsunā  || 11.70 ||

kṣatrajātamapakāri vairi me tannihatya bahuśaḥ śamaṃ gataḥ  |
suptasarpa iva daṇḍaghaṭṭanādroṣito'smi tava vikramaśravāt || 11.71 ||

maithilasya dhanuranyapārthivaistvaṃ kilānamitapūrvamakṣaṇoḥ  |
tanniśamya bahavatā samarthaye vīryaśṛṅgamiva bhagnamātmanaḥ  || 11.72 ||

anyadā jagati rāma ityayaṃ śabda uccarita eva māmagāt |
vrīḍamāvahati me sa saṃprati vyastavṛttirudayonmukhe tvayi  || 11.73 ||

bibhrato'stramacale'pyakuṇṭhitaṃ dvau matau mama ripū samāgasau  |
homadhenuharaṇācca haihayastvaṃ ca irtimapahartumudyataḥ  || 11.74 ||

kṣatriyāntakaraṇo'pi vikramastena māmavati nājite tvayi  |
pāvakasya mahimā sa gaṇyate kakṣavajjvalati sāgare'pi yaḥ  || 11.75 ||

viddhi cāttabalamojasā hareraiśvaraṃ dhanurabhāji yattvayā  |
khātamūlamanilo nadīrayaiḥ pātayatyapi mṛdustaṭadrumam  || 11.76 ||

tanmadīyamidamāyudhaṃ jyayā saṃgamayya saśaraṃ vikṛṣyatām  |
tiṣṭhatu pradhanamevamapyahaṃ tulyabāhutarasā jitastvayā  || 11.77 ||

kātaro'si yadi vodgatārciṣā tarjitaḥ paraśudhārayā mama  |
jyānighātakaṭhināṅgulirvṛthā badhyatāmabhayayācanāñjaliḥ  || 11.78 ||

evamuktavati bhīmadarśane bhārgave smitavikampitādharaḥ  |
taddhanurgrahaṇameva rāghavaḥ pratyapadyata samarthamuttaram  || 11.79 ||

pūrvajanmadhanuṣā samāgataḥ so'timātralaghudarśano'bhavat |
kevalo'pi subhago navāmbudaḥ kiṃ punastridaśacāpalāñchitaḥ  || 11.80 ||

tena bhūminihitaikakoṭi tatkārmukaṃ ca balinādhiropitam  |
niṣprabhaśca ripurāsa bhūbhṛtāṃ dhūmaśeṣa iva dhūmaketanaḥ  || 11.81 ||

tāvubhāvapi parasparasthitau vardhamānaparihīnatejasau  |
paśyati sma janatā dinātyaye pārvaṇau śaśidivākarāviva  || 11.82 ||

taṃ kṛpāmṛduravekṣya bhārgavaṃ rāghavaḥ skhalitavīryamātmani  |
svaṃ ca saṃhitamamoghamāśugaṃ vyājahāra harasūnasaṃnibhaḥ  || 11.83 ||

na prahartumalamasmi nidayaṃ vipra ityabhibhavatyapi tvayi  |
śaṃṣa kiṃ gatimanena pattriṇā hanmi lokamuta te makhārjitam  || 11.84 ||

pratyuvāca tamṛṣirna tattvatastvāṃ na vedmi puruṣaṃ purātanam  |
gāṃ gatasya tava dhāma vaiṣṇavaṃ kopito hyasi mayā didṛkṣuṇā  || 11.85 ||

bhasmasātkṛtavataḥ pitṛdviṣaḥ pātrasācca vasudhāṃ sasāgarām  |
āhito jayaviparyayo'pi me ślāghya eva parameṣṭhinā tvayā  || 11.86 ||

tadgatiṃ matimatāṃ varepsitāṃ puṇyatīrthagamanāya rakṣa me  |
pīḍayiṣyati na māṃ khilīkṛtā svargapaddhatirabhogalolupam  || 11.87 ||

pratyapadyata tatheti rāghavaḥ prāṅmukhaśca visasarja sāyakam  |
bhārgavasya sukṛto'pi so'bhavatsvargamārgaparigho duratyayaḥ  || 11.88 ||

rāghavo'pi caraṇau taponidheḥ kṣamyatāmiti vadan samaspṛṣat |
nirjiteṣu tarasā tarasvināṃ śatruṣu praṇatireva kīrtaye  || 11.89 ||

rājasatvamavadhūya mātṛkaṃ pitryamasmi gamitaḥ śamaṃ yadā  |
nanvaninditaphalo mama tvayā nigraho'pyayamanugrahīkṛtaḥ  || 11.90 ||

sādhu yāmyahamavighnamastu te devakāryamupapādayiṣyataḥ  |
ūcivāniti vacaḥ salakṣmaṇaṃ lakṣmaṇāgrajamṛṣistirodadhe  || 11.91 ||

svaṃ niveśya kila dhāma rāghave vaiṣṇavaṃ viditaviṣṇutejasi  |
svastidānamadhikṛtya cākṣayaṃ bhārgavo'tha nijamāśramaṃ yayau  || 11.91* ||

tasmin gate vijayinaṃ parirabhya rāmaṃ snehādamanyata pitā punareva jātam  |
tasyābhavatkṣaṇaśucaḥ paritoṣalābhaḥ kakṣāgnilaṅghitataroriva vṛṣṭipātaḥ  || 11.92 ||

atha pathi gamayitvā kḷparamyopakārye katicidavanipālaḥ śarvarīḥ śarvakalpah |
puramaviśadayodhyāṃ maithilīdarśanīnāṃ kuvalayitagavākṣāṃ locanairaṅganānām  || 11.93 ||


_______________________________________________________________________________



nirviṣṭaviṣayasnehaḥ sa daśāntamupeyivān  |
āsīdāsannanirvāṇaḥ pradīpārcirivoṣasi  || 12.1 ||

taṃ karṇamūlamāgatya rāme śrīrnasyatāmiti  |
kaikeyīśaṅkayevāha palitacchadmanā jarā  || 12.2 ||

sā paurān paurakāntasya rāmasyābhyudayaśrutiḥ  |
pratyekaṃ hlādayāṃ cakre kulyevodyānapādapān  || 12.3 ||

tasyābhiṣekasaṃbhāraṃ kalpitaṃ krūraniścayā  |
dūṣayāmāsa kaikeyī śokoṣṇaiḥ pārthivāśrubhiḥ  || 12.4 ||

sā kilāśvāsitā caṇḍī bhartrā tatsaṃśrutau varau  |
udvavāmendrasiktā bhūrbilamagnāvivoragau  || 12.5 ||

tayoścaturdaśaikena rāmaṃ prāvrājayatsamāḥ  |
dvitīyena sutasyaicchadvaidhavyaikaphalāṃ śriyam  || 12.6 ||

pitrā dattāṃ rudan rāmaḥ prāṅmahīṃ pratyapadyata  |
paścādvanāya gaccheti tadājñāṃ mudito'grahīt || 12.7 ||

dadhato maṅgalakṣaume vasānasya ca valkale  |
dadṛśurvismitāstasya mukharāgaṃ samaṃ janāḥ  || 12.8 ||

sa sītālakṣmaṇasakhaḥ satyādgurumalopayan  |
viveśa daṇḍakāraṇyaṃ pratyekaṃ ca satāṃ manaḥ  || 12.9 ||

rājāpi tadviyogārtaḥ smṛtvā śāpaṃ svakarmajam  |
śarīratyāgamātreṇa śuddhilābhamamanyata  || 12.10 ||

viproṣitakumāraṃ tad(?) rājyamastamiteśvaram  |
randhrānveṣaṇadakṣāṇāṃ dviṣāmāmiṣatāṃ yayau  || 12.11 ||

athānāthāḥ prakṛtayo mātṛbandhunivāsinam  |
maulairānāyayāmāsurbhartaṃ stambhitāśrubhiḥ  || 12.12 ||

śrutvā tathāvidhaṃ mṛtyuṃ kaikeyītanayaḥ pituḥ  |
māturna kevalaṃ svasyāḥ śriyo'pyāsītparāṅmukhaḥ  || 12.13 ||

sasainyaścānvagādrāmaṃ darśitānāśramālayaiḥ  |
tasya paśyan sasaumitrerudaśrurvasatidrumān  || 12.14 ||

citrakūṭavanasthaṃ ca kathitasvargatirguroḥ  |
lakṣmyā nimantrayāṃ cakre tamanucchiṣṭasaṃpadā  || 12.15 ||

sa hi prathamaje tasminnakṛtaśrīparigrahe  |
parivettāramātmānaṃ mene svīkaraṇādbhuvaḥ  || 12.16 ||

tamaśakyamapākraṣṭuṃ nirdeśātsvargiṇaḥ pituḥ  |
yayāce pāduke paścātkartuṃ rājyādhidevate  || 12.17 ||

sa visṛṣṭastathetyuktvā bhrātrā naivāviśatpurīm  |
nandigrāmagatastasya rājyaṃ nyāsamivābhunak || 12.18 ||

dṛḍhabhaktiriti jyeṣṭhe rājyatṛṣṇāparāṅmukhaḥ  |
mātuḥ pāpasya śuddhyarthaṃ prāyaścittamivākarot || 12.19 ||

rāmo'pi saha vaidehyā vane vanyena vartayan  |
cacāra sānujaḥ śānto vṛddhekṣvākuvrataṃ yuvā  || 12.20 ||

prabhāvastambhitacchāyamāśritaḥ sa vanaspatim  |
kadācidaṅke sītāyāḥ śiśye kiṃcidiva śramāt || 12.21 ||

aindriḥ kila nakhaistasyā vidadāra stanau dvijaḥ  |
priyopabhogacihneṣu paurobhāgyamivācaran  || 12.22 ||

mṛgamāṃsaṃ tataḥ sītāṃ rakṣantīmātape dhṛtam  |
pakṣatuṇḍanakhāghātairbabādhe vāyaso balāt || 12.22* ||

tasminnāsthadiṣīkāstraṃ rāmo rāmāvabhodhitaḥ  |
ātmānaṃ mumuce tasmādekanetravyayena saḥ  || 12.23 ||

rāmastvāsannadeśatvādbharatāgamanaṃ punaḥ  |
āśaṅkyotsukasāraṅgāṃ citrakūṭasthalīṃ jahau  || 12.24 ||

prayayāvātitheyeṣu vasannṛṣikuleṣu saḥ  |
dakṣiṇāṃ diśamṛkṣeṣu vārṣikeṣviva bhāskaraḥ  || 12.25 ||

babhau tamanugacchantī videhādhipateḥ sutā  |
pratiṣiddhāpi kaikeyyā lakṣmīriva guṇonmukhī  || 12.26 ||

anusūyātisṛṣṭena puṇyagandhena kānanam  |
sā cakārāṅgarāgeṇa puṣpoccalita ṣaṭpadam  || 12.27 ||

saṃdhyābhrakapiṣastatra virādho nāma rākṣasaḥ  |
atiṣṭhanmārgamāvṛtya rāmasyendoriva grahaḥ  || 12.28 ||

sa jaharā tayormadhye maithilīṃ lokaśoṣaṇaḥ  |
nabhonabhasyayorvṛṣṭimavagraha ivāntare  || 12.29 ||

taṃ viniṣpiṣya kākutsthau purā dūṣayati shtalīm  |
gandhenāśucinā ceti vasudhāyāṃ nicakhnatuḥ  || 12.30 ||

pañcavaṭyāṃ tato rāmaḥ śāsanātkumbhajanmanaḥ  |
anapoṣhasthitistasthau vindhyādriḥ prakṛtāviva  || 12.31 ||

rāvaṇāvarajā tatra rāghavaṃ madanāturā  |
abhipede nidāghārtā vyālīva malayadrumam  || 12.32 ||

sā sītāsaṃnidhāveva taṃ vavre kathitānvayā  |
atyārūḍho hi nārīṇāmakālajño maobhavaḥ  || 12.33 ||

kalatravānahaṃ bāle kanīyāṃsaṃ bhajasva me  |
iti rāmo vṛṣasyantīṃ vṛṣaskandhaḥ śaśāsa tām  || 12.34 ||

jyeṣṭhābhigamanātpūrvaṃ tenāpyanabhinanditā  |
sābhūdrāmāśrayā bhūyo nadīvobhayakūlabhāk || 12.35 ||

saṃrambhaṃ maithilīhāsaḥ kṣaṇaṃ saumyāṃ nināya tām  |
nivātastimitāṃ velāṃ candrodaya ivodadheḥ  || 12.36 ||

phalamasyopahāsasya sadyaḥ prāpsyasi paśya mām  |
mṛgyaḥ paribhavo vyāghryāmityavehi tvayā kṛtam  || 12.37 ||

ityuktvā maithilīṃ bharturaṅke nirviśatīṃ bhayāt |
rūpaṃ śūrpaṇakhānāmnaḥ sadṛśaṃ pratyapadyata  || 12.38 ||

lakṣmaṇaḥ prathamaṃ śrutvā kokilāmañjubhāṣiṇīm  |
śivāghorasvanāṃ paścādbubudhe vikṛteti tām  || 12.39 ||

parṇaśālāmatha kṣipraṃ vidhṛtāsiḥ praviśya saḥ  |
vairūpyapaunaruktyena bhīṣaṇāṃ tāmayojayat || 12.40 ||

sā vakranakhadhāriṇyā veṇukarkaśaparvayā  |
aṅkuśākārayāṅgulyā tāvatarjayadambare  || 12.41 ||

prāpya cāśu jansthānaṃ kharādibhyastathāvidham  |
rāmopakramamācakhyau rakṣaḥparibhavaṃ navam  || 12.42 ||

mukhāvayavalūṇāṃ tāṃ nairṛtā yatpurodadhuḥ  |
rāmābhiyāyināṃ teṣāṃ tadevābhūdamaṅgalam  || 12.43 ||

udāyudhānāpatatastān dṛptān prekṣya rāghavaḥ  |
nidadhe vijayāśaṃsāṃ cāpe sītāṃ ca lakṣmaṇe  || 12.44 ||

eko dāśarathī rāmo yātudhānāḥ sahasraśaḥ  |
te tu yāvanta evājau tāvāṃśca dadṛśe sa taiḥ  || 12.45 ||

asajjanena kākutsthaḥ prayuktamatha dūṣaṇam  |
na cakṣame śubhācāraḥ sa dūṣaṇamivātmanaḥ  || 12.46 ||

taṃ śaraiḥ pratijagrāha kharatiśirasau ca saḥ  |
khramaśaste punastasya cāpātsamamivodyayuḥ  || 12.47 ||

taistrayāṇāṃ śitairbāṇairyathāpūrvaviśuddhibhiḥ  |
āyurdehātigaiḥ pītaṃ rudhiraṃ tu patatribhiḥ  || 12.48 ||

tasmin rāmaśarotkṛtte bale mahati rakṣasām  |
utthitaṃ dadṛśe'nyacca kabandhebhyo na kiṃcana  || 12.49 ||

sā bāṇavarṣiṇaṃ rāmaṃ yodhayitvā suradviṣām  |
aprabodhāya suṣvāpa gṛdhracchāye varūthinī  || 12.50 ||

rāghavāstravidīrṇānāṃ rāvaṇaṃ prati rakṣasām  |
teṣāṃ śūrpaṇakhaivaikā duṣpratvṛttiharābhavat || 12.51 ||

nigrahātsvasurāptānāṃ vadhācca dhanadānujaḥ  |
rāmeṇa nihataṃ mene padaṃ daśasu mūrdhasu  || 12.52 ||

rakṣasā mṛgarūpeṇa vañcayitvā sa rāghavau  |
jaharā sītāṃ pakṣīndraprayāsakṣaṇavighnitaḥ  || 12.53 ||

tau sītānveṣiṇau gṛdhraṃ lūnapakṣamapaśyatām  |
prāṇairdaśarathaprīteranṛṇaṃ kaṇṭhavartibhiḥ  || 12.54 ||

sa rāvaṇahṛtāṃ tābhyāṃ vacasācaṣṭa maithilīm  |
ātmanaḥ sumahatkarma vraṇairāvedya saṃsthitaḥ  || 12.55 ||

tayosrāvaṇahṛtāṃ tābhyāṃ pitṛvyāpattiśokayoḥ  |
pitarīvāgnisaṃskārātparā vavṛtire kriyāḥ  || 12.56 ||

vadhanirdhūtaśāpasya kabandhasyopadeśataḥ  |
mumūrcha sakhyaṃ rāmasya samānavyasane harau  || 12.57 ||

sa hatvā vālinaṃ vīrastatpade cirakāṅkṣite  |
dhātoḥ sthāna ivādeśaṃ sugrīvaṃ saṃnyaveśayat || 12.58 ||

itastataśca vaidehīmanveṣṭuṃ bhartṛcoditāḥ  |
kapayaścerurārtasya rāmasyeva manorathāḥ  || 12.59 ||

pravṛttāvupalabdhāyāṃ tasyāḥ saṃpātidarśanāt |
mārutiḥ sāgaraṃ tīrṇaḥ saṃsāramiva nirmamaḥ  || 12.60 ||

dṛṣṭā vicinvatā tena laṅkāyāṃ rākṣasīvṛtā  |
jānakī viṣavallībhiḥ parīteva mahauṣadhiḥ  || 12.61 ||

tasyai bharturabhijñāmamaṅgulīyaṃ dadau kapiḥ  |
pratyudgatamivānuṣṇaistadānandāśrubhindubhiḥ  || 12.62 ||

nirvāpya priyasaṃdeśaiḥ sītāmakṣavadhoddhataḥ  |
sa dadāha purīṃ laṅkāṃ kṣaṇasoḍhārinigrahaḥ  || 12.63 ||

pratyabhijñānaratnaṃ ca rāmāyādarśayatkṛtī  |
hṛdayaṃ svayamāyātaṃ vaidehyā iva mūrtimat || 12.64 ||

sa prāpa hṛdayanyastamaṇiparśanimīlitaḥ  |
apayodharasaṃsargaṃ priyāliṅgananirvṛtim  || 12.65 ||

śrutvā rāmaḥ priyodantaṃ mene tatsaṃgamotsukaḥ  |
mahārṇavaparikṣepaṃ laṅkāyāḥ parikhālaghum  || 12.66 ||

sa pratasthe'rināśāya harisainyairanudrutaḥ  |
na kevalaṃ dharāpṛṣṭhe vyomni saṃbādhavartibhiḥ  || 12.67 ||

nirviṣṭamudadheḥ kūle taṃ prapede vibhīṣaṇaḥ  |
snehādrākṣasalakṣmyeva buddhimādiśya coditaḥ  || 12.68 ||

tasmai niśācaraiśvaryaṃ pratiśuśrāva rāghavaḥ  |
kāle khalu samārabdhāḥ phalaṃ badhnanti nītayaḥ  || 12.69 ||

sa setuṃ bandhayāmāsa plavagairlavaṇāmbhasi  |
rasātalādivonmagnaṃ śeṣaṃ svapnāya śārṅgiṇaḥ  || 12.70 ||

tenottīrya pathā laṅkāṃ rodhayāmāsa piṅgalaiḥ  |
dvitīyaṃ hemaprākāraṃ kurvadbhiriva vānaraiḥ  || 12.71 ||

raṇaḥ pravavṛte tatra bhīmaḥ plavagarakṣasām  |
digvijṛmbhitakākutsthapaulastyajayaghoṣaṇaḥ  || 12.72 ||

pādapāviddhaparighaḥ śilāniṣpiṣṭamudgaraḥ  |
atiśastranakhanyāsaḥ śailarugṇa mataṅgajaḥ  || 12.73 ||

atha rāmaśiraśchedadarśanodbhrāntacetanām  |
sītāṃ māyeti śaṃsantī trijaṭā samajīvayat || 12.74 ||

kāmaṃ jīvati me nātha iti sā vijahau śucam  |
prāṅmatvā satyamasyāntaṃ jīvitāsmīti lajjitā  || 12.75 ||

garuḍāpātaviśliṣṭameghanādāstrabandhanaḥ  |
dāśarathyoḥ kṣaṇakleśaḥ svapnavṛtta ivābhavat || 12.76 ||

tato bibheda paulastyaḥ śaktyā vakṣasi lakṣmaṇam  |
rāmastvanāhato'pyāsīdvidīrṇahṛdayaḥ śucā  || 12.77 ||

sa mārutisamānītamahauṣadhihatavyathaḥ  |
laṅkāstrīṇāṃ punaścakre vilāpācāryakaṃ śaraiḥ  || 12.78 ||

sa nādaṃ meghanādasya dhanuścendrāyudhaprabham  |
meghasyeva śaratkālo na kiṃcitparyaśeṣayat || 12.79 ||

kleśena mahatā nidrāṃ tyājitaṃ raṇadurjayam  |
rāvaṇaḥ preṣayāmāsa yuddhāyānujamātmanaḥ  || 12.79  ||

jaghāna sa tadādeśātkapīnugrānanekaśaḥ  |
viveśa ca purīṃ laṅkāṃ samādāya harīśvaram  || 12.79  ||

kumbhakarṇaḥ kapīndreṇa tulyāvasthaḥ svasuḥ kṛtaḥ  |
rurodha rāmaṃ śṛṅgīva ṭaṅkacchinnamanaḥśilaḥ  || 12.80 ||

akāle bodhito bhrātrā priyasvapno vṛthā bhavān  |
rāmeṣubhiritīvāsau dīrghanidrāṃ praveśitaḥ  || 12.81 ||

itarāṇyapi rakṣāṃsi peturvānarakoṭiṣu  |
rajāṃsi samarotthāni racchoṇitanandīṣviva  || 12.82 ||

niryayāvatha paulasthyaḥ punaryuddhāya mandirāt |
arāvaṇamarāmaṃ vā jagadadyeti niścitaḥ  || 12.83 ||

rāmaṃ padātimālokya laṅkeṣaṃ ca varūthinam  |
hariyugyaṃ rathaṃ tasmai parjighāya puraṃdaraḥ  || 12.84 ||

tamādhūtadvajapaṭaṃ vyomagaṅgormivāyubhiḥ  |
devasūtabhujālambī jaitramadhyāsta rāghavaḥ  || 12.85 ||

mātalistasya māhendramāmumoca tanucchadam  |
yatrotpaladadalaklaibyamastrāṇyāpuḥ suradviṣām  || 12.86 ||

anyonyadarśanaprāptavikramāvasaraṃ cirāt |
rāmarāvaṇayoryuddhaṃ caritārthamivābhavat || 12.87 ||

bhujamūrdhorubāhulyādeko'pi dhandānujaḥ  |
dadṛśe so'yathāpūrvo mātṛvaṃśa iva sthitaḥ  || 12.88 ||

jetāraṃ lokapālānāṃ svamukhairarciteśvaram  |
rāmastulitakailāsamarātiṃ bahvamanyata  || 12.89 ||

tasya sphurati paulastyahsītāsaṃgamaśaṃsini  |
nicakhānādhikakrodhaḥ śaraṃ savyetare bhuje  || 12.90 ||

rāvaṇasyāpi rāmāsto bhittvā hṛdayamāśugaḥ  |
viveśa bhuvamākhyātumuragebhya iva priyam  || 12.91 ||

vacasaiva tayorvākyamastramastreṇa nighnatoḥ  |
anyonyajayasaṃrambho vavṛdhe vādinoriva  || 12.92 ||

vikramavyatihāreṇa astramastreṇa nighnatoḥ  |
jayaśrīrantarā vedirmattavāraṇayoriva  || 12.93 ||

kṛtapratikṛtaprītaistayormuktāṃ surāsuraiḥ  |
parasparaṃ śaravrātāḥ puṣpavṛṣṭiṃ na sehire  || 12.94 ||

ayaḥśaṅkucitāṃ rakṣaḥ śataghnīmatha śatrave  |
hṛtāṃ vaivasvatasyeva kūṭaśālmalimakṣipat || 12.95 ||

rāghavo rathamaprāptāṃ tāmāśāṃ ca suradviṣām  |
ardhacandramukhairbāṇaiściccheda kadalīsukham  || 12.96 ||

amoghaṃ saṃdadhe cāsmai dhanuṣyakeadhnurdharaḥ  |
brāhmamastraṃ priyāśokaśalyaniṣkarṣaṇauṣadham  || 12.97 ||

tadvyomni daśadhā bhinnaṃ dadṛśe dīptimanmukham  |
vapurmahoragasyeva karālaphaṇamaṇḍalam  || 12.98 ||

tena mantraprayuktena nimeṣārdhādapātayat |
sa rāvaṇaśiraḥpaṅktimajñātavraṇavedanām  || 12.99 ||

bālārkapratimevāpsu vīcibhinnā patiṣyataḥ  |
rarāja rakṣaḥkāyasya kaṇṭhacchedapraṃparā  || 12.100 ||

marutāṃ paśyatāṃ tasya śirāṃsi patitānyapi  |
mano nātiviśaśvāsa punaḥ saṃdhānaśaṅkinām  || 12.101 ||

atha madagurupakṣairlokapāladvipānāmanugatamalivṛndairgaṇḍabhittīrvihāya  |
upanatamaṇibandhe mūrdhni paulastyaśatroḥ surabhi suravimuktaṃ puṣpavarṣaṃ papāta  || 12.102 ||

yantā hareḥ sapadi saṃhṛtakārmukajyamāpṛcchya rāghavamanuṣṭhitadevakāryam  |
nāmāṅkarāvaṇaśarāṅkitaketuyaṣṭimūrdhvaṃ rathaṃ harisahasrayujaṃ nināya  || 12.103 ||

raghupatirapi jātavedoviśuddhāṃ pragṛhya priyāṃ priyasuhṛdi vibhīṣaṇe saṃgamayya śriyaṃ vairiṇaḥ  |
ravisutasahitena tenānuyātaḥ sasaumitriṇā bhujavijitavimānaratnādhirūḍhaḥ pratasthe purīm  || 12.104 ||


_______________________________________________________________________________





athāmanaḥ śabdaguṇaṃ guṇajñaḥ padaṃ vimānena vigāhamānaḥ  |
ratnākaraṃ vīkṣya mithaḥ sa jāyāṃ rāmābhidhāno harirityuvāca  || 13.1 ||

vaidehi paśy' ā malayādvibhaktaṃ matsetunā phenilamamburāśim  |
chāyāpatheneva śaratprasannamākāśamāviṣkṛtacārutāram  || 13.2 ||

guroryiyakṣoḥ kapilena medhye rasātalaṃ saṃkramite turaṃge  |
tadarthamurvīmavadārayadbhiḥ pūrvaiḥ kilāyaṃ parivardhito naḥ  || 13.3 ||

garbhaṃ dadhatyarkamarīcayo'smādvivṛddhimatrāśnuvate vasūni  |
abindhanaṃ vahnimasau bibharti prahlādanaṃ jyotirajanyanena  || 13.4 ||

tāṃ tāmavasthāṃ pratipadyamānaṃ sthitaṃ daśa vyāpya diśo mahimnā  |
viṣṇorivāsyānavadhāraṇīyamīdṛktayā rūpamiyattayā vā  || 13.5 ||

nābhiprarūḍhāmburuhāsanena saṃstūyamānaḥ prathamena dhātrā  |
amuṃ yugāntocitayogandiraḥ saṃhṛtya lokān puruṣo'dhiśete  || 13.6 ||

pakṣacchidā gotrabhidāttagandhāḥ śaraṇyamenaṃ śataśo mahīdhrāḥ  |
nṛpā ivopaplavinaḥ parebhyo dharmottaraṃ madhyamamāśrayante  || 13.7 ||

rasātalādādibhavena puṃsā bhuvaḥ prayuktodvahanakriyāyāḥ  |
asyācchamambhaḥ pralayapravṛddhaṃ muhūrtavaktrāvaraṇaṃ babhūva  || 13.8 ||

mukhārpaṇeṣu prakṛtipragalbhāḥ svayaṃ taraṅgādharadānadakṣaḥ  |
ananyasāmānyakalatravṛttiḥ pibatyasau pāyayate ca sindhūḥ  || 13.9 ||

sasattvamādāya nadīmukhāmbhaḥ saṃmīlayanto vivṛtānanatvāt |
amī śirobhistimayaḥ sarandhrairūrdhvaṃ vitanvanti jalapravāhān  || 13.10 ||

mātaṅganakraiḥ sahasotpatadbhirbhinnān dvidhā paśya samudraphenān  |
kapolasaṃsarpitayā ya eṣāṃ vrajanti karṇa kṣaṇacāmaratvam  || 13.11 ||

velānilāya prasṛtā bhujaṃgā mahormivisphūrjathunirviśeṣāḥ  |
sūryāṃśusaṃparkasamṛddharāgairvyajyanta ete maṇibhiḥ phaṇasthaiḥ  || 13.12 ||

tavādharasparadhiṣu vidrumeṣu paryastametatsahasormivegāt |
ūrdhvāṅkuraprotamukhaṃ kathaṃcitkleśadapakrāmati śaṅkhayūtham  || 13.13 ||

pravṛttamātreṇa payāṃsi pātumāvartavegādbhramatā ghanena  |
ābhāti bhūyiṣṭhamayaṃ samudraḥ pramathyamāno giriṇeva bhūyaḥ  || 13.14 ||

dūrādayaścakranibhasya tanvī tamālatālīvanarājinīlā  |
ābhāti velā lavaṇāmburāśerdhārānibaddheva kalaṅkalekhā  || 13.15 ||

velānilaḥ ketakareṇubhiste saṃbhāvayatyānanamāyatākṣi  |
māmakṣamaṃ maṇḍanakālahānervettīva bimbādharabaddhatṛṣṇam  || 13.16 ||

ete vayaṃ saikatabhinnaśuktiparyastamuktāpaṭalaṃ payodheḥ  |
prāptā muhūrtena vimānavegātkūlaṃ phalāvarjitapūgamālam  || 13.17 ||

kuruṣva tāvatkarabhoru paścānmārge mṛgaprekṣiṇi dṛṣtipātam  |
eṣā vidūrībhavataḥ samudrātsakānanā niṣpatatīva bhūmiḥ  || 13.18 ||

kvacitpathā saṃcarate surāṇāṃ kvacidghanānāṃ patatāṃ kvacicca  |
yathāvidho me manso'bhilāṣaḥ pravartate paśya tathā vimānam  || 13.19 ||

asau mahendradvipadānagandhī trimārgagāvīcivimardaśītaḥ  |
ākāśavāyurdinayauvanotthānācāmati svedalavānmukhe te  || 13.20 ||

kareṇa vātāyanalambitena spṛṣṭastvayā caṇḍi kutūhalinyā  |
āmuñcatīvābharaṇaṃ dvitīyamudbhinnavidyudvalayo ghanaste  || 13.21 ||

amī janasthānamapoḍhavighnaṃ matvā samārabdhanavoṭajāni  |
adhyāsate cīrabhṛto yathāsvaṃ cirojjhitānyāśramamaṇḍalāni  || 13.22 ||

saiṣā sthalī yatra vicinvatā tvāṃ bhraṣṭaṃ mayā nūpuramekamurvyām  |
adṛśyata tvaccaraṇāravindaviśleṣaduḥkhādiva baddhamaunam  || 13.23 ||

tvaṃ rakṣasā bhīru yato'panītā taṃ mārgametāḥ kṛpayā latā me  |
adarśayan vaktumaśaknuvatyaḥ śākhābhirāvarjitapallavābhiḥ  || 13.24 ||

mṛgyaśca darbhāṅkuranirvyapekṣāstavāgatijñaṃ samabodhayanmām  |
vyāpārayantyo diśi dakṣiṇasyāmutpakṣmarājīni vilocanāni  || 13.25 ||

etadgirermālayavataḥ purastādāvirbhavatyambarlekhi śṛṅgam  |
navaṃ yatra ghanairmayā ca tvadviprayogāśru samaṃ visṛṣṭam  || 13.26 ||

gandhaśca dhārāhatapalvalānāṃ kādambamardhodgatakesaraṃ ca  |
snigdhāśca kekāḥ śikhināṃ babhūvuryasminasahyāni vinā tvayā me  || 13.27 ||

pūrvānubhūtaṃ smaratā ca yatra kampottaraṃ bhīru tavopagūḍham  |
guhāvisārīṇyativāhitāni mayā kathaṃcidghanagarjitāni  || 13.28 ||

āsārasiktakṣitibāṣpayogānmāmakṣiṇodyatra vibhinnakośaiḥ  |
viḍambyamānā navakandalaiste vivāhadhūmāruṇalocanaśrīḥ  || 13.29 ||

upāntavānīravanopagūdhānyālakṣyapāriplavasārasāni  |
dūrāvatīrṇā pibatīva khedādamūni pampāsalilāni dṛṣṭiḥ  || 13.30 ||

atrāviyuktāni rathāṅganāmnāmanyonyadattotpalakesarāṇi  |
dvandvāni dūrāntaravartinā te mayā priye saspṛhamīkṣitāni  || 13.31 ||

imāṃ tatāśokalatāṃ ca tanvīṃ stanābhirāmastabakābhinamrām  |
tvatprāptibuddhyā pariripsamānaḥ saumitriṇā sāsramahaṃ niṣiddhaḥ  || 13.32 ||

amūrvimānāntaralambinīnāṃ śrutvā svanaṃ kāñcanakiṅkiṇīnām  |
pratyudvrajantīva khamutpatantyo godāvarīsārasapaṅktayastvām  || 13.33 ||

eṣā tvayā peśalamadhyayāpi ghaṭāmbusaṃvardhitabālacūtā  |
āhlādayatyunmukhakṛṣṇasārā dṛṣṭa cirātpañcavaṭī mano me  || 13.34 ||

atrānugodaṃ mṛgayānivṛttastaraṅgavātema vinītakhedaḥ  |
rahastvadutsaṅganiṣaṇṇamūrdhā smarāmi vānīragṛheṣu suptaḥ  || 13.35 ||

bhrūbheda mātreṇa padānmaghonaḥ prabhraṃśayāṃ yo nahuṣaṃ cakāra  |
tasyāvilāmbhaḥpariśuddhihetorbhaumo muneḥ sthānaparigraho'yam  || 13.36 ||

tretāgnidhūmāgramanindyakīrtestasyedamākrāntavimānamārgam  |
ghrātvā havirgandhi rajovimuktaḥ samaśnute me laghimānamātmā  || 13.37 ||

etanmunermānini śātakarṇeḥ pañcāpsaro nāma vihāravāri  |
ābhāti paryantavanaṃ vidūrānmeghāntarālakṣyamivendubimbam  || 13.38 ||

purā sa darbhāṅkuramātravṛttiścaranmṛgaiḥ sārdhamṛṣirmaghonā  |
samādhibhītena kilopanītaḥ pañcāpsaroyauvanakūṭabhandham  || 13.39 ||

tasyāyamantarhitasaudhabhājaḥ prasaktasaṃgītamṛdaṅgaghoṣaḥ  |
viyadgataḥ puṣpakacandraśālāḥ kṣaṇaṃ pratiṣrunmukharāḥ karoti  || 13.40 ||

havirbhujāmedhavatāṃ caturṇāṃ madhye lalāṭaṃtapasaptasaptiḥ  |
asau tapasyatyaparastapasvī nāmnā sutīkṣṇaścaritena dāntaḥ  || 13.41 ||

amuṃ sahāsaprahitekṣaṇāni vyājārdhasaṃdarśitamekhalāni  |
nālaṃ vikartuṃ janitendraśaṅkaṃ surāṅganāvibhramaceṣṭitāni  || 13.42 ||

eṣo'kṣamālāvalayaṃ mṛgāṇāṃ kaṇḍūyitāraṃ kuśasūcilāvam  |
sabhājane me bhujamūrdhvabāhuḥ savyetaraṃ prādhvamitaḥ prayuṅkte  || 13.43 ||

vācaṃyamatvātpraṇatiṃ mamaiṣa kampena kiṃcitpratigṛhya mūrdhnaḥ  |
dṛṣṭiṃ vimānavyavadhānamuktāṃ punaḥ sahasrāciṣi saṃnidhatte  || 13.44 ||

adaḥ śaraṇyaṃ śarabhaṅganāmnastapovanaṃ pāvanamāhitāgneḥ  |
cirāya saṃtarpya samidhiragniṃ yo mantrapūtāṃ tanumapyahauṣīt || 13.45 ||

chāyāvinītādhvapariśrameṣu bhūyiṣṭhasaṃbhāvyaphaleṣvamīṣu  |
tasyātithīnāmadhunā saparyā sthitā suputreṣviva pādapeṣu  || 13.46 ||

dhārāsvanodgāridarīmukho'sau śṛṅgāgralagnāmbudavaprapaṅkaḥ  |
badhnāti me bandhuragātri cakṣurdṛptaḥ kakudmāniva citrakūṭaḥ  || 13.47 ||

eṣā prasannastimitapravāhā saridvidūrāntarabhāvatanvī  |
mandākinī bhāti nagopakaṇṭhe muktāvalī kaṇṭhagateva bhūmeḥ  || 13.48 ||

ayaṃ sujāto'nugiraṃ tamālaḥ pravālamādāya sugandhi yasya  |
karṇārpiten' tākaravaṃ kapolamapārthyakālāgurupattralekhaṃ vt || 13.49 ||

anigrahatrāsavinītasattvamapuṣpaliṅgātphalabandhivṛkṣam  |
vanaṃ tapaḥsādhanametadatrerāviṣkṛtodagrataraprabhāvam  || 13.50 ||

atrābhiṣekāya tapodhanānāṃ saptarśihastoddhṛtahemapadmām  |
pravartayāmāsa kil' ānusūyā trisrotasaṃ tryambakamaulimālām  || 13.51 ||

vīrāsanairdhyānajuṣāmṛṣīnāmamī samādhyāsitavedimadhyāḥ  |
nivātaniṣkampatayā vibhānti yogādhirūḍhā iva śākhino'pi  || 13.52 ||

tvayā purastādupayācito yaḥ so'yaṃ vaṭaḥ śyāma iti pratītaḥ  |
rāśirmaṇīnāmiva gāruḍānāṃ sapadmarāgaḥ phalito vibhāti  || 13.53 ||

kvacitprabhā cāndramasī tamobhiśmuktāmayī yaṣṭirivānuviddhā  |
anyatra mālā sitapaṅkajānāmindīvarairutkhacitāntareva  || 13.54 ||

kvacitkhagānāṃ priyamānasānāṃ kādambasaṃsargavatīva  |
anyatra śubhrā śaradabhralekhā bhaktirbhuvaścandanakalpiteva  || 13.55 ||

kvacitprabhā cāndramasī tamobhiśchāyāvilīnaiḥ śabalīkṛteva  |
anyatra śubhrā śaradabhralekhā randhreṣvivālakṣyanabhaḥpradeśā  || 13.56 ||

kvacicca kṛṣṇoragabhūṣaṇeva bhasmāṅgarāgā tanurīśvarasya  |
paśyānavadyāṅgi vibhāti gaṅgā bhinnapravāhā yamunātaraṅgaiḥ  || 13.57 ||

tamisrayā śubhraniśeva bhinnā kundasragindīvaramālayeva  |
kṛttirhareḥ kṛṣṇamṛgatvaceva bhūtiḥ smarāreriva kaṇṭhabhāsā  || 13.57  ||

dṛśyārdhayā śāradameghalekhā nirdhūtanistriṃśarucā viśeva  |
gavākṣakālāgurudhūmarājyā harmyasthalīlepasudhā naveva  || 13.57  ||

tuṣārasaṃghātaśilā himādrerjātyāñjanaprastaraśobhayeva  |
patatriṇāṃ manasagocarāṇāṃ tśreṇīva kādambavihaṃgapaṅktyā  || 13.57  ||

nitāntaśuddhasphuṭikāśayogādvaiḍūryakāntyā raśanāvalīva  |
gaṅgā raverātmajayā sametā puṣpyatyudāraṃ parabhāgalekhā  || 13.57  ||

samudrapatnyorjalasaṃnipāte pūtātmanāmatra kilābhiṣekāt |
tattvāvabodhena vināpi bhūyastanutyajāṃ nāsti śarīrabandhaḥ  || 13.58 ||

puraṃ niṣādādhipateridaṃ tadyasminmayā maulimaṇiṃ vihāya  |
jaṭāsu baddhāsvarudatsumantraḥ kaikeyi kāmāḥ phalitāstaveti  || 13.59 ||

payodharaiḥ puṇyajanāṅganānāṃ nirviṣṭahemāmbujareṇu yasyāḥ  |
brāhmaṃ saraḥ kāraṇamāptavāco buddherivāvyaktamudāharanti  || 13.60 ||

jalāni yā tīranikhātayūpā vahatyayodhyāmanu rājadhānīm  |
turaṃgamedhāvabhṛtavatīrṇairikṣvākubhiḥ puṇyatarīkṛtāni  || 13.61 ||

yāṃ saikatotsaṅgasukhocitānāṃ prājyaiḥ payobhiḥ parivardhitānām  |
sāmānyadhātrīmiva mānasaṃ me saṃbhāvayatyuttarakosalānām  || 13.62 ||

seyaṃ madīyā jananīva tena mānyena rājñā sarayūrviyuktā  |
dūre vasantaṃ śiśirānilairmāṃ taraṅgahastairupagūhatīva  || 13.63 ||

viraktasaṃdhyākapiśaṃ purastādyato rajaḥ pārthivamujjihīte  |
śaṅke hanūmatkathitapravṛttiḥ pratyudgato māṃ bharataḥ sasainyaḥ  || 13.64 ||

addhā śriyaṃ pālitasaṃgarāya pratyarpayiṣyatyanaghāṃ sa sādhuḥ  |
hatvā nivṛttāya mṛdhe kharādīn saṃrakṣitāṃ tvāmiva lakṣmaṇo me  || 13.65 ||

asau puraskṛtya guruṃ padātiḥ paścādavasthāpitavāhinīkaḥ  |
vṛddhairamātyaiḥ saha cīravāsā māmarghyapāṇirbharato'bhyupaiti  || 13.66 ||

pitrā nisṛṣṭāṃ madapekṣayā yaḥ śriyaṃ yuvāpyaṅkagatāmabhoktā  |
iyanti varṣāṇi tayā sahogramabhyasyatīva vratamāsidhāram  || 13.67 ||

etāvaduktavati dāśarathau tadīyāmicchāṃ vimānamadhidevatayā viditvā  |
dyotiṣpathādavatatāra savismayābhirudvīkṣitaṃ prakṛtibhirbharatānugābhiḥ  || 13.68 ||

tasmātpuraḥsaravibhīṣaṇadarśitena sevāvicakṣaṇaharīśvaradattahastaḥ  |
yānādavātaradadūramahītalena mārgeṇa bhaṅgiracitasphaṭikena rāmaḥ  || 13.69 ||

ikṣvākuvaṃśagurave prayataḥ praṇamya sa bhrātaraṃ bharatamarghyaparigrahānte  |
paryaśrurasvajata mūrdhani copajaghrau tadbhaktyapoḍhapitṛrāhyamahābhiṣeke  || 13.70 ||

śmaśrupravṛddhijanitānanavikriyāṃśca plakṣān prarohajaṭilāniva mantrivṛddhān  |
anvagrahītpraṇamataḥ śubhadṛṣṭipātairvārttānuyogamadhurākṣarayā ca vācā  || 13.71 ||

durjātabandhurayamṛkṣaharīśvaro me paulastya eṣa samareṣu puraḥ prahartā  |
ityādṛtena kathitau raghunandanena vyutkramya lakṣmaṇamubhau bharato vavande  || 13.72 ||

saumitriṇā tadanu saṃsasṛje sa cainamutthāpya namraśirasaṃ bhṛśamāliniṅga  |
rūḍhendrajitpraharaṇavraṇakarkaśena kliśyannivāsya bhujamadhyamuraḥsthalena  || 13.73 ||

rāmājñayā haricamūpatayastadānīṃ kṛtvā manuṣyavapurāruruhurgajendrān  |
teṣu kṣaratsu bahudhā madavāridhārāḥ śailādhirohaṇasukhānyupalebhire te  || 13.74 ||

sānuplavaḥ prabhurapi kṣaṇadācarāṇāṃ bheje rathān daśarathaprabhavānuśiṣṭaḥ  |
māyāvikalparacitairapi ye tadīyairna syandanaistulitakṛtrimabhaktiśobhāḥ  || 13.75 ||

bhūyastato raghupatirvilasatpatākamadhyāsta kāmagati sāvarajo vimānam  |
doṣātanaṃ budhabṛhaspatiyogadṛśyastārāpatistaralavidyudiv'ābhravṛndam  || 13.76 ||

tatreśvareṇa jagatāṃ pralayādivorvīṃ varṣātyayena rucamabhraghanādivendoḥ  |
rāmeṇa maithilasutāṃ daśakaṇṭhakṛcchrātpratyuddhṛtāṃ dhṛtimatīṃ bharato vavande  || 13.77 ||

laṅkeśvarapraṇatibhaṅgadṛḍhavrataṃ tad(?) vandyaṃ yugaṃ caraṇayorjanakātmajāyāḥ  |
jyeṣṭhānuvṛttijaṭilaṃ ca śiro'sya sādhoranyonyapāvanamabhūdubhayaṃ sametya  || 13.78 ||

krośārdhaṃ prakṛtipuraḥsareṇa gatvā kākutsthaḥ stimitajavena puṣpakeṇa  |
śatrughnaprativihitopakāryamāryaḥ sāketopavanamudāramadhyuvāsa  || 13.79 ||


_______________________________________________________________________________




uttiṣṭha vatse nanu sānujo'sau daśāntaraṃ tatra samaṃ prapanne  |
apaśyatāṃ dāśarathī jananyau chedādivopaghnatarorvratatyau  || 14.1 ||

ubhāvubhābhyāṃ praṇatau hatārī yathākramaṃ vikramaśobhinau tau  |
vispaṣṭamasrāndhatayā na dṛṣṭau jñātau sutasparśasukhopalambhāt || 14.2 ||

ānandajaḥ śokajamaśru bāṣpastayoraśītaṃ śiśiro bibheda  |
gaṅgāsarayvorjalamuṣṇatpataṃ himādrinisyanda ivāvatīrṇaḥ  || 14.3 ||

te putrayornairṛtaśastramārgānārdrānivāṅge sadayaṃ spṛśantyau  |
apīpsitaṃ kṣatrakulāṅganānāṃ na vīrasūśabdamakāmayetām  || 14.4 ||

kleśāvahā bharturalakṣaṇāhaṃ sīteti nāma svamudīrayantī  |
svargapratiṣṭhasya gurormahiṣyāvabhaktibhedena vadhūrvavande  || 14.5 ||

uttiṣṭha vatse nanu sānujo'sau vṛttena bhartā śucinā tavaiva  |
kṛcchraṃ mahattīrṇa iti priyārhāṃ tāmūcatuste priyamapyamithyā  || 14.6 ||

athābhiṣekaṃ raghuvaṃśaketoḥ prārabdhamānandajalairjananyoḥ  |
nirvartayāmāsuramātyavṛddhāstīrthāhṛtaiḥ kāñcanakumbhatoyaiḥ  || 14.7 ||

saritsamudrān sarasīśca gatvā rakṣaḥkapīndrairupapāditāni  |
tasyāpatanmūrdhni jalāni jiṣṇorvindhyasya meghaprabhavā ivāpaḥ  || 14.8 ||

tapasviveṣakriyayāpi tāvadyaḥ prekṣaṇīyaḥ sutarāṃ babhūva  |
rājendranepathyavidhānaśobhā rasyoditāsītpunaruktadoṣā  || 14.9 ||

sa maularakṣoharimiśrasainyastūryasvanānanditapauravargaḥ  |
viveṣa saudhodgatalājavarṣāmuttoraṇāmanvayarājadhānīm  || 14.10 ||

saumitriṇā sāvarajena mandamādhūtavālavyajano rathasthaḥ  |
dhṛtātapatro bharatena sākṣādupāyasaṃghāta iva pravṛddhaḥ  || 14.11 ||

prāsādakālāgurudhūmarājistasyāḥ puro vāyuvaśena bhinnā  |
vanānnivṛttena raghūdvahena muktā svayaṃ veṇirivābhāse  || 14.12 ||

śvaśrūjanānuṣṭhitacāruveṣāṃ karṇīrathasthāṃ raghuvīrapatnīm  |
prāsādavātāyanadṛśyabandhaiḥ sāketanāryo'ñjalibhiḥ praṇemuḥ  || 14.13 ||

sphuratprabhāmaṇḍalamānusūyaṃ sā bibhratī śāśvatamaṅgarāgam  |
rarāja śuddheti punaḥ svapuryai saṃdarśita vahnigateva bhartrā  || 14.14 ||

veśmāni rāmaḥ paribarhavanti viśrāṇya sauhārdhanidhiḥ suhṛdbyaḥ  |
bāṣpāyamāṇo balimanniketamālekhyaśeṣasya piturviveśa  || 14.15 ||

kṛtāñjalistatra yadamba satyānnābhraśyata svargaphalādgururnaḥ  |
taccintyamānaṃ sukṛtaṃ taveti jahāra lajjāṃ bharatasya mātuḥ  || 14.16 ||

tathā ca sugrīvavibhīśaṇādīnupācaratkṛtrimasaṃvidhābhiḥ  |
saṃkalpamātroditasiddhayaste krāntā yathā cetasi vismayena  || 14.17 ||

sabhājanāyopagatān sa divyānmunīn puraskṛtya hatasya śatroḥ  |
śuśrāva tebhyaḥ prabhavādi vṛttaṃ svavikrame gauravamādadhānam  || 14.18 ||

pratiprayāteṣu tapodhaneṣu sukhādavijñātagatārdhamāsān  |
sītāsvahastopahṛtāgryapūjān rakṣaḥkapīndrān visasarja rāmaḥ  || 14.19 ||

taccātmacintāsulabhaṃ vimānaṃ hṛtaṃ surāreḥ saha jīvitena  |
kailāsanāthodvahanāya bhūyaḥ puṣpaṃ divaḥ puṣpakamanvamaṃsta  || 14.20 ||

piturniyogādvanavāsamevaṃ nistīrya rāmaḥ pratipannarājyaḥ  |
dharmārthakāmeṣu samāṃ prapede yathā tathaivāvarajeṣu vṛttim  || 14.21 ||

sarvāsu mātṛṣvapi vatsalatvātsa nirviśeṣapratipattirāsīt |
ṣaḍānanāpītapayodharāsu netā camūnāmiva kṛttikāsu  || 14.22 ||

tenārthavāṃllobhaparāṅmukhena tena ghnatā vighnabhayaṃ kriyāvān  |
tenāsa lokaḥ pitṛmān vinetrā tenaiva śokāpanudena putrī  || 14.23 ||

sa paurakāryāṇi samīkṣya kāle reme videhādhipaterduhitrā  |
upasthitaścāru vapustadīyaṃ kṛtvopabhogotsukayeva lakṣmyā  || 14.24 ||

tayoryathāprārthitamindriyārthānāseduṣoḥ sadmasu citravatsu  |
prāptāni duḥkhānyapi daṇḍakeṣu saṃcintyamānāni sukhānyabhūvan  || 14.25 ||

athādhikasnigdhavilocanena mukhena sītā śarapāṇḍureṇa  |
ānandayitrī pariṇeturāsīdanakṣaravyañjitadohadena  || 14.26 ||

tāmaṅkamāropya kṛśāṅgayaṣṭiṃ varṇāntarākrāntapayodharāgrām  |
vilajjamānāṃ rahasi pratītaḥ prapraccha rāmāṃ ramaṇo'bhilāṣam  || 14.27 ||

sā daṣṭanīvārabalīnihiṃsraiḥ saṃbaddha vaikhānasakanyakāni  |
iyeṣa bhūyaḥ kuśavanti gantuṃ bhāgīrathītīratapovanāni  || 14.28 ||

tasyai pratiśrutya raghupravīrastad(?) īpsitaṃ pārśvacarānuyātaḥ  |
ālokayiṣyanmuditāmayodhyāṃ prāsādamabhraṃlihamāruroha  || 14.29 ||

ṛddhāpaṇaṃ rājapathaṃ sa paśyan vigāhyamānāṃ sarayūṃ ca naubhiḥ  |
vilāsibhiścādhyuṣitāni pauraiḥ puropakaṇṭhopavanāni reme  || 14.30 ||

sa kiṃvadantīṃ vadatāṃ purogaḥ svaṛttamuddiśya viśuddhavṛttaḥ  |
sarpādhirājorubhujo'pasarpaṃ papraccha bhadraṃ vijitāribhadraḥ  || 14.31 ||

nirbandhapṛṣṭaḥ sa jagāda sarvaṃ stuvanti paurāścaritaṃ tvadīyam  |
anyatra rakṣobhavanoṣitāyāḥ parigrahānmānavadeva devyāḥ  || 14.32 ||

kalatranindāguruṇā kilaivamabhyāhataṃ kīrtiviparyayeṇa  |
ayoghanenāya ivābhitaptaṃ vaidehibandhorhṛdayaṃ vidadre  || 14.33 ||

kimātmanirvādakathāmupekṣe jāyāmadoṣāmuta saṃtyajāmi  |
ityekapakṣāśrayaviklavatvādāsītsa dolācalacittavṛttiḥ  || 14.34 ||

niścitya cānanyanivṛtti vācyaṃ tyāgena patnyāḥ parimārṣṭumaicchat |
api svadehātkimutendriyārthādyaśodhanānāṃ hi yaśo garīyaḥ  || 14.35 ||

sa saṃnipātyāvarajān hataujāstadvikriyādarśanaluptaharṣān  |
kaulīnamātmāśrayamācacakṣe tebhyaḥ punaścedamuvāca vākyam  || 14.36 ||

rājarṣivaṃśasya raviprasūterupasthitaḥ paśyata kīdṛśo'yam  |
mattaḥ sadācāraśuceḥ kalaṅkaḥ payodavātādiva darpaṇasya  || 14.37 ||

paureṣu so'haṃ vahulībhavantamapāṃ taraṅgeṣviva tailabindum  |
soḍhuṃ na tatpūrvamavarṇamīśe ālānikaṃ sthāṇumiva dviependraḥ  || 14.38 ||

tasyāpanodāya phalapravṛttāvupasthitāyāmapi nirvyapekṣaḥ  |
tyakṣyāmi vaidehasutāṃ purastātsamudranemiṃ piturājñayeva  || 14.39 ||

avaimi caināmanagheti kiṃ tu lokāpavādo balavānmato me  |
chāyā hi bhūmeḥ śaśino malatvenāropitā śuddhimataḥ prajābhiḥ  || 14.40 ||

rakṣovadhānto na ca me prayāso vyarthaḥ sa vairapratimocanāya  |
amarṣaṇaḥ śoṇitakāṅkṣayā kiṃ padā spṛśantaṃ daśati dvijihvaḥ  || 14.41 ||

tadeṣa sargaḥ karuṇārdracittairna me bhavadbhiḥ pratiṣedhanīyaḥ  |
yadyarthitā nirhṛtavācyaśalyān prāṇānmayā dhārayituṃ ciraṃ vaḥ  || 14.42 ||

ityuktavantaṃ janakātmajāyāṃ nitāntarūkṣābhiniveśamīśam  |
na kaścana bhrātṛṣu teṣu śakto niṣeddhumāsīdanuvartituṃ vā  || 14.43 ||

sa lakṣmaṇaṃ lakṣmaṇapūrvajanmā vilokya lokatrayagītakīrtiḥ  |
saumyeti cābhāṣya yathārthabhāṣī sthitaṃ nideśe pṛthagādideśa  || 14.44 ||

prajāvatī dohadaśaṃsinī te tapovaneṣu spṛhayālureva  |
saumyeti cābhāṣya yathārthabhāṣī prāpayya vālmīkipadaṃ tyajainām  || 14.45 ||

sa śuśruvānmātari bhārgaveṇa piturniyogātprahṛtaṃ dviṣadvat |
pratyagrahīdagrajaśāsanaṃ tadājñā gurūṇāṃ hyavicāraṇīyā  || 14.46 ||

athānukūlaśravaṇapratītāmatrasnubhiryuktadhuraṃ turaṃgaiḥ  |
rathaṃ sumantra pratipannaraśmimāropya vaidehasutāṃ pratasthe  || 14.47 ||

sā nīyamānā rucirān pradeśān priyaṃkaro me priya ityanandat |
nābuddha kalpadrumatāṃ vihāya jātaṃ tamātmanyasipattravṛkṣam  || 14.48 ||

jugūha tasyāḥ pathi lakṣmaṇo yatsavyetareṇa sphuratā tadakṣṇā  |
ākhyātamasyai guru bhāvi duḥkhamatyantaluptapriyadarśanena  || 14.49 ||

sā durnimittopagatādviṣādātsadyaḥ parimlānamukhāravindā  |
rājñaḥ śivaṃ sāvarajasya bhūyādityāśaśaṃse karaṇairabāhyaiḥ  || 14.50 ||

gurorniyogādvanitāṃ vanānte sādhvīṃ sumitrātanayo vihāsyan  |
avāryatevotthitavīcihastairjahnorduhitrā shitayā purastāt || 14.51 ||

rathātsa yantrā nigṛhītavāhāttāṃ bhrātṛhyāyāṃ puline'vatārya  |
gaṅgāṃ niṣādāhṛtanauviśeṣastatāra saṃdhāmiva satyasaṃdhaḥ  || 14.52 ||

atha vyavasthāpitavākkathaṃcitsaumitrirantargatabāṣpakaṇṭhaḥ  |
autpātiko megha ivāśmavarṣaṃ mahīpateḥ śāsanamujjagāra  || 14.53 ||

tato'bhiṣaṅgānilavipraviddhā prabhraśyamānābharaṇasprasūnā  |
svamūrtilābhaprakṛtiṃ dharitrīṃ lateva sītā sahasā jagāma  || 14.54 ||

ikṣvākuvaṃśaprabhavaḥ kathaṃ tvāṃ tyajedakasmātpatirāryavṛttaḥ  |
iti kṣitiḥ saṃśayiteva tasyai dadau praveśaṃ jananī na tāvat || 14.55 ||

sā luptasaṃjñā na viveda duḥkhaṃ pratyāgatāsuḥ samatapyatāntaḥ  |
tasyāḥ sumitrātmajayatnalabdho mohādabhūtkaṣṭataraḥ prabodhaḥ  || 14.56 ||

na cāvadadbharturavarṇamāryā nirākariṣṇorvṛjinādṛte'pi  |
ātmānameva sthiraduḥkhabājaṃ punaḥ punarduṣkṛtinaṃ nininda  || 14.57 ||

āśvāsya rāmāvarajaḥ satīṃ tāmākhyātavālmīkiniketamārgaḥ  |
nighnasya me bhartṛnideśaraukṣyaṃ devi kṣamasveti babhūva namraḥ  || 14.58 ||

sītā samutthāpya jagāda vākyaṃ prītāsmi te saumyacirāya jīva  |
viḍaujasā viṣṇurivāgrajena bhrātrā yaditthaṃ paravānasi tvam  || 14.59 ||

śvaśrūjanaṃ sarvamanukrameṇa vijñāpaya prāpitamatpraṇāmaḥ  |
prajāniśekaṃ mayi vartamānaṃ sūnoranudhyāyata cetaseti  || 14.60 ||

vācyastvayā madvacanātsa rājā vahnau viśuddhāmapi yatsamakṣam  |
māṃ lokavādaśravaṇādahāsīḥ śrutasya kiṃ tatsadṛśaṃ kulasya  || 14.61 ||

kalyāṇabuddheratha vā tavāyaṃ na kāmacāro mayi śaṅkanīyaḥ  |
mamaiva janmāntarapātakānāṃ vipākavisphūrjathuraprasahyaḥ  || 14.62 ||

upasthitāṃ pūrvamapāsya lakṣmīṃ vanaṃ mayā sārdhamasi prapannaḥ  |
tadāspadaṃ prāpya tayātiroṣātsoḍhāsmi na tvadbhavane vasantī  || 14.63 ||

niśācaropaplutabhartṛkāṇāṃ tapasvinīnāṃ bhavataḥ prasādāt |
bhūtvā śaraṇyā śaraṇārthamanyāṃ kathaṃ prapatsye tvayi dīpyamāne  || 14.64 ||

kiṃ vā tavātyantaviyogamoghe kuryāmupekṣāṃ hatajīvite'smin  |
syādrakṣaṇīyaṃ yadi me na tejastvadīyamantargatamantarāyaḥ  || 14.65 ||

sāhaṃ tapaḥ sūryaniviṣṭadṛṣṭirūrdhvaṃ prasūtescarituṃ yatiṣye  |
tathā yathā me jananāntare'pi tvameva bhartā na ca viprayogaḥ  || 14.66 ||

nṛpasya varṇāśramarakṣaṇaṃ yatsa eva dharmo manunā praṇītaḥ  |
nirvāsitāpyevamatastvayāhaṃ tapasvisāmānyamavekṣaṇīyā  || 14.67 ||

tatheti tasyāḥ pratigṛhya vācaṃ rāmānuje dṛśṭipathaṃ vyatīte  |
sā muktakaṇṭhaṃ vyasanātibhārāccakranda vignā kurarīva bhūyaḥ  || 14.68 ||

nṛtyaṃ mayūrāḥ kusumāni vṛkṣā darbhānupāttān vijahurhariṇyaḥ  |
tasyāḥ prapanne samaduḥkhabhāvamatyantamāsīdruditaṃ vane'pi  || 14.69 ||

tāmabhyagacchadruditānusārī kaviḥ kuśedhmāharaṇāya yātaḥ  |
niṣādaviddhāṇḍajadarśanotthaḥ ślokatvamāpadyata yasya śokaḥ  || 14.70 ||

tamaśru netrāvaraṇaṃ pramṛjya sītā vilāpādviratā vavande  |
tasyai munirdohadaliṅgadarśī dāśvān supurtrāśiṣamityuvāca  || 14.71 ||

jāne viṣṛśṭāṃ praṇidhānatastvāṃ mithyāpavādakṣubhitena bhartrā  |
tanmā vyathiṣṭhā viṣayāntarasthaṃ prāptāsi vaidehi piturniketam  || 14.72 ||

uthkātalokatrayakaṇṭake'pi satyapratijñe'pyavikatthane'pi  |
tvāṃ pratyakasmātkaluṣapravṛttāvastyeva manyurbharatāgraje me  || 14.73 ||

tavendukīrtiḥ śvaśuraḥ sakhā me satāṃ bhavocchedakaraḥ pitā te  |
dhuri sthitā tvaṃ patidevatānāṃ kiṃ tanna yenāsi mamānukampyā  || 14.74 ||

tapasvisaṃsargavinitatsattve tapovane vītabhayā vasāsmin  |
ito bhaviṣyatyanaghaprasūterapatyasaṃskāramayo vidhiste  || 14.75 ||

aśūnyatīrāṃ munisaṃniveśaistamo'pahantrīṃ tamasāṃ vigāhya  |
tatsaikatotsaṅgabalikriyābhiḥ saṃpatsyate te manasaḥ prasādaḥ  || 14.76 ||

puṣpaṃ phalaṃ cārtavamāharantyo bījaṃ ca bāleyamakṛṣṭarohi  |
vinodayiṣyanti navābhiṣaṅgāmudāravāco munikanyakāstvām  || 14.77 ||

payoghaṭairāśramabālavṛkṣān saṃvardhayantī svabalānurūpaiḥ  |
asaṃśayaṃ prāktanayopapatteḥ stanaṃdhayaprītimavāpsyasi tvam  || 14.78 ||

anugrahapratyabhinandinīṃ tāṃ vālmīkirādāya dayārdracetāḥ  |
sāyaṃ mṛgādhyāsitavedipārśvaṃ svamāśramaṃ śāntamṛgaṃ nināya  || 14.79 ||

tāmarpayāmāsa ca śokadīnāṃ tadāgamaprītiṣu tāpasīṣu  |
nirviṣṭasārāṃ pitṛbhirhimāṃśorantyāṃ kalāṃ darśa ivauṣadhīṣu  || 14.80 ||

tā iṅgudīsnehakṛtapradīpamāstīrṇamedhyājinatalpamantaḥ  |
tasyai saparyānupadaṃ dinānte nivāsahetoruṭajaṃ viteruḥ  || 14.81 ||

tatrābhiṣekaprayatā vasantī prayuktapūjā vidhinātithibhyaḥ  |
vanyena sā valkalinī śarīraṃ patyuḥ prajāsaṃtataye babhāra  || 14.82 ||

api prabhuḥ sānuśayo'dhunā syātkimutsukaḥ śakrajito'pi hantā  |
śaśaṃsa sītāparidevanāntamanuṣṭhitaṃ śāsanamagrajāya  || 14.83 ||

babhūva rāmaḥ sahasā sabāṣpastuṣāravarṣīva sahasyacandraḥ  |
kaulīnabhītena gṛhānnirastā na tena vaidehasutā manastaḥ  || 14.84 ||

nigṛhya śokaṃ svayameva dhīmān varṇāśramāvekṣaṇajāgarūkaḥ  |
sa bhrātṛsādhāraṇabhogamṛddhaṃ rājyaṃ rajoriktamanāḥ śaśāsa  || 14.85 ||


tāmekabhāryāṃ parivādabhīroḥ sādhvīmapi tyaktavato nṛpasya  |
vakṣasyasaṃghaṭṭasukhaṃ vasantī reje sapatnīrahiteva lakṣmīḥ  || 14.86 ||

sītāṃ hitvā daśamukharipurnopayema yadanyāṃ tasyā eva pratikṛtisakho yatkratūnājahāra  |
vṛttāntena śravaṇaviṣayaprāpiṇā tena bhartuḥ sā durvāraṃ kathamapi parityāgaduḥkhaṃ viṣehe  || 14.87 ||


_______________________________________________________________________________





kṛtasītāparityāgaḥ sa ratnākaramekhalām  |
bubhūje pṛthivīpālaḥ pṛthivīmeva kevalām  || 15.1 ||

lavaṇena viluptejyāstāmisreṇa tamabhyayuḥ  |
munayo yamunābhājaḥ śaraṇyaṃ śaraṇārthinaḥ  || 15.2 ||

avekṣya rāmaṃ te tasminna prajahruḥ svatejasā  |
trāṇābhāve hi śāpāstrāḥ kurvanti tapaso vyayam  || 15.3 ||

pratiśuśrāva kākutsthastebhyo vighnapratikriyām  |
dharmasaṃrakṣaṇāyaiva pravṛttirbhuvi śārṅgiṇaḥ  || 15.4 ||

te rāmāya vadhopāyamācakhyurvibudhaviṣaḥ  |
durjayo lavaṇaḥ śūlī viśūlaḥ prārthyatāmiti  || 15.5 ||

ādideśātha śatrughnaṃ teṣāṃ kṣemāya rāghavaḥ  |
kariṣyanniva nāmāsya yathārthamarinigrahāt || 15.6 ||

yaḥ kaścana raghūṇāṃ hi paramekaḥ paraṃtapaḥ  |
apavāda ivotsargaṃ vyāvartayitumīśvaraḥ  || 15.7 ||

agrajena prayuktāśīstadā dāśarathī rathī  |
yayau vanstahliḥ paśyan puṣpitāḥ surabhīrabhīḥ  || 15.8 ||

rāmādeśādanupadaṃ senāṅgaṃ tasya siddhaye  |
paścādadhyayanārthasya dhātoradhirivābhavat || 15.9 ||

ādiṣṭavartmā munibhiḥ sa gacchaṃstapatāṃ varaḥ  |
virarāja rathapṛṣṭhairvālakhilyairivāṃśumān  || 15.10 ||

tasya mārgavaśādekā babhūva vasatiryataḥ  |
rathasvanotkaṇṭhamṛge vālmīkīye tapovane  || 15.11 ||

tamṛṣiḥ pūjayāmāsa kumāraṃ klāntavāhanam  |
tapaḥprabhāvasiddhābhirviśeṣapratipattibhiḥ  || 15.12 ||

tasyāmevāsya yāminyāmantarvatnī prajāvatī  |
sutāvasūta saṃpannau kośadaṇḍāviva kṣitiḥ  || 15.13 ||

saṃtānaśravaṇādbhrātuḥ saumitriḥ saumanasyavān  |
prāñjalirmunimāmantrya prātaryuktaratho yayau  || 15.14 ||

sa ca prāpa madhūpaghnaṃ kumbhīnasyāśca kukṣijaḥ  |
vanātkaramivādāya sattvarāśimupasthitaḥ  || 15.15 ||

dhūmadhūmro vasāghandhī jvālābabhruśiroruhaḥ  |
kravyādgaṇaparīvāraścitāgniriva jaṅgamaḥ  || 15.16 ||

apaśulaṃ tamāsādya lavaṇaṃ lakṣmaṇānujaḥ  |
rurodha saṃmukhīno hi jayo randhraprahāriṇām  || 15.17 ||

nātiparyāptamālakṣya matkukṣeradya bhojanam  |
diṣṭyā tvamasi me dhātrā bhīgtenevopapāditaḥ  || 15.18 ||

iti saṃtarjya śatrughnaṃ rākṣasastajjighāṃsayā  |
prāṃśumutpāṭayāmāsa mustāstambamiva drumam  || 15.19 ||

saumitrerniśitairbāṇairantarā śakalīkṛtaḥ  |
gātraṃ puṣparajaḥ prāpa na śākhī nairṛteritaḥ  || 15.20 ||

vināśāttasya vṛkṣasya rakṣastasmai mahopalam  |
prajighāya kṛtāntasya muṣṭiṃ pṛthagiva sthitam  || 15.21 ||

aindramastramupādāya śatrughnena sa tāḍitaḥ  |
sikatābhyo'pi hi parāṃ prapede paramāṇutām  || 15.22 ||

dakṣiṇaṃ doṣamudyamya rākṣasastamupādravat |
ekatāla ivopātapavanaprerito giriḥ  || 15.23 ||

kārṣnena pattrinā śatruḥ sa bhinnarhṛdayaḥ patan  |
ānināya bhuvaḥ kampaṃ jahārāśramavāsinām  || 15.24 ||

vayasāṃ paṅktayaḥ peturhatasyopari rakṣasaḥ  |
tatpratidvandino mūrdhni divyāḥ kusumavṛṣṭayaḥ  || 15.25 ||

sa hatvā lavaṇaṃ vīrastadā mene mahaujasaḥ  |
bhrātuḥ sodaryamātmānamindrajidvadhaśobhinaḥ  || 15.26 ||

tasya saṃstūyamānasya caritārthaistapasvibhiḥ  |
śuśubhe vikramodagraṃ vrīḍayāvanataṃ śiraḥ  || 15.27 ||

upakūlaṃ sa kālindyāḥ purīṃ pauruṣabhūṣaṇaḥ  |
