nirmame nirmamo'rtheṣu mathurāṃ madhurākṛtiḥ  || 15.28 ||

yā saurājyaprakāśābhirbabhau pauravibhūtibhiḥ  |
svargābhiṣyandavamanaṃ kṛtvevopaniveśitā  || 15.29 ||

tatra saudhagataḥ paśyan yamunāṃ cakravākinīm  |
hema bhaktimatīṃ bhūmeḥ praveṇīmiva pripiye  || 15.30 ||

sakhā daśarathasyātha janakasya ca mantrakṛt |
saṃcaskārobhayaprītyā maithileyau yathāvidhi  || 15.31 ||

sa tau kuśalavonmṛṣṭagarbhakledau tadākhyayā  |
kaviḥ kuśalavāveva cakāra kila nāmataḥ  || 15.32 ||

sāṅgaṃ ca vedamadhyāpya kiṃcidutkrāntaśaiśavau  |
svakṛtiṃ gāpayāmāsa kaviprathamapaddhatim  || 15.33 ||

rāmasya madhuraṃ vṛttaṃ gāyantau māturagrataḥ  |
tadviyogavyathāṃ kiṃcicchithilīcakratuḥ sutau  || 15.34 ||

itare'pi raghorvaṃśyāstrayastretāgnitejasaḥ  |
tadyogātpativatnīṣu patnīṣvāsan dvisūnavaḥ  || 15.35 ||

śatrughātini śatrughnaḥ subāhau ca bahuśrute  |
mathurāvidiśe sūnvornidadhe pūrvajotsukaḥ  || 15.36 ||

bhūyastapovyayo mā bhūdvālmīkeriti so'tyagāt |
maithilītanayodgītaniṣpandamṛgamāśramam  || 15.37 ||

vaśī viveśa cāyodhyāṃ rathyāsaṃskāraśobhinīm  |
lavaṇasya vadhātpaurairatigauravamīkṣitaḥ  || 15.38 ||

sa dadarśa sabhāmadhye sabhāsadbhirupasthitam  |
rāmaṃ sītāparityāgādasāmaṇyapatiṃ bhuvaḥ  || 15.39 ||

tamabhyanandatpraṇataṃ lavaṇāntakamagrajaḥ  |
kālanemivadhātprītasturāṣāḍiva śārṅgiṇam  || 15.40 ||

sa pṛṣṭaḥ sarvato vārttāmākhyādrājñe na saṃtatim  |
pratyarpayiṣyataḥ kāle kaverādyasya śāsanāt || 15.41 ||

atha jānapado vipraḥ śiśumaprāptayauvanam  |
avatāry' āṅkaśayyāsthaṃ dvāri cakranda bhūpateḥ  || 15.42 ||

śocanīyāsi vasudhe yā tvaṃ daśarathāccyutā  |
rāmahastamanuprāpya kaṣṭātkaṣṭataraṃ gatā  || 15.43 ||

śrutvā tasya śuco hetuṃ goptā jihrāya rāghavaḥ  |
na hyakālabhavo mṛtyurikṣvākupadamaspṛśat || 15.44 ||

sa muhūrtaṃ sahasveti dvijamāśvāsya duḥkhitam  |
yānaṃ sasmāra kauberaṃ vaivasvatajigīṣayā  || 15.45 ||

āttaśastrastadadhyāsya pratisthaḥ sa raghūdvahaḥ  |
uccacāra purastasya gūḍharūpā sarasvatī  || 15.46 ||

rājan prajāsu te kaścidapacāraḥ pravartate  |
tamanviṣya praśamayerbhavitāsi tataḥ kṛtī  || 15.47 ||

ityāptavacanādrāmo vineṣyan varṇavikriyām  |
diśaḥ papāta pattreṇa veganiṣkampaketunā  || 15.48 ||

atha dhūmābhitāmrākṣaṃ vṛkṣākhāvalambinam  |
dadarśa kaṃcidaikśvākastapasyantamadhomukham  || 15.49 ||

pṛṣṭanāmānvayo rājñā sa kilācaṣṭa dhūmapaḥ  |
ātmānaṃ śambukaṃ nāma śūdraṃ surapadārthinam  || 15.50 ||

tapasyanadhikāritvātprajānāṃ tamaghāvaham  |
śīrṣacchedyaṃ paricchidya niyantā śastramādade  || 15.51 ||

sa tadvaktraṃ himakliṣṭakiñjalkamiva paṅkajam  |
jyotiṣkaṇāhataśmaśru kaṇṭhanālādapāharat || 15.52 ||

kṛtaṇḍaḥ svayaṃ rājñā lebhe śūdraḥ satāṃ gatim  |
tapasā duścareṇāpi na svamārgavilaṅghinā  || 15.53 ||

raghunātho'pyagastyena mārgasaṃdarśitātmanā  |
mahaujasā saṃyuyuje śaratkāla ivendunā  || 15.54 ||

kumbhayoniralaṃkāraṃ tasmai divyaparigraham  |
dadau dattaṃ samudreṇa pītenevātmaniṣkrayam  || 15.55 ||

taṃ dadhanmaithilīkaṇṭhanirvyāpāreṇa bāhunā  |
paścānnivavṛte rāmaḥ prākparāsurdvijātmajaḥ  || 15.56 ||

tasya pūrvoditāṃ nindāṃ dvijaḥ putrasamāgataḥ  |
stutyā nivartayāmāsa trāturvaivasvatādapi  || 15.57 ||

tamadhvarāya muktāśvaṃ rakṣaḥkapinareśvarāḥ  |
meghāḥ sasyamivāmbhobhirabhyavarṣannupāyanaiḥ  || 15.58 ||

digbhyo nimantritāścainamabhijagmurmaharṣayaḥ  |
na bhaumānyeva dhiṣṇyāni hitvā jyotirmayānyapi  || 15.59 ||

upaśalyaniviṣṭaistaiścaturdvāramukhī babhau  |
ayodhyā sṛṣṭalokeva sadyaḥ paitāmahī tanuḥ  || 15.60 ||

ślāghyastyāgo'pi vaidehyāḥ patyuḥ prāgvaṃśavāsinaḥ  |
ananyahāneḥ tasyāsītsaiva jāyā hiraṇmayī  || 15.61 ||

vidheradhikasaṃbhārastataḥ pravavṛte makhaḥ  |
āsan yatra kriyāvighnā rākṣasā eva rakṣiṇaḥ  || 15.62 ||

atha prācetasopajñaṃ rāmāyaṇamitastataḥ  |
maithileyau kuśalavau jagaturgurucoditau  || 15.63 ||

vṛttaṃ rāmasya vālmīkeḥ kṛtistau kiṃnarasvanau  |
kiṃ tadyena mano hartumalaṃ syātāṃ na śṛṇvatām  || 15.64 ||

rūpe gīte ca mādhuryaṃ tayostajjñairniveditam  |
dadarśa sānujo rāmaḥ śuśrāva ca kutūhalī  || 15.65 ||

tadgītaśravaṇaikāgrā saṃsadaśrumukhī babhau  |
himanisyandinī prātarnivāgteva vanasthalī  || 15.66 ||

vayoveṣavisaṃvādi rāmasya ca tayośca sā  |
janatā prekṣya sādṛśyaṃ nākśikampaṃ vyatiṣṭhata  || 15.67 ||

ubhayorna tathā lokaḥ prāvīṇyena visiṣmiye  |
nṛpateḥ prītidāneṣu vītaspṛhatayā yathā  || 15.68 ||

geye kena vinītau vāṃ kasya ceyaṃ kaveḥ kṛtiḥ  |
iti rājñā svayaṃ pṛṣṭau tau vālmīkimaśaṃsatām  || 15.69 ||

atha sāvarajo rāmaḥ prācetasamupeyivān  |
urikrtyātmano dehaṃ rājyamasmai nyavedayat || 15.70 ||

sa tāvākhyāya rāmāya maithileyau tadātmajau  |
kaviḥ kāruṇiko vavre sītāyāḥ saṃparigraham  || 15.71 ||

tāta śuddhā samakṣaṃ naḥ snuṣā te jātavedasi  |
daurātmyādrakṣasastāṃ tu nātratyāḥ śraddadhuḥ prajāḥ  || 15.72 ||

tāḥ svacāritramuddiśya pratyāyayatu maithilī  |
tataḥ putravatīmenāṃ pratipatsye tadājñayā  || 15.73 ||

iti pratiśrute rājñā jānakīmāsramānmuniḥ  |
śiṣyairānāyayāmāsa svasiddhiṃ niyamairiva  || 15.74 ||

anyedyuratha kākutsthaḥ saṃnipātya puraukasaḥ  |
kavimāhvāyayāmāsa prastutapratipattaye  || 15.75 ||

svarasaṃskāravatyeva putrābhyāṃ saha sītayā  |
ṛcevodarciṣaṃ sūryaṃ rāmaṃ munirupasthitaḥ  || 15.76 ||

kāṣāyaparivītena svapadārpitacakṣuṣā  |
kavimāhvāyayāmāsa śāntena vapuṣaiva sā  || 15.77 ||

janāstadālokapathātpratisaṃhṛtacakṣuṣaḥ  |
tasthuste'vāṅmukhāḥ sarve phalitā iva sālayaḥ  || 15.78 ||

tāṃ dṛṣṭiviṣaye bharturmunirāsthitaviṣṭaraḥ  |
kuru niḥsaṃśayaṃ vatse svavṛtte lokamityaśāt || 15.79 ||

atha vālmīkśiṣyeṇa puṇyamāvarjitaṃ payaḥ  |
ācamyodīrayāmāsa sītā satyāṃ sarasvatīm  || 15.80 ||

vāṅmanaḥkarmabhiḥ patyau vyabhicāro yathā na me  |
tathā viśvaṃbhare devi māmantardhātumarhasi  || 15.81 ||

evamukte tayā sādhvyā randhrātsadyobhavādbhuvaḥ  |
śātahradamiva jyotiḥ prabhāmaṇḍalamudyayau  || 15.82 ||

tatra nāgaphaṇotkṣiptasiṃhāsananiṣeduṣī  |
samudraraśanā sākṣātprādurāsīdvasuṃdharā  || 15.83 ||

sā sītāmaṅkamāropya bhartari prahitekṣaṇām  |
mā meti vyāharatyeva tasmin pātālamabhyagāt || 15.84 ||

dharāyāṃ tasya saṃrambhaṃ sītāpratyarpaṇaiṣiṇaiḥ  |
gururvidhibalāpekṣī śamayāmāsa dhanvinaḥ  || 15.85 ||

ṛṣīn visṛjya yajñānte suhṛdaśca puraskṛtān  |
rāmaḥ sītāgataṃ snehaṃ nidadhe tadapatyayoḥ  || 15.86 ||

yudhājitastu saṃdeśātsa deśa sindhunāmakam  |
dadau dattaprabhāvāya bharatāya dhṛtaprajaḥ  || 15.87 ||

bharatastatra gandharvān yudhi nijitya kevalam  |
ātodyaṃ grāhayāmāsa samatyājayadāyudham  || 15.88 ||

sa takṣapuṣkalau putrau rājadhānyostadākhyayoḥ  |
abhiṣicyābhiṣekārhau rāmāntikamagātpunaḥ  || 15.89 ||

aṅgadaṃ candraketuṃ ca lakṣmaṇo'pyātmasaṃbhavau  |
śāsanādraghunāthasya cakre kārāpatheśāvarau  || 15.90 ||

ityāropitaputrāste jananīnāṃ janeśvarāḥ  |
bhartṛlokaprapannānāṃ nivāpān vidadhuḥ kramāt || 15.91 ||

upetya muniveṣo'tha kālaḥ provāca rāghavam  |
rahaḥsaṃvādinau pāsyedāvāṃ yastaṃ tyajeriti  || 15.92 ||

tatheti pratipannāya vivṛtātmā nṛpāya saḥ  |
ācakhyau divamadhyāsva śāsanātparameṣṭhinaḥ  || 15.93 ||

vidvānapi tayordvāḥstahaḥ samayaṃ lakṣmaṇo'bhinat |
bhīto durvāsasaḥ śāpādrāmasaṃdarśanārthinaḥ  || 15.94 ||

sa gatvā sarayūtīraṃ dehatyāgena yogavit |
cakāra vitathāṃ bhrātuḥ pratijñāṃ pūrvajanmanaḥ  || 15.95 ||

tasminnātmacaturbhāge prāṅnākamadhitasthuṣi  |
rāghavaḥ śithilaṃ tasthau bhuvi dharmastripādiva  || 15.96 ||

sa niveśya kuśāvatyāṃ ripunāgāṅkuṣaṃ kuśam  |
śarāvatyāṃ satāṃ sūktairjanitāśrulavaṃ lavam  || 15.97 ||

udakpratasthe sthiradhīḥ sānujo'gnipuraḥsaraḥ  |
anvitaḥ pativātsalyādgṛhavarjamayodhyayā  || 15.98 ||

jagṛhustasya cittajñāḥ padavīṃ harirākṣasāḥ  |
kadambamukulasthūlairabhivṛṣṭaṃ prajāśrubhiḥ  || 15.99 ||

upasthitavimānena tena bhaktānukampinā  |
cakre tridivaniḥṣreṇiḥ sarayūranuyāyinām  || 15.100 ||

yadgopratarakalpo'bhutsaṃmardastatra majjatām  |
atastadākhyayā tīrthaṃ pāvanaṃ bhuvi paprathe  || 15.101 ||

sa vibhurvibudhāṃśeṣu pratipannātmamūrtiṣu  |
tridaśībhūtapaurāṇāṃ svargāntaramakalpayat || 15.102 ||

nirvartyaivaṃ daśamukhaśiraśchedakāryaṃ surāṇāṃ viṣvaksenaḥ svatanumaviśatsarvalokapratiṣṭhām  |
laṅkānāthaṃ pavanatanayaṃ cobhayaṃ sthāpayitvā kīrtistambhadvayamiva girau dakṣiṇe cottare ca  || 15.103 ||


_______________________________________________________________________________





athetare sapta raghupravīrā jyeṣṭhaṃ purojanmatayā juṇaiśca  |
cakruḥ kuśaṃ ratnaviśeṣabhājaṃ saubhrātrameṣāṃ hi kulānusāri  || 16.1 ||

te setuvārttāgajabandhamukhyairabhyucchritāḥ karmabhirapyavandhyaiḥ  |
anyonyadeśapravibhāgasīmāṃ velāṃ samudrā iva na vyatīyuḥ  || 16.2 ||

caturbhujāṃśaprabhavaḥ sa teṣāṃ dānapravṛtteranupāratānām  |
suradvipānāmiva sāmayonirbhinno'ṣṭhadā viprasasāra vaṃśaḥ  || 16.3 ||

athārdharātre stimitapradīpe śayyāgṛhe suptajane prabuddhaḥ  |
kuśaḥ pravāsasthakalatraveṣāmadṛṣṭapūrvāṃ vanitāmapaśyat || 16.4 ||

sā sādhusādhāraṇapārthivarddheḥ sthitvā purastātpuruhūtabhāsaḥ  |
jetuḥ pareṣāṃ jayaśabdapūrvaṃ tasyāñjaliṃ bandhumato babandha  || 16.5 ||

athānupoḍhārgalamapyagāraṃ chāyāmivādarśatalaṃ praviṣṭām  |
savismayo dāśarathestanūjaḥ provāca pūrvārdhaviṣṛṭatalpaḥ  || 16.6 ||

labhdāntarā sāvaraṇe'pi gehe yogaprabhāvo na ca lakṣyate te  |
bibharṣi cākāramanirvṛtānāṃ mṛṇālinī haimamivoparāgam  || 16.7 ||

kā tvaṃ śubhe kasya parigraho vā kiṃ vā madabhyāgamakāraṇaṃ te  |
ācakṣva matvā vaśināṃ raghūṇāṃ manaḥ parastrīvimukhapravṛtti  || 16.8 ||

tamabravītsā guruṇā navadyā yā nītapaurā svapadonmukhena  |
tasyāḥ puraḥ saṃprati vītanāthāṃ jānīhi rājannadhidevatāṃ mām  || 16.9 ||

vasaukasārāmabhibhūya sāhaṃ saurājyabaddhotsavayā vibhūtyā  |
samagraśaktau tvayi sūryavaṃṣye sati prapannā karuṇāmavasthām  || 16.10 ||

viśīrṇatalpāṭṭaśato niveśaḥ paryastaśālaḥ prabhuṇā vinā me  |
viḍambayatyastanimagnasūryaṃ dināntamugrānilabhinnamegham  || 16.11 ||

niśāsu bhāsvatkalanūpurāṇāṃ yaḥ saṃcaro'bhūdabhisārikāṇām  |
nadanmukholkāvicitāmiṣābhiḥ sa vāhyate rājapathaḥ śivābhiḥ  || 16.12 ||

āsphālitaṃ yatpramadākarāgrairmṛdaṅgadhīradhvanimanvagacchat |
vanyairidānīṃ mahiṣaistadambhaḥ śṛṅgāhataṃ krośati dīrghikāṇām  || 16.13 ||

citradvipāḥ padmavanāvatīrṇāḥ kareṇubhirdattamṛṇālabhaṅgāḥ  |
nakhāṅkuśāghātavibhinnakumbhāḥ saṃrabdhasiṃhaprahṛtaṃ vahanti  || 16.16 ||

stambheṣu yoṣitpratiyātanānāmutkrāntavarṇakramadhūsarāṇām  |
stanottarīyāṇi bhavanti saṅgānnirmokapaṭṭāḥ phaṇibhirvimuktāḥ  || 16.17 ||

kālāntaraśyāmasudheṣu naktamitastato rūḍhatṛṇāṅkureṣu  |
ta eva muktāguṇaśuddhayo'pi harmyeṣu mūrchanti na candrapādāḥ  || 16.18 ||

āvarjya śākhāḥ sadayaṃ ca yāsāṃ puṣpāṇyupāttāni vilāsinībhiḥ  |
vanyaiḥ pulindairiva vānaraistāḥ kliśyanta udyānalatā madīyāḥ  || 16.19 ||

rātrāvanāviṣkṛtadīpabhāsaḥ kāntāmukhaśrīviyutā divāpi  |
tiraskriyante kṛmitantujālairvicchinnadhūmaprasarā gavākṣāḥ  || 16.20 ||

balikriyāvarjitasaikatāni snānīyasaṃsargamanāpanuvanti  |
upāntavānīragṛhāṇi dṛṣṭvā śūnyāni dūye sarayūjalāni  || 16.21 ||

tadarhasīmāṃ vasatiṃ visṛjya māmabhyupaituṃ kularājadhānīm  |
hitvā tanuṃ kāraṇamānuṣīṃ tāṃ yathā guruste paramātmamūrtim  || 16.22 ||

tatheti tasyāḥ praṇayaṃ pratītaḥ pratyagrahītprāgraharo raghūṇām  |
pūrapyabhivyaktamukhaprasādā śarīrabandhena tirobabhūva  || 16.23 ||

tadadbhutaṃ saṃsadi rārtrivṛttaṃ prātardvijebhyo nṛpatiḥ śaśaṃsa  |
śrutvā ta enaṃ kularājadhānyā sākṣātpatitve vṛtamabhayanandan  || 16.24 ||

kuśāvatīṃ śrotriyasātsa kṛtvā yātrānukūle'hani sāvarodhaḥ  |
anudruto vāyurivābhravṛndaiḥ sainyairayodhyābhimukhaḥ pratasthe  || 16.25 ||

sā ketumālopavanā bṛhadbhirvihāraśailānugateva nāgaiḥ  |
senā rathodāragṛhā prayāṇe tasyābhavajjaṅgamarājadhānī  || 16.26 ||

tenātapatrāmalamaṇḍalena prasthāpitaḥ pūrvanivāsabhūmim  |
babhau balaughaḥ śaśinoditena velāmudanvāniva nīyamānaḥ  || 16.27 ||

tasya prayātasya varūthinīnāṃ pīḍāmaparyāptavatīva soḍhum  |
vasuṃdharā viṣṇupadaṃ dvitīyamadhyāruroheva rajaśchalena  || 16.28 ||

udyacchamānā gamanāya paścātpuro niveśe pathi ca vrajantī  |
sā yatra senā dadṛṣe nṛpasya tatraiva sāmagryamatiṃ cakāra  || 16.29 ||

tasya dvipānāṃ madavārisekātkhurābhighātācca turaṃgamāṇām  |
reṇuḥ prapede pathi paṅkabhāvaṃ paṅko'pi reṇutvamiyāya netuḥ  || 16.30 ||

mārgaiṣiṇī sā kaṭakāntareṣu vaindhyeṣu senā bahudhā vibhinnā  |
cakāra reveva mahāvirāvā baddhapratiśrunti guhāmukhāni  || 16.31 ||

sa dhātubhedāruṇayānanemiḥ prabhuḥ prayāṇadhvanimiśratūryaḥ  |
vyalaṅghayadvindhyamupāyanāni paśyan pulindairupapāditāni  || 16.32 ||

tīrthe tadīye gajasetubandhātpratīpagāmuttarato'sya gaṅgām  |
ayatnavālagvyajanībabhūvurhaṃsā nabholaṅghanalolakpakṣāḥ  || 16.33 ||

sa pūrvajānāṃ kapilena roṣādbhasmāvaśeṣīkṛtavigrahāṇām  |
surālayaprāptinimittamambhastraisrotasaṃ naululitaṃ vavande  || 16.34 ||

ityadhvanaḥ kaiścidahobhirante kūlaṃ samāsādya kuśaḥ sarayvāḥ  |
vedipratiṣṭhān vitatādhvarāṇāṃ yūpānapaśyacchataśo raghūṇām  || 16.35 ||

ādhūya śākhāḥ kusumadrumāṇāṃ spṛṣṭvā ca śītān sarayūtaraṅgān  |
taṃ klāntasainyaṃ kularājadhānyāḥ pratyujjagāmopavanāntavāyuḥ  || 16.36 ||

athopaśalye ripumagnaśalyastasyāḥ puraḥ paursakhaḥ sa rājā  |
kuladhvajastāni caladhvajāni niveśayāmāsa balī balāni  || 16.37 ||

tāṃ śilpisaṃghāḥ prabhuṇā niyuktāstathāgatāṃ saṃbhṛtasādhanatvāt |
puraṃ navīcakrurapāṃ visargānmeghā nidāghaglapitāmivorvīm  || 16.38 ||

tataḥ saparyāṃ sapaśūpahārāṃ puraḥ parārdhyapratimāgṛhāyāḥ  |
upoṣitairvāstuvidhānavidbhirnirvartayāmāsa raghupavīraḥ  || 16.39 ||

tasyāḥ sa rājopapadaṃ niśāntaṃ kāmīva kāntāhṛdayaṃ praviśya  |
yathārhamanyairanujīvilokaṃ saṃbhāvayāmāsa gṛhaistadīyaiḥ  || 16.40 ||

sā mandurāsaṃśrayibhisturaṃgaiḥ śālāvidhistambhagataiśca nāgaiḥ  |
pūrābabhāse vipaṇisthapaṇyā sarvāṅganaddhābharaṇeva nārī  || 16.41 ||

vasan sa tasyāṃ vasatau raghūṇāṃ purāṇaśobhāmadhiropitāyām  |
na maithileyaḥ spṛhayāṃ babhūva bhartre divo nāpyalakeśvarāya  || 16.42 ||

athāsya ratnagrathitottarīyamekāntapāṇḍustanalambihāram  |
niḥśvāsahāryāṃśukamājagāma gharmaḥ priyā veṣamivopadeṣṭum  || 16.43 ||

agastyacihnādayanātsamīpaṃ diguttarā bhāsvati saṃnivṛtte  |
ānandaśītamiva bhāṣpavṛṣṭiṃ himasrutiṃ haimavatīṃ sasarja  || 16.44 ||

pravṛddhatāpo divaso'timātramatyarthameva kṣaṇadā ca tanvī  |
ubhau virodhakriyayā vibhinnau jāyāpatī sānuśayāvivāstām  || 16.45 ||

dine dine śaivalavantyadhastātsopānaparvāṇi vimuñcadambhaḥ  |
uddaṇḍapadmaṃ gṛhadīrghikāṇāṃ nārīnitambadvayasaṃ babhūva  || 16.46 ||

vaneṣu sāyanatanamallikānāṃ vijṛmbhaṇodgandhiṣu kuḍmaleṣu  |
pratyekanikṣiptapadaḥ saśabdaṃ saṃkhyāmivaiṣāṃ bhramaraścakāra  || 16.47 ||

svedānuviddhārdranakhakṣatāṅke saṃdaṣṭabhūyiṣṭhaśikhaṃ kapole  |
cyutaṃ na karṇādapi kāminīnāṃ śirīṣapuṣpaṃ sahasā papāta  || 16.48 ||

yantrapravāhaiḥ śiśiraiḥ parītān rasena dhautānmalayodbhavasya  |
śilāviśeṣānadhiśayya ninyurdhārāgṛheṣvātapamṛddhimantaḥ  || 16.49 ||

snānārdramukteṣvanudhūpavāsaṃ vinyastasāyantanamallikeṣu  |
kāmo vasantātyayamandavīryaḥ keśeṣu lebhe balamaṅganānām  || 16.50 ||

āpiñjarā baddharajaḥkaṇatvānmañjaryudārāśuśubhe'rjunasya  |
dagdhvāpi dehaṃ giriśena roṣātkhaṇḍīkṛtā jyeva manobhvasya  || 16.51 ||

manojñagandhaṃ sahakārabhaṅgaṃ purāṇasīdhuṃ navapāṭalaṃ ca  |
saṃbadhnatā kāijaneṣu doṣāḥ sarve nidāghāvadhinā pramṛṣṭāḥ  || 16.52 ||

janasya tasmin samaye vigāḍhe babhūvaturdvau saviśeṣakāntau  |
tāpāpanodakṣamapāda sa codayastho nṛpatiḥ śaśī ca  || 16.53 ||

athormilolonmadarājahaṃse rodholatāpuṣpavahe sarayvāḥ  |
vihartumicchā vanitāsakhasya tasyāmbhasi grīṣmasukhe babhūva  || 16.54 ||

sa tīrabhūmau vihitopakāryāmānāyibhistāmapakṛṣṭanakrām  |
vigāhituṃ śrīmahimānurūpaṃ pracakrame cakradharaprabhāvaḥ  || 16.55 ||

sā tīrasopānapathāvatārādanyonyakeyūravighaṭṭinībhiḥ  |
sanūpurakṣobhapadābhirāsīdudvignahaṃsā saridaṅganābhiḥ  || 16.56 ||

parasparābhyukṣaṇatatparāṇāṃ tāsāṃ nṛpo majjanarāgadarśī  |
nausaṃśrayaḥ pārśvagatāṃ kirātīmupāttavālavyajanāṃ babhāṣe  || 16.57 ||

paśyāvarodhaiḥ śataśo madīyairvigāhyamāno galitāṅgarāgaiḥ  |
saṃdhyodayaḥ sābhra ivaiṣa varṇaṃ puṣyatyanekaṃ sarayūpravāhaḥ  || 16.58 ||

viluptamantaḥpurasundarīṇāṃ yadañjanaṃ naululitābhiradbhiḥ  |
tadbadhnatībhirmadarāgaśobhāṃ vilocaneṣu pratimuktamāsām  || 16.59 ||

etā guruśroṇipayodharatvādātmānamudvohuḍhumaśaknuvantyaḥ  |
gāḍhāṅgadairbāhubhiraspu bālāḥ kleśottaraṃ rāgavaśātplavante  || 16.60 ||

amī śirīṣaprasavāvataṃsāḥ prabhraṃśino vārivihāriṇīnām  |
pāriplavāḥ srotasi nimnagāyāḥ śaivālalolāṃśchalayanti mīnān  || 16.61 ||

āsāṃ jalāsphālanatatparāṇāṃ muktāphalaspardhiṣu śīkareṣu  |
payodharotsarpiṣu śīryamāṇāḥ saṃlakṣyate na cchiduro'pi hāraḥ  || 16.62 ||

āvartaśobhā natanābhikānterbhaṅgyo bhruvāṃ dvandvacarāḥstanānām  |
jātāni rūpāvayavopamānānyadūravartīni vilāsinīnām  || 16.63 ||

tīrasthalībarhibhirutkalāpaiḥ prasnigdhakekairabhinandyamānam  |
śrotreṣu saṃmūrchati raktamāsāṃ gītānugaṃ vārimṛdaṅgavādyam  || 16.64 ||

saṃdaṣṭavastreṣvabalānitambeṣvinduprakāśāntaritoḍukalpāḥ  |
amī jalāpūritasūtramārgā maunaṃ bhajante raśanākalāpāḥ  || 16.65 ||

etāḥ karotpīḍitavāridhārā darpātsakhībhirvadaneṣu siktāḥ  |
vakretarāgrairalakaistaruṇyaścūrṇāruṇān vārilavān vamanti  || 16.66 ||

udbaddhakeśaścyutapattrarekho viśleṣimuktāphalapattraveṣṭaḥ  |
manojña eva pramadāmukhānāmambhovihārākulito'pi veṣaḥ  || 16.67 ||

sa nauvimānādavatīrya reme vilolahāraḥ saha tābhirapsu  |
skandhāvalagnoddhṛtapadminīkaḥ kareṇubhirvanya iva dvipendraḥ  || 16.68 ||

tato nṛpenānugatāḥ striyastā bhrājiṣṇunā sātiśayaṃ virejuḥ  |
prāgeva muktā nayanābhirāmāḥ prāpyendranīlaṃ kimutonmayūkham  || 16.69 ||

varṇodakaiḥ kāñcanaśṛṅgamuktaistamāyatākṣyaḥ praṇayādasiñcan  |
tathāgataḥ so'tirarāṃ babhāse sadhātunisyanda ivādrirājaḥ  || 16.70 ||

tenāvarodhapramadāsakhena vigāhanānena saridvarāṃ tām  |
ākāśagaṅgāratirapsarobhirvṛto marutvānanuyātalīlaḥ  || 16.71 ||

yatkumbhayoneradigamya rāmaḥ kuśāya rājyena samaṃ dideśa  |
tadasya jaitrābharaṇaṃ viharturajñātapātaṃ salile mamajja  || 16.72 ||

snātvā yathākāmamasau sadārastīropakāryāṃ gatamātra eva  |
divyena śūnyaṃ valayena bāhumupoḍhanepathyavidhirdadarśa  || 16.73 ||

jayaśriyaḥ saṃvananaṃ yatastadāmuktapūrvaṃ guruṇā ca yasmāt |
sehe'sya na bhraṃśamato na lobhātsa tulyapuṣpābharaṇo hi dhīraḥ  || 16.74 ||

tataḥ samājñāpayadāśu sarvānānāyinastadvicaye nadīṣṇān  |
vandhyaśramāste sarayūṃ vigāhya tamūcurāmlānamukhaprasādāḥ  || 16.75 ||

kṛtaḥ prayatno na ca deva labdhaṃ magnaṃ payasyābharaṇottamaṃ te  |
nāgena laulyātkumudena nūnamupāttamantarhradavāsinā tat || 16.76 ||

tataḥ sa kṛtvā dhanurātatajyaṃ dhanurdharaḥ kopavilohitākṣaḥ  |
gārutmataṃ tīragatastarasvī bhujaṃganāśāya samādade'stram  || 16.77 ||

tasmin hradaḥ saṃhitamātra eva kṣobhātsamāviddhataraṅgahastaḥ  |
rodhāṃsyabhighnannavapātamagnaḥ karīva vanyaḥ paruṣaṃ rarāsa  || 16.78 ||

tasmātsamudrādiva mathyamānādudvṛttanakrātsahasonmamajja  |
lakṣmyeva sārdhaṃ surarājavṛkṣaḥ kanyāṃ puraskṛtya bhujaṃgarājaḥ  || 16.79 ||

vibhūṣaṇapratyupahārahastamupasthitaṃ vīkṣya viśāṃpatistam  |
sauparṇamastraṃ pratisaṃjahāra praheṣvanirbandharuṣo hi santaḥ  || 16.80 ||

trailokyanāthaprabhavaṃ prabhāvātkuśaṃ dviṣāmaṅkuśamastravidvān  |
mānonantenāpyabhivandya mūrdhnā mūrdhābhiṣiktaṃ kumudo babhāṣe  || 16.81 ||

avaimi kāryāntaramānuṣasya viṣṇoḥ sutākhyāmaparāṃ tanuṃ tvām  |
so'haṃ kathaṃ nāma tavācareyamārādhanīyasya dhṛtervibhātam  || 16.82 ||

karābhighātotthitakandukeyamālokya bālātikutūhalena  |
javātpatajjyotirivāntarikṣādādatta jatrābharaṇaṃ tvadīyam  || 16.83 ||

tadetadājānuvilambinā te jyāghātarekhākiṇa lāñchanena  |
bhujena rakṣāparigheṇa bhūmerupaitu yogaṃ punaraṃsalena  || 16.84 ||

imāṃ svasāraṃ ca yavīyasīṃ me kumudvatīṃ nārhasi nānumantum  |
ātmāparādhaṃ nudatīṃ cirāya śuśrūṣayā pārthiva pādayoste  || 16.85 ||

ityūcivānupahṛtābharaṇaḥ kṣitīśaṃ ślāghyo bhavān svajana ityanubhāṣitāram  |
saṃyojayāṃ vidhivadāsa sametabandhuḥ kanyāmayena kumudaḥ kulabhūṣaṇena  || 16.86 ||

tasyāḥ spṛṣṭe manujapatinā sāhacaryāya haste māṅgalyorṇāvalayini puraḥ pāvakasyocchikhasya  |
divyastūryadhvanirudacaradvyaśnuvāno digantān gandhodagraṃ tadanau vavṛṣuḥ puṣpamāścaryameghāḥ  || 16.87 ||

itthaṃ nāgastribhuvanaguroraurasaṃ maithileyaṃ labdhvā bandhuṃ tamapi ca kuśaḥ pañcamaṃ takṣakasya  |
ekaḥ śaṅkāṃ pitṛvadhariporatyajadvainateyācchāntavyālāmavanimaparaḥ paurakāntaḥ śaśāsa  || 16.88 ||


_______________________________________________________________________________





aithiṃ nāma kākutsthātputramāpakumudvatī  |
paścimādyāminīyāmātprasādamiva cetanā  || 17.1 ||

sa pituḥ pitṛmān vaṃśaṃ putramāpakumudvatī  |
apunātsavitevobhau mārgāvuttaradakṣiṇau  || 17.2 ||

tamādau kulavidyānāmarthamarthavidāṃ varaḥ  |
paścātpārthivakanyānāṃ pāṇimagrāhayatpitā  || 17.3 ||

jātyastenābhijātena śūraḥ śauryavatā kuśaḥ  |
amanyataikamātmānamanekaṃ vaśinā vaśī  || 17.4 ||

sa kulocitamindrasya sāhāyakamupeyivān  |
jaghāna samare daityaṃ durjayaṃ tena so'vadhi  || 17.5 ||

taṃ svasā nāgarājasya kumudasya kumudvatī  |
anvagātkumudānandaṃ śaśāṅkamiva kaumudī  || 17.6 ||

tayordivaspaterāsīdekaḥ siṃhāsanārdhabāk |
dvitīyāpi sakhī śacyāḥ pārijātāṃśabhāginī  || 17.7 ||

tadātmasaṃbhavaṃ rājye mantrivṛddāḥ samādadhuḥ  |
smarantaḥ paścimāmājñāṃ bhartuḥ saṃgrāmayāyinaḥ  || 17.8 ||

te tasya kalpayāmāsurabhiṣekāya śilpibhiḥ  |
vimānaṃ navamudvedi catuḥstambhapratiṣṭam  || 17.9 ||

tatrainaṃ hemakumbheṣu saṃbhṛtaistīrthavāribhiḥ  |
upatasthuḥ prakṛtayo bhadrapīṭhopaveśitam  || 17.10 ||

nadadhbiḥ snigdhagambhīraṃ tūryairāhatapuṣkaraiḥ  |
anvamīyata kalyāṇaṃ tasyāvicchinnasaṃtati  || 17.11 ||

dūrvāyavāṅkuraplakṣatvagabhinnapuṭottarān  |
jñātivṛddhaiḥ prayuktān sa bheje nīrājanāvidhīn  || 17.12 ||

purohitapurogāstaṃ jiṣṇuṃ jaitrairatharvabhiḥ  |
upacakramire pūrvamabhiṣektuṃ dvijātayaḥ  || 17.13 ||

tasyaughamahatī mūrdhni nipatantī vyarocata  |
saśabdamabhiṣekaśrīrgaṅgeva tripuradviṣaḥ  || 17.14 ||

stūyamānaḥ kṣaṇe tasminnalakṣyata sa bandibhiḥ  |
pravṛddha iva prajanyaḥ cātakairabhinanditaḥ  || 17.15 ||

tasya sanmantrapūtābhiḥ snānamadbhiḥ pratīcchataḥ  |
vavṛdhe vaidyutasyāgnervṛṣṭisekādiva dyutiḥ  || 17.16 ||

sa tāvadabhiṣekānte snātakebhyo dadau vasu  |
yāvatteṣāṃ samāpyeran yajñāḥ paryāptadakṣiṇāḥ  || 17.17 ||

te prītamanasastasmai yāmāśiṣamudīrayan  |
sā tasya karmanirvṛttairdūraṃ paścātkṛtā phalaiḥ  || 17.18 ||

bandhacchedaṃ sa baddhānāṃ vadhārhāṇāmavadhyatām  |
dhuryāṇāṃ ca dhuro mokṣamadohaṃ cādiṣadgavām  || 17.19 ||

krīḍāpatatriṇo'pyasya pañjarasthāḥ śukādayaḥ  |
labdhamokṣāstadādeśādyatheṣṭagatayo'bhavan  || 17.20 ||

tataḥ kakṣāntaranyastaṃ gajadantāsanaṃ śuci  |
sottaracchadamadhyāsta nepathyagrahaṇāya saḥ  || 17.21 ||

taṃ dhūpāśyānakeśāntaṃ toyanirṇiktapāṇayaḥ  |
ākalpasādhanaistaistairupaseduḥ prasādhakāḥ  || 17.22 ||

te' sya muktāguṇonnaddhaṃ maulimantargatasrajam  |
pratyūpuḥ padmarāgeṇa prabhāmaṇḍalaśobhinā  || 17.23 ||

candanenāṅgarāgaṃ ca mṛganābhisugandhinā  |
samāpayya tataścakruḥ pattraṃ vinyastarocanam  || 17.24 ||

āmuktābharaṇaḥ sragvī haṃsacihnadukūlavān  |
āsīdatiśayaprekṣyaḥ sa rājyaśrīvadhūvaraḥ  || 17.25 ||

nepathyadarśinaśchāyā tasyādarśe hiraṇmaye  |
virarājodite sūrye merau kalpataroriva  || 17.26 ||

sa rājakakudavyagrapāṇibhiḥ pārśvavaribhiḥ  |
yayāvudīritālokaḥ sudharmānavamāṃ sabhām  || 17.27 ||

vitānasahitaṃ tatra bheje paitṛkamāsanam  |
cūḍāmaṇibhirudghṛṣṭapādapīṭhaṃ mahīkṣitām  || 17.28 ||

śuśubhe tena cākrāntaṃ maṅgalāyatanaṃ mahat |
śrīvatsalakṣaṇaṃ vakṣaḥ kaustubheneva kaiśavam  || 17.29 ||

babhau bhūyaḥ kumāratvādādhirājyamavāpya saḥ  |
rekhā bhāvādupārūḍhaḥ sāmagryamiva candramāḥ  || 17.30 ||

prasannamukharāgaṃ taṃ smitapūrvābhibhāṣiṇam  |
mūrtimantamamanyanta viśvāsamanujīvinaḥ  || 17.31 ||

sa puraṃ puruhūtaśrīḥ kalpadrumanibhadhvajām  |
kramamāṇaścakāra dyāṃ nāgenairāvataujasā  || 17.32 ||

tasyaikasyocchritaṃ chattraṃ mūrdhni tenāmalatviṣā  |
pūrvarājaviyogauṣmyaṃ kṛtsnasya jagato hṛtam  || 17.33 ||

dhūmādagneḥ śikhāḥ paścādudayādaṃśavo raveḥ  |
so'tītya tejasāṃ vṛttiṃ samamevotthito guṇaiḥ  || 17.34 ||

taṃ prītiviśadairnetrairanvayuḥ paurayoṣitaḥ  |
śaratprasannairjyotirbhirvibhāvarya iva dhruvam  || 17.35 ||

ayodhyādevatāścainaṃ praśastāyatanārcitāḥ  |
anudadhyuranudhyeyaṃ sāṃnidhyaiḥ pratimāgataiḥ  || 17.36 ||

yāvannāśyāyate vedirabhiṣekajalāplutā  |
tāvadevāsya velāntaṃ pratāpaḥ prāpa duḥsahaḥ  || 17.37 ||

vasiṣṭhasya gurormantrāḥ sāyakāstasya dhanvinaḥ  |
kiṃ tatsādhyaṃ yadubhaye sādhayeyurna saṃgatāḥ  || 17.38 ||

sa dharmasthasakhaḥ śaśvadarthipratyarthināṃ svayam  |
dadarśa saṃśayacchedyān vyavahārānatandritaḥ  || 17.39 ||

tataḥ paramabhivyaktāsaumanasyaniveditaiḥ  |
yuyoja pākābhimukhairbhṛtyān vijñāpanāphalaiḥ  || 17.40 ||

prajāstadguruṇā nadyo nabhaseva vivardhitāḥ  |
tasmiṃstu bhūyasīṃ vṛddhiṃ nabhasye tā ivāyayuḥ  || 17.41 ||

yaduvāca na tanmithyā yaddadau na jahāra tat |
so'bhūdbhagnavrataḥ śatrūnuddhṛtya pratiropayan  || 17.42 ||

vayorūpavibhūtīnāmekaikaṃ madakāraṇam  |
tāni tasmin samastāni na tasyotsiṣice manaḥ  || 17.43 ||

itthaṃ janitarāgāsu prakṛtiṣvanuvāsaram  |
akṣobhyaḥ sa navo'pyāsīddṛḍhamūla iva drumaḥ  || 17.44 ||

anityāḥ śatravo bāhyā viprakṛṣṭāśca te yataḥ  |
ataḥ so'bhyantarānnityān ṣaṭpūrvamajayadripūn  || 17.45 ||

prasādābhimukhe tasmiṃścapalāpi svabhāvataḥ  |
nikaṣe hemarekheva śrīrāsīdanapāyinī  || 17.46 ||

kātaryaṃ kevalā nītiḥ śauryaṃ śvāpadaceṣṭitam  |
ataḥ siddhiṃ sametābhyāmubhābhyāmanviyeṣa saḥ  || 17.47 ||

na tasya maṇḍale rājño nyastapraṇidhidīdhiteḥ  |
adṛṣṭamabhavatkiṃcidvyabhrasyeva vivasvataḥ  || 17.48 ||

rātriṃdivavibhāgeṣu yadādiṣṭaṃ mahīkṣitām  |
tatsiṣeve niyogena sa vikalpaparāṅmukhaḥ  || 17.49 ||

mantraḥ pratidinaṃ tasya babhūva saha mantribhiḥ  |
sa jātu sevyamāno'pi guptadvāro na sūcyate  || 17.50 ||

pareṣu sveṣu ca kṣiptairavijñātaparasparaiḥ  |
so'pasarpairjajāgāra yathākālaṃ svapannapi  || 17.51 ||

durgāṇi durgrhāṇyāsaṃstasya roddhurapi dviṣām  |
na hi siṃho gajāskandī bhayādgiriguhāśayaḥ  || 17.52 ||

bahvyamukhyāḥ samārambhāḥ pratyavekṣyā niratyayāḥ  |
garbhaśālisadharmāṇastasya gūḍhaṃ vipecire  || 17.53 ||

apathena pravavṛte na jātūpacito'pi saḥ  |
vṛddhau nadīmukhenaiva prasthānaṃ lavaṇāmbhasaḥ  || 17.54 ||

kāmaṃ pratkṛtivairāgyaṃ sadyaḥ śamayituṃ kṣamaḥ  |
yasya kāryaḥ pratīkāraḥ sa tannaivodapādayat || 17.55 ||

śakeṣvevābhavadyātrā tasya śaktimataḥ sataḥ  |
samīraṇasahāyo'pi nāmbhaḥprārthī davānalaḥ  || 17.56 ||

na dharmamarthakāmābhyāṃ babādhe na ca tena tau  |
nārthaṃ kāmena kāmaṃ vā so'rthena sadṛśastriṣu  || 17.57 ||

hīnānyanupakartḥṇi pravṛddhāni vikurvate  |
tena madhyamaśaktīni mitrāṇi shtāpitānyataḥ  || 17.58 ||

parātmanoḥ paricchidya śaktyādīnāṃ balābalam  |
yayāvebhirbaliṣṭhaścetparasmādāsta so'nyathā  || 17.59 ||

kośenāśrayaṇīyatvamiti tasyārthasaṃgrahaḥ  |
ambugarbho hi jīmūtaścātakairabhinandyate  || 17.60 ||

parakarmāpahaḥ so'bhūdudyataḥ sveṣu karmasu  |
āvṛṇodātmano randhraṃ randhreṣu praharan ripūn  || 17.61 ||

pitrā saṃvardhito nityaṃ kṛtāstraḥ sāṃparāyikaḥ  |
tasya daṇḍavato daṇḍaḥ svadehānna vyaśiṣyata  || 17.62 ||

sarpasyeva śiroratnaṃ nāsya śaktitrayaṃ paraḥ  |
sa cakarṣa parasmāttadayaskānta ivāyasam  || 17.63 ||

vāpīṣviva sravantīṣu vaneṣūpavaneṣviva  |
sārthāḥ svairaṃ svakīyeṣu cerurveśmasvivādriṣu  || 17.64 ||

tapo rakṣan sa vighnebhyastaskarebhyaśca saṃpadaḥ  |
yathāsvamāśramaiścakre varṇairapi ṣaḍsaṃśabhāk || 17.65 ||

khanibhiḥ suṣuve ratnaṃ kṣetraiḥ sasyaṃ vanairgajān  |
dideśa vetanaṃ tasmai rakṣāsadṛśameva bhūḥ  || 17.66 ||

sa guṇānāṃ balānāṃ ca ṣaṇṇāṃ ṣaṇmukhavikramaḥ  |
babhūva viniyogajñaḥ sādhanīyeṣu vastuṣu  || 17.67 ||

iti kramātprayuñjāno rāja nītiṃ caturvidhām  |
ā tīrthādapratīghātaṃ sa tasyāḥ phalamānaśe  || 17.68 ||

kūṭayuddhavidhijñe'pi tasmin sanmārgayodhini  |
bheje'bhisārikāvṛttiṃ jayaśrīrvīragāminī  || 17.69 ||

prāyaḥ pratāpabhagnatvādarīṇāṃ tasya durlabhaḥ  |
raṇo gandhavipasyeva gandhabhinnānyadantinaḥ  || 17.70 ||

pravṛddhau hīyate candraḥ samudro'pi tathāvidhaḥ  |
sa tu tasamavṛddhiśca na cābhūttāviva kṣayī  || 17.71 ||

santastasyābhigamanādatyarthaṃ mahataḥ kṛṣāḥ  |
udadheriva jīmūtāḥ prāpurdātṛtvamarthinaḥ  || 17.72 ||

stūyamānaḥ sa jihrāya stutyameva samācaran  |
tathāpi vavṛdhe tasya tatkāridveṣino yaśaḥ  || 17.73 ||

duritaṃ darśanena ghnaṃstattvārthena nudaṃstamaḥ  |
prajāḥ svatantrayāṃ cakre śaśvatsūrya ivoditaḥ  || 17.74 ||

indoragatayaḥ padme sūryasya kumude'ṃśavaḥ  |
guṇāstasya vipakṣe'pi guṇino lebhire'ntaram  || 17.75 ||

parābhisaṃdhānaparaṃ yadyapyasya viceṣṭitam  |
jigīṣoraśvamedhāya dharmyameva babhūva tat || 17.76 ||

evamudyan prabhāveṇa śāstgranirdiṣṭavartmanā  |
vṛṣeva devo devānāṃ rājñāṃ rājā babhūva saḥ  || 17.77 ||

pañcamaṃ lokapālānāṃ tamūcuḥ sāmyayogataḥ  |
bhūtānāṃ mahatāṃ ṣaṣṭhamaṣṭamaṃ kulabhūbhṛtām  || 17.78 ||

dūrāpavarjitacchattraistasyājñāṃ śāsanārpitām  |
dadhuḥ śirobhirbhūpālā devaḥ pauraṃdarīmiva  || 17.79 ||

ṛtvijaḥ sa tathānarca dakṣiṇābhirmahākratau  |
yathā sādhāraṇībhūtaṃ nāmāsya dhanadasya ca  || 17.80 ||

indrādvṛṣṭirniyamitagadodrekavṛttiryamo'bhūdyādonāthaḥ śivajalapathaḥ karmaṇe naucarāṇām  |
pūrvāpekṣī tadanu vidadhe kośavṛddhiṃ kuberastasmin daṇḍopanatacaritaṃ bhejire lokapālāḥ  || 17.81 ||


_______________________________________________________________________________




sa naiṣadhasyārthapateḥ sutāyāmutpādayāmāsa niṣiddhaśatruḥ  |
anūnasāraṃ niṣadhānnagendrātputraṃ yamāhurniṣadhākhyameva  || 18.1 ||

tenoruvīryeṇa pitā prajāyai kalpiṣyamāṇena nananda yūnā  |
suvṛṣtiyogādiva jīvalokaḥ sasyena saṃpattiphalātmakena  || 18.2 ||

śabdādi nirviśya sukhaṃ cirāya tasmin pratiṣṭhāpitarājaśabdaḥ  |
kaumudvateyaḥ kumudāvadātairdyāmarjitāṃ karmabhirāruroha  || 18.3 ||

pautraḥ kuśasyāpi kuśeśayākṣaḥ sasāgarāṃ sāgaradhīracetāḥ  |
ekātapoatrāṃ bhuvamekavīraḥ purārgalādīrghabhujo bubhoja  || 18.4 ||

tasyānalaujāstanayastadante vaṃśaśriyaṃ prāpa nalābhidhānaḥ  |
yo naḍvalānīva gajaḥ pareṣāṃ balānyamṛdnānnalinābhavaktraḥ  || 18.5 ||

nabhaścarairgītayaśyāmatanuṃ tanūjaṃ nabhastalaśyāmatanuṃ tanūjam  |
khyātaṃ nabhaḥśabdamayena nāmnā kāntaṃ nabhomāsamiva prajānām  || 18.6 ||

tasmai viṣrjyottarakosalānāṃ dharmottarastatprabhave prabhutvam  |
mṛgairajaryaṃ jaraspodaiṣṭamadehabandhāya punarbabandha  || 18.7 ||

tena dvipānāmiva puṇḍarīko rājñāmajayyo'jani puṇḍarīkaḥ  |
śānte pitaryāhṛtapuṇḍarīkā yaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣamivāśritā śrīḥ  || 18.8 ||

sa kṣemadhanvānamamoghadhanvā putraṃ prajākṣemavidhānadakṣam  |
kṣmāṃ lambhayitvā kṣamayopapannaṃ vane tapaḥ kṣāntataraścacāra  || 18.9 ||

anīkinīnāṃ samare'grayāyī tasyāpi devapratimaḥ suto'bhūt |
vyaśrūyatānīkapadāvasānaṃ devādi nāma tridive'pi yasya  || 18.10 ||

pitā samārādhanatatpareṇa putreṇa putrī sa yathaiva tena  |
putrastathaivādhikavatsalena sa tena pitrā pitṛmān babhūva  || 18.11 ||

pūrvastayorātmasame ciroḍhāmātmodbhave varṇacatuṣṭayasya  |
dhuraṃ nidhāyaikanidhirguṇānāṃ jagāma yajvā yajamānalokam  || 18.12 ||

vaśī sutastasya vaśaṃvadatvātsveṣāmivāsīddviṣatāmapīṣṭaḥ  |
sakṛd(?) vivignānapi hi prayuktaṃ mādhuryamīṣṭe hariṇān grahītum  || 18.13 ||

ahīnagurnāma sa gāṃ samagrāmahīnabāhudraviṇaḥ śaśāsa  |
yo hīna saṃsargaparāṅmukhatvādmādhuryamīṣṭe hariṇān grahītum  || 18.14 ||

guroḥ sa cānantaramantarjñaḥ puṃsāṃ pumānādya ivāvatīrṇaḥ  |
upakramairaskhalitaiścaturbhiścaturdigīśaścaturo babhūva  || 18.15 ||

tasmin prayāte paralokayātrāṃ jetaryarīṇāṃ tanayaṃ tadīyam  |
uccaiḥśirastvājjitapāriyātraṃ lakṣmīḥ siṣeve kila pāriyātram  || 18.16 ||

tasyābhavatsūnurudāraśīlaḥ śilaḥ śilāpaṭṭaviśālavakṣāḥ  |
jitāripakṣo'pi śilīmukhairyaḥ śālīnatāmavrajadīḍyamānaḥ  || 18.17 ||

tamātmasaṃpannamaninditātmā kṛtvā yuvānaṃ yuvarājameva  |
sukhāni so'bhuṅkta sukhoparodhi vṛttaṃ hi rājñāmuparuddhavṛttam  || 18.18 ||

taṃ rāgabandhiṣvavitṛptameva bhogeṣu saubhāgyaviśeṣabhogyam  |
vilāsinīnāmaratikṣamāpi jarā vṛthā matsariṇī jahāra  || 18.19 ||

unnābha ityudagtasnāmadheyastasyāyathārthonnatanābhhirandhraḥ  |
suto'bhavatpaṅkajanābhakalpaḥ kṛtsnasya nābhirnṛpamaṇḍalasya  || 18.20 ||

tataḥ paraṃ vajradharaprabhāvastadātmajaḥ saṃyati vajraghoṣaḥ  |
babhūva vajrākarabhuṣaṇāyāḥ patiḥ pṛthivyāḥ kila vajranābhaḥ  || 18.21 ||

tasmin gate dyāṃ sukṛtopalabdhāṃ tatsaṃbhavaṃ śaṅkhaṇamarṇavāntā  |
utkhātaśatruṃ vasudhopatasthe ratnopahārairuditaiḥ khanibhyaḥ  || 18.22 ||

tasyāvasāne haridaśvadhāmā pitryaṃ prapede padamaśvirūpaḥ  |
velātaṭeṣūṣitasainikāśvaṃ purāvido yaṃ dhyuṣitāśvamāhuḥ  || 18.23 ||

ārādhya viśveśvaramīśvareṇa tena kṣiterviśvasaho'dhijajñe  |
pātuṃ saho viśvasakhaḥ samagrāṃ viśvaṃbharāmātmajamūrtirātmā  || 18.24 ||

aṃśe hiraṇyākśaripoḥ sa jāte hiraṇyanābhe tanaye nayajñaḥ  |
dviṣāmasahyaḥ sutarāṃ tarūṇāṃ hiraṇyaretā sānilo'bhūt || 18.25 ||

pitā pitḥṇāmanṛṇastamante vayasyanantāni sukhāni lipsuḥ  |
rājānamājānuvilambibāhuṃ kṛtvā kṛtī valkalavān babhūva  || 18.26 ||

kausalya ityuttarakosalānāṃ patyuḥ pataṃgānvayabhūṣaṇasya  |
tasyaurasaḥ somasutaḥ suto'bhūnnetrotsavaḥ soma iva dvitīyaḥ  || 18.27 ||

kausalya ityuttarakosalānāṃ sa brahmabhūyaṃ gatimājagāma  |
brahmiṣṭhamādhāya nije'dhikāre brahmiṣṭhameva svatanuprasūtam  || 18.28 ||

yaśobhirābrahmasabhaṃ prakāśaḥ samyagmahīṃ śāsati śāsanāṅkām  |
prajāściraṃ suprajasi prajeśe nanandurānandajalāvilākṣyaḥ  || 18.29 ||

pātrīkṛtātmā gurusevanena spṛṣṭākṛtiḥ pattrarathendraketoḥ  |
taṃ putriṇāṃ puṣkarapattranetraḥ putraḥ samāropayadagrasaṃkhyām  || 18.30 ||

vaṃśasthitiṃ vaṃśakareṇa tena saṃbhāvya bhāvī sa sakhā maghonaḥ  |
upaspṛṣan sparśanivṛttalaulyastripuṣkareṣu tridaśatvamāpa  || 18.31 ||

tasya prabhānirjitapuṣparāgaṃ pauṣyaṃ tithau puṣyamasūta patnī  |
yasminnapuṣyannudite samagrāṃ puṣṭiṃ janāḥ puṣya iva dvitīye  || 18.32 ||

mahīṃ mahecchaḥ parikīrya sūnau manīṣiṇe jaiminaye'rpitātmā  |
tasmātsayogādadhigamya yogamajanmane'kalpata janmabhīruḥ  || 18.33 ||

tataḥ paraṃ tatprabhavaḥ prapede dhruvopameyo dhruvasaṃdhirurvīm  |
yasminnabhūjjyāyasi satyasaṃdhe saṃdhirdhruvaḥ saṃnamatāmarīṇām  || 18.34 ||

sute śiśāveva sudarśanākhye darśātyayendupriyadarśane saḥ  |
mṛgāyatākṣo mṛgayāvihārī siṃhādavāpadvipadaṃ nṛsiṃhaḥ  || 18.35 ||

svargāminastasya tamaikamatyādamātyavargaḥ kulatantumekam  |
anāthadīnāḥ prakṛtīravekṣya sāketanāthaṃ vidhivaccakāra  || 18.36 ||

vavendunā tannabhasopameyaṃ śāvaikasiṃhena ca kānanena  |
raghoḥ kulaṃ kuḍmalapaṅkajena toyena cāprauṣhanarendramāsīt || 18.37 ||

lokena bhāvī pitureva tulyaḥ saṃbhāvito mauliparigrahātsaḥ  |
dṛṣṭo hi vṛṇvan kalabhapramāṇo'pyāśāḥ purovātamavāpya meghaḥ  || 18.38 ||

taṃ rājavīthyāmadhihasti yāntamādhoraṇālambitamagryaveṣam  |
ṣaḍvarṣadeśiyamapi prabhutvātpraikṣanta paurāḥ pitṛgauraveṇa  || 18.39 ||

kāmaṃ na so'kalpata paitṛkasya siṃhāsanasya pratipūraṇāya  |
tejomahimnā punarāvṛtātmā tadvyāpa cāmīkarapiñjareṇa  || 18.40 ||

tasmādadhaḥ kiṃcidivāvatīrṇāvasaṃspṛśantau tapanīyapīṭham  |
sālaktakau bhūpatayaḥ prasiddhairvavandire maulibhirasya pādau  || 18.41 ||

maṇau mahānīla iti prabhāvādalpapramāṇe'pi yathā na mithyā  |
śabdo mahārāja iti pratītastathaiva tasmin yuyuje'rbhake'pi  || 18.42 ||

paryantasaṃcāritacāmarasya kapolalolobhayakākapakṣat |
tasyānanāduccarito vivādaścakshāla velāsvapi nārṇavānām  || 18.43 ||

nirvṛttajāmbūnadapaṭṭaśobhe nyastaṃ lalāṭe tilakaṃ dadhānaḥ  |
tenaiva śūnyānyarisundarīṇāṃ mukhāni sa smeramukhaścakāra  || 18.44 ||

śirīṣapuṣpādhikasaukumāryaḥ khedaṃ sa yāyādapi śrutavṛddhayogāt |
nitāntagurvīmapi cānubhāvāddhuraṃ dharitryā bibharāṃ babhūva  || 18.45 ||

nyastākṣarāmakṣarabhūmikāyāṃ kārtsnyena gṛhṇāti lipiṃ na yāvat |
sarvāṇi tāvacchrutavṛddhayogātphalānyupāyuṅkta sa daṇḍanīteḥ  || 18.46 ||

urasyaparyāptabhāgā prauḍhībhaviṣyantamudīkṣamāṇā  |
saṃjātalajjeva tamātapatrachhāyāchalenopajugūha lakṣmīḥ  || 18.47 ||

anaśnuvānena yugopamānamabaddhamaurvīkiṇalāñchanena  |
aspṛṣṭakhaḍgatsaruṇāpi cāsīdrakṣāvatī tasya bhujena bhūmiḥ  || 18.48 ||

na kevalaṃ gacchati tasya kāle yayuḥ śarīrāvayavā vivṛddhim  |
vaṃśyā guṇāḥ khalvapi lokakāntāḥ prārambhasūkṣmāḥ prathimānamāpuḥ  || 18.49 ||

sa pūrvajanmāntaradṛṣṭapārāḥ smarannivākleśakaro gurūṇām  |
tisrastrivargādhigamasya mūlaṃ jagrāha vidyāḥ prakṛtīśca pitryāḥ  || 18.50 ||

vyūhya sthitaḥ kiṃcidivottarārdhamunnaddhacūḍo'ñcitasvyajānuḥ  |
ākarṇamākṛṣṭasabānadhanvā vyarocat' āste sa vinīyamānaḥ  || 18.51 ||

atha madhu vanitāṃ netranirveśanīyaṃ manasijatarupuṣpaṃ rāgabandhapravālam  |
akṛtakavidhi sarvāṅgīṇamākalpajātaṃ vilasitapadamādyaṃ yauvanaṃ sa prapede  || 18.52 ||

pratikṛtiracanābhayo dūtisaṃdarśitābhyaḥ samadhikatararūpāḥ śuddhasaṃtānakāmaiḥ  |
adhivividuramātyairāhṛtāstasya yūnaḥ prathamaparigṛhīte śrībhuvau rājakanyāḥ  || 18.53 ||


_______________________________________________________________________________




agnivarṇamabhiṣicya rāghavaḥ sve pade tanayamagnitejasam  |
śiśriye śrutavatāmapaścimaḥ paścime vayasi naimiṣaṃ vaśī  || 19.1 ||

tatra tīrthasalilena dīrghikāstalpamantaritabhūmibhiḥ kuśaiḥ  |
saudhavāsamuṭajena vismṛtaḥ saṃcikāya phalaniḥspṛhastapaḥ  || 19.2 ||

labdhapālanavidhau na tatsutaḥ khedamāpa guruṇā hi medinī  |
bhoktumeva bhujanirjitadviṣā na prasādhayitumasya kalpitā  || 19.3 ||

so'dhikāramabhikaḥ kulocitaṃ kāścana svayamavartayatsamāḥ  |
taṃ niveśya saciveṣvataḥ paraṃ strīvidheyanavayauvano'bhavat || 19.4 ||

kāminīsahacarasya kāminastasya veśmasu mṛdaṅganādiṣu  |
ṛddhimantamadhikarddhiruttaraḥ pūrvamutsavamapohadutsavaḥ  || 19.5 ||

indriyārthapariśūnyamakṣarmaḥ soḍhumekamapi sa kṣaṇātaram  |
antare ca viharan divāniśaṃ na vyapaikṣata samutsukāḥ prajāḥ  || 19.6 ||

gauravādyadapi jātu mantriṇāṃ darśanaṃ prakṛtikāṅkṣitaṃ dadau  |
tadgavākṣavivarāvalambinā kevalena caraṇena kalpitam  || 19.7 ||

taṃ kṛtapraṇatayo'nujīvinaḥ komalātmanakharāgarūṣitam  |
bhejire navadivākarātapaspṛṣṭapaṅkajatulādhirohaṇam  || 19.8 ||

yuvanonnatavilāsinīstanakṣobhalolakamalāśca dīrghikāḥ  |
gūḍhamohanagṛhāstadambubhiḥ sa vyagāhata vigāḍhamanmathaḥ  || 19.9 ||

tatra sekahṛtalocanāñjanairdhautarāgaparipāṭalādharaiḥ  |
aṅganāstamadhikaṃ vyalobhayannarpitaprakṛtikāntibhirmukhaiḥ  || 19.10 ||

ghrāṇakāntamadhugandhakarṣiṇīḥ pānabhūmiracanāḥ priyāsakhaḥ  |
abhyapadyata sa vāsitāsakhaḥ puṣpitāḥ kamalinīriva dvipaḥ  || 19.11 ||

sātirekamadakāraṇaṃ rahastena dattamabhileṣuraṅganāḥ  |
tābhirapyupahṛtaṃ mukhāsavaṃ so'pibadbakulatulyadohadaḥ  || 19.12 ||

aṅkamaṅkaparivartanocite tasya ninyaturaśūnyatāmubhe  |
vallakī ca hṛdayaṃgamasvanā valguvāgapi ca vāmalocanā  || 19.13 ||

sa svayaṃ prahatapuṣkaraḥ kṛtī lolamālyavalayo haranmanaḥ  |
nartakīrabhinayātilaṅghinīḥ pārśvavartiṣu guruṣvalajjavat || 19.14 ||

cāru nṛtyavigame ca tanmukhaṃ svedabhinnatilakaṃ pariśramāt |
premadattavadanāniaḥ manaḥ so'nvajīvadamarālakeśvarau  || 19.15 ||

tasya sāvaraṇadṛṣṭasaṃdhayaḥ kāmyavastuṣu naveṣu saṅginaḥ  |
vallabhābhirupasṛtya cakrire sāmibhuktaviṣayāḥ samāgamāḥ  || 19.16 ||

aṅgulīkisalayāgratarjanaṃ bhrūvibhaṅgakuṭilaṃ ca vīkṣitam  |
mekhalābhirasakṛcca bandhanaṃ vañcayan praṇayinīravāpa saḥ  || 19.17 ||

tena dūtividitaṃ niṣeduṣā pṛṣṭhataḥ suratavārarātriṣu  |
śuśruve priyajanasya kātaraṃ vipralambhapariśaṅkino vacaḥ  || 19.18 ||

laulyametya gṛhiṇīparigrahānnartakīṣvasulabhāsu tadvapuḥ  |
vartate sma sa kathaṃcidālikhannaṅgulīkṣaraṇasannavartikaḥ  || 19.19 ||

premagarvitavipakṣamatsarādāyatācca madanānmahīkṣitam  |
ninyurutsavavidhicchalena taṃ devya ujjitaruṣaḥ kṛtārthatām  || 19.20 ||

prātaretya paribhogaśobhinā darśanena kṛtakhaṇḍanavyathāḥ  |
prāñjaliḥ praṇayinīḥ prasādayan so'dunotpraṇayamantharaḥ punaḥ  || 19.21 ||

svapnakīrtitavipakṣamaṅganāḥ darśanena kṛtakhaṇḍanavyathāḥ  |
pracchadāntagalitāśrubindubhiḥ krodhabhinnavalayairvivartanaiḥ  || 19.22 ||

kḷptapuṣpaśayanāṃllatāgṛhānetya dūtikṛtamārgadarśanaḥ  |
anvabhūtparijanāṅganārataṃ so'varodhabhayavepathūttaram  || 19.23 ||

nāma vallabhajanasya te mayā prāpya bhāgyamapi tasya kāṅkṣyate  |
lolupaṃ bata mano mameti taṃ gotraviskhalitamūcuraṅganāḥ  || 19.24 ||

cūrṇababhru lulitasragākulaṃ chinnamekhalamalaktakāṅkitam  |
utthitasya śayanaṃ vilāsinastasya vibhramartatānyapāvṛṇot || 19.25 ||

sa svayaṃ caraṇarāgamādadhe yoṣitaṃ na ca tathā samāhitaḥ  |
lobhyamānanayanaḥ ślathāṃśukairmekhalāguṇapadairnitambibhiḥ  || 19.26 ||

cumbane viparivartitādharaṃ hastarodhi raśanāvighaṭṭane  |
vighnitecchamapi tasya sarvato manmathendhanamabhūdvadhūratam  || 19.27 ||

darpaṇeṣu paribhogadarśinīrnarmapūrvamanupṛṣṭhasaṃsthitaḥ  |
chāyayā smitamanojñayā vadhūrhrīnimīlitamukhīścakāra saḥ  || 19.28 ||

kaṇṭhasaktamṛdubāhubandhanaṃ nyastapādatalamagrapādayoḥ  |
prārthayanta śayanotthitaṃ priyāstaṃ niśātyayavisargacumbanam  || 19.29 ||

prekṣya darpaṇatalsthamātmano rājaveṣamatiśakraśobhinam  |
pipriye sa na tathā yathā yuvā vyaktalakṣma paribhogamaṇḍanam  || 19.30 ||

mitrakṛtyamapadiśya pārśvataḥ prasthitaṃ tamanavasthitaṃ priyāḥ  |
vidma he śaṭha palāyanacchalānyañjaseti rurudhuḥ kacagrahaiḥ  || 19.31 ||

tasya nirdayaratiśramālasāḥ kaṇṭhasūtramapadiśyayoṣitaḥ  |
adhyaśerata bṛhad(?) bhujāntaraṃ pīvarastanaviluptacandanam  || 19.32 ||

saṃgamāya niśi gūḍhacāriṇaṃ cāradūtikathitaṃ puro gatāḥ  |
vañcayiṣyasi kutastamovṛtaḥ kāmuketi cakṛṣustamaṅganāḥ  || 19.33 ||

yoṣitāmuḍupaterivāciṣāṃ sparśanirvṛtimasāvanāpnuvan  |
āruroha kumudākaropamāṃ rātrijāgaraparo divāśayaḥ  || 19.34 ||

veṇunā daśanapīḍitādharā vīṇayā nakhapadāṅkitoravaḥ  |
śilapakārya ubhayena vejitāstaṃ vijihmanayanā vyalobhayan  || 19.35 ||

aṅgasattvavacanāśrayaṃ mithaḥ strīṣu nṛtyamupadhāya darśayan  |
sa prayoganipuṇaiḥ prayoktṛbhiḥ saṃjagharṣa saha mitrasaṃnidhau  || 19.36 ||

aṃsalambikuṭajārjunasrajastasya nīparajasāṅgarāgiṇaḥ  |
prāvṛṣi pramadabarhiṇeṣvabhūtkṛtimādriṣu vihāravibhramaḥ  || 19.37 ||

vigrahācca śayane parāṅmukhīrnānunetumabalāḥ sa tatvare  |
ācakāṅkṣa ghanaśabdaviklavāstā vivṛtya viśatīrbhujāntaram  || 19.38 ||

kārttikīṣu savitānaharmyabhāgyāminīṣu lalitāṅganāsakhaḥ  |
anvabhuṅkta surataśramāpahāṃ meghamuktaviśadāṃ sa candrikām  || 19.39 ||

saikataṃ ca sarayūṃ vivṛṇvatīṃ śroṇibimbamiva haṃsamekhalam  |
svapriyāvilasitānukāriṇīṃ saudhajālvivarairvyalokayat || 19.40 ||

marmarairagurudhūpagandhibhirvyaktahemarśanaistamekataḥ  |
jahrurāgrathanamokṣalolupaṃ haimanairnivsanaiḥ sumadhyamāḥ  || 19.41 ||

arpitastimitadīpadṛṣṭayo garbhaveśmasu nivātakukṣiṣu  |
tasya sarvasuratāntarakṣamāḥ sākṣitāṃ śiśirarātrayo yayuḥ  || 19.42 ||

dakṣiṇena pavanena saṃbhṛtaṃ prekṣya cūtakusumaṃ sapallavam  |
anvanaiṣuravadhūtavigrahāstaṃ durutsahaviyogamaṅganāḥ  || 19.43 ||

tāḥ svamaṅkamadhiropya dolayā preṅkhayan parijanāpaviddhayā  |
muktarajju nibiḍaṃ bhayacchalātkaṇṭhabandhanamavāpa bāhubhiḥ  || 19.44 ||

taṃ payodharaniṣaktacandanairmauktikagrathitacārubhūṣaṇaiḥ  |
grīṣmaveṣavidhibhiḥ siṣevire śroṇilambimaṇimekhalāḥ priyāḥ  || 19.45 ||

yatsa bhagnasahakāramāsavaṃ raktapāṭalasamāgamaṃ papau  |
tena tasya madhunirgamātkṛśaścittayonirabhavatpunarnavaḥ  || 19.46 ||

evamindriyasukhāni nirviśannanyakāryavimukhaḥ sa pārthivaḥ  |
ātmalakṣaṇaniveditānṛtūnatyavāhayadanaṅgavāhitaḥ  || 19.47 ||

taṃ pramattamapi na prabhāvataḥ śekurākramitumanyapārthivāḥ  |
āmayastu ratirāgasaṃbhavo dakṣaśāpa iva candramakṣiṇot || 19.48 ||

dṛṣṭadoṣamapi tanna so'tyajatsaṅgavastu bhiṣajāmanāśravaḥ  |
svādubhistu viṣayairhṛtastato duḥkhamindriyagaṇo nivāryate  || 19.49 ||

tasya pāṇḍuvadanālpabhūṣaṇā sāvalambagamanā mṛdusvanā  |
yakṣmaṇāpi parihānirāyayau kāmayānasamavasthayā tulām  || 19.50 ||

vyoma paścimakalāsthitendu vā paṅkaśeṣamiva gharmapalvalam  |
rājñi tatkulamabhūtkṣayāture vāmanārciriva dīpabhājanam  || 19.51 ||

bāḍhameṣu divaseṣu karma sādhayati putrajanmane  |
ityadarśitarujo'sya mantriṇaḥ śaśvadūcuraghaśaṅkinīḥ prajāḥ  || 19.52 ||

sa tvanekavanitāsakho'pi san pāvanīmanavalokya saṃtatim  |
vaidyayatnaparibhāvinaṃ gadaṃ na pradīpa iva vāyumatyagāt || 19.53 ||

taṃ gṛhopavana eva saṃgatāḥ paścimakratuvidā purodhasā  |
rogaśāntimapadiśya mantriṇaḥ saṃbhṛte śikhini gūḍhamādadhuḥ  || 19.54 ||

taiḥ kṛtaprakṛtimukhyasaṃgrahairāśu tasya sahadharmacāriṇī  |
sādhu dṛṣṭaśubhagarbhalakṣaṇā pratyapadyata narādhipaśriyam  || 19.55 ||

tasyāstathāvidhanarendravipattiśokāduṣṇairvilocanajalaiḥ prathamābhitaptaḥ  |
nirvāpitaḥ kanakakumbhamukhojjhitena vaṃśābhiṣekavidhinā śiśireṇa garbhaḥ  || 19.56 ||

taṃ bhāvāya prasavasamayākāṅkṣiṇīnāṃ prajānāmantargūḍhaṃ kṣitiriva babhau bījamuṣṭiṃ dadhānā  |
maulaiḥ sārdhaṃ sthavirasacivairhemasiṃhāsanasthā rājñī rājyaṃ vidhivadaśiṣadbharturavyāhatājñā  || 19.57 ||




additionalverses(kṣepaka)


ākīryamāṇamāsannavidhibhiḥ samidāharaiḥ  |
vaikhānasairadṛśyāgnipratyudgamanavṛttibhiḥ  || 1.49* ||

imāṃ devīmṛtusnātāṃ smṛtvā sapadi satvaraḥ  |
pradakṣiṇakriyātītastasyāḥ kopamajījanaḥ  || 1.75* ||

tāmralalāṭajāṃ rekhāṃ bibhratī sāsitetarām  |
saṃdhyā prātipadeneva pratibhinnā himāṃśunā  || 1.82* ||

gāndharvamastraṃ taditaḥ pratīccha prayogasaṃhāravibhaktamantram  |
saṃdhyā prātipadeneva na cārihiṃsā vijayaḥ svahaste  || 5.57* ||

nidrāvaśaṃ tvayi gate śaśinā kathaṃcidātmānamānanarucā bhavato viyujya  |
lakṣmīrvibhātasamaye'pi hi darśanena paryutsukā praṇayinī niśi khaṇḍiteva  || 5.67* ||

mandaṃ vivāti himasaṃbhṛtaśītabhāvaḥ saṃsṛjyate sarasijairaruṇāṃśubhinnaiḥ  |
saurabhyamīpsuriva te mukhamārutasya yanno guṇān prati niśāpariṇāmavāyuḥ  || 5.69* ||

iti sa vihatanidrastalpamalpetarāṃśaḥ suragaja iva gaṅgāsaikataṃ supratīkaḥ  |
parijanavanitānāṃ pādayorvyāpṛtānāṃ valayamaṇividiṣṭaṃ pracchadāntaṃ mumoca  || 5.75* ||

sa tatra mañeṣcu vimānakalpeṣvākalpasaṃmūrchitarūpaśobhān  |
siṃhāsanasthānnṛpatīnapaśyatyūpān praśastāniva haimavedīn  || 6.1* ||

yadā yadā rājakumārikāsau na pūrvapūrvaṃ ganayāṃ cakāra  |
tadā tadā nāmitarepharekhāmāśālatā pallavinī babhūva  || 6.67* ||

lalāṭabaddhabhṛkuṭītaraṅgaistanutyajāṃ dantanipīḍitoṣṭhaiḥ  |
ātastare bhallanikṛttakaṇṭhairhuṃkāragarbhairdviṣatāṃ śirobhiḥ  || 7.38* ||

atha vīkṣya guṇaiḥ pratiṣṭhitaṃ prakṛtiṣvātmajamābhigamikaiḥ  |
padavīṃ pariṇāmadeśinīṃ raghurādatta vanāntagāminīm  || 8.9* ||

namastrimūrtaye tubhyaṃ guṇatrayavibhāgāya  |
prāksṛṣṭe[ḥ] kevalātmane paścādbhad[r]amupeyuṣe  || 10.19* ||

te'pi tūrṇamavagamya śāmbhavamāsamāharaṇakarmatatparāḥ  |
svāṃ sakṣiptatikarkaśaṃ hi taccikṣipurdaśāarathātmajāgrataḥ  || 11.43* ||

nistriṃśakalpasya nidherjalānāmeṣā tamāladrumarāja nīlā  |
dūrādarālabhru vibhāti velā kalaṅkarekhā malineva dhārā  || 13.15* ||

pratyāgatau tatra cirapravāsādapaśyatāṃ dāśarathī jananyau  |
kumudvatī śītamarīcilekhe diveva rūpāntaradurvibhāvye  || 14.1* ||

keyaṃ vane lakṣmaṇa lakṣmaṇeti dīnākṣaraṃ roditi yoṣiduccaiḥ  |
āṃ jñātameṣā janakātmajeti kavirvicintyāntikamājagāma  || 14.69* ||

tadākarṇya munervākyaṃ rāmo rājīvalocanaḥ  |
samaṃ harṣaviṣādābhyāṃ yuyuje nītikovidaḥ  || 15.71* ||

gaccha lakṣmaṇa śīghraṃ tvaṃ mā bhūddharmaviparyayaḥ  |
tyāgo vāpi vadho vāpi sādhūnāmubhayaṃ samam  || 15.94* ||

tasmādbabhūvātha dalābhidhāno dalānvitaḥ padmadalābhadṛṣṭiḥ  |
kundāgra danto ripudantisiṃhaḥ patiḥ pṛthivyāḥ kulakairivenduḥ  || 18.16* ||

hitvātha bhogāṃstapasottamena triviṣṭapaṃ prāptavati kṣitīśe  |
tadātmajaḥ sāgaradhīracetāḥ śaśāsa pṛthvīṃ sakalāṃ nṛsomaḥ  || 18.19* ||
